
I'll proceed with the domain-by-domain DDL generation starting with Domain 1: Core Infrastructure. This is the foundation upon which all other domains depend.

## PHASE 2: PostgreSQL DDL Implementation

### Domain 1: Core Infrastructure DDL

```sql
-- =============================================
-- CORE INFRASTRUCTURE DOMAIN
-- Foundation for multi-tenant hospital system
-- =============================================

-- Enable required extensions
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pg_trgm";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "btree_gin";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "btree_gist";

-- Create core schema
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS core;

-- Custom domain types
CREATE DOMAIN core.email_type AS VARCHAR(254) 
    CHECK (VALUE ~ '^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z|a-z]{2,}$');

CREATE DOMAIN core.phone_type AS VARCHAR(20) 
    CHECK (VALUE ~ '^\+?[1-9]\d{1,14}$');

CREATE DOMAIN core.currency_type AS NUMERIC(18,2);

CREATE DOMAIN core.percentage_type AS NUMERIC(5,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0 AND VALUE <= 100);

CREATE DOMAIN core.blood_group_type AS SMALLINT 
    CHECK (VALUE IN (1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8));
-- 1=A+, 2=A-, 3=B+, 4=B-, 5=AB+, 6=AB-, 7=O+, 8=O-

-- =============================================
-- TENANTS TABLE
-- Top-level organizational units
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.tenants (
    tenant_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    tenant_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    legal_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    tax_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- Encrypted in application layer
    address TEXT NOT NULL,
    contact_email core.email_type NOT NULL,
    contact_phone core.phone_type NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    activation_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    deactivation_date DATE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL,
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    
    -- Soft delete
    is_deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by UUID,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_tenant_code UNIQUE (tenant_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_activation_dates CHECK (
        (is_active = TRUE AND deactivation_date IS NULL) OR
        (is_active = FALSE AND deactivation_date IS NOT NULL)
    )
);

-- =============================================
-- BRANCHES TABLE
-- Individual hospital locations
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.branches (
    branch_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    branch_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    address TEXT NOT NULL,
    city VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    state VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    country VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    postal_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    phone core.phone_type NOT NULL,
    emergency_phone core.phone_type NOT NULL,
    is_24_hours BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    bed_capacity INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    license_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    accreditation_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    -- 1=Not Accredited, 2=NABH, 3=JCI, 4=ISO, 5=Other
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL,
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    
    -- Soft delete
    is_deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by UUID,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_branch_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_bed_capacity CHECK (bed_capacity >= 0)
);

-- =============================================
-- COUNTRIES TABLE
-- International country codes
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.countries (
    country_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    country_code CHAR(2) NOT NULL, -- ISO 3166-1 alpha-2
    country_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    iso_code CHAR(3) NOT NULL, -- ISO 3166-1 alpha-3
    phone_code VARCHAR(5) NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_country_code UNIQUE (country_code),
    CONSTRAINT uq_iso_code UNIQUE (iso_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- STATES TABLE
-- State/Province definitions
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.states (
    state_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    country_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.countries(country_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    state_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    state_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_state_code UNIQUE (country_id, state_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- CITIES TABLE
-- City definitions
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.cities (
    city_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    state_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.states(state_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    city_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    timezone VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_city_state UNIQUE (state_id, city_name)
);

-- =============================================
-- DEPARTMENTS TABLE
-- Organizational units within branches
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.departments (
    department_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    department_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    department_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    department_type SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    -- 1=Clinical, 2=Administrative, 3=Support, 4=Research
    parent_department_id UUID REFERENCES core.departments(department_id),
    cost_center_code VARCHAR(50),
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL,
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_department_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, department_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_parent_department CHECK (parent_department_id != department_id)
);

-- =============================================
-- DESIGNATIONS TABLE
-- Job titles and positions
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.designations (
    designation_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    designation_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    designation_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    job_grade VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    pay_band VARCHAR(20),
    min_salary core.currency_type,
    max_salary core.currency_type,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_designation_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, designation_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_salary_range CHECK (min_salary <= max_salary)
);

-- =============================================
-- SPECIALIZATIONS TABLE
-- Medical specialties
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.specializations (
    specialization_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    specialization_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    specialization_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    category SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    -- 1=Medical, 2=Surgical, 3=Diagnostic, 4=Allied Health
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_specialization_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, specialization_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- USERS TABLE
-- System users with authentication
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.users (
    user_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    username VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    password_hash BYTEA NOT NULL, -- bcrypt hashed
    email core.email_type NOT NULL,
    first_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    last_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    date_of_birth DATE NOT NULL,
    gender SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    -- 1=Male, 2=Female, 3=Other, 4=Unknown
    employee_id VARCHAR(50),
    department_id UUID REFERENCES core.departments(department_id),
    designation_id UUID REFERENCES core.designations(designation_id),
    specialization_id UUID REFERENCES core.specializations(specialization_id),
    license_number VARCHAR(100),
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    last_login_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    must_change_password BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    password_expiry_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT (CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '90 days'),
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL,
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    
    -- Soft delete
    is_deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by UUID,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_username UNIQUE (tenant_id, username),
    CONSTRAINT uq_employee_id UNIQUE (tenant_id, employee_id),
    CONSTRAINT chk_password_expiry CHECK (password_expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE),
    CONSTRAINT chk_age CHECK (date_of_birth <= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '18 years')
);

-- =============================================
-- ROLES TABLE
-- System roles
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.roles (
    role_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    role_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    role_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    role_description TEXT,
    is_system_role BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_role_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, role_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- PERMISSIONS TABLE
-- Fine-grained permissions
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.permissions (
    permission_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    permission_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    permission_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    permission_description TEXT,
    module VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    sub_module VARCHAR(50),
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_permission_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, permission_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- USER_ROLES TABLE
-- User to role assignment
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.user_roles (
    user_role_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    role_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.roles(role_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    assigned_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    valid_from DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    valid_until DATE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_user_role UNIQUE (user_id, role_id),
    CONSTRAINT chk_valid_dates CHECK (valid_until IS NULL OR valid_until > valid_from)
);

-- =============================================
-- ROLE_PERMISSIONS TABLE
-- Role to permission assignment
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.role_permissions (
    role_permission_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    role_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.roles(role_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    permission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.permissions(permission_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    assigned_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_role_permission UNIQUE (role_id, permission_id)
);

-- =============================================
-- INDEXES FOR CORE INFRASTRUCTURE
-- =============================================

-- Tenants indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_tenants_active ON core.tenants(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_tenants_created ON core.tenants(created_at);

-- Branches indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_branches_tenant ON core.branches(tenant_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_branches_active ON core.branches(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_branches_location ON core.branches(country, state, city);

-- Departments indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_departments_branch ON core.departments(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_departments_parent ON core.departments(parent_department_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_departments_active ON core.departments(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

-- Users indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_users_tenant ON core.users(tenant_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_users_department ON core.users(department_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_users_active ON core.users(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_users_name ON core.users(first_name, last_name);
CREATE INDEX idx_users_employee ON core.users(employee_id) WHERE employee_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_users_login ON core.users(last_login_at) WHERE last_login_at IS NOT NULL;

-- Role-Permission indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_role_perms_role ON core.role_permissions(role_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_role_perms_perm ON core.role_permissions(permission_id);

-- User-Role indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_user_roles_user ON core.user_roles(user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_user_roles_role ON core.user_roles(role_id);

-- =============================================
-- AUDIT TRIGGER FUNCTION
-- =============================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.updated_at = NOW();
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Apply audit trigger to all tables with updated_at
CREATE TRIGGER trg_tenants_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON core.tenants 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_branches_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON core.branches 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_departments_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON core.departments 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_users_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON core.users 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

-- =============================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY POLICIES
-- =============================================

-- Enable RLS on all tables
ALTER TABLE core.tenants ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE core.branches ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE core.departments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE core.users ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE core.roles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE core.permissions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Tenant isolation policy
CREATE POLICY policy_tenant_isolation ON core.tenants
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_tenant_isolation ON core.branches
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_tenant_isolation ON core.departments
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_tenant_isolation ON core.users
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_tenant_isolation ON core.roles
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_tenant_isolation ON core.permissions
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

-- =============================================
-- INITIAL DATA (System roles and permissions)
-- =============================================

-- Insert system roles
INSERT INTO core.roles (tenant_id, role_code, role_name, role_description, is_system_role, is_active) VALUES
('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'SYS_ADMIN', 'System Administrator', 'Full system access', TRUE, TRUE),
('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'TENANT_ADMIN', 'Tenant Administrator', 'Tenant-level administration', TRUE, TRUE),
('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'HR_MANAGER', 'HR Manager', 'Human resources management', TRUE, TRUE);

-- Insert basic permissions
INSERT INTO core.permissions (tenant_id, permission_code, permission_name, permission_description, module, is_active) VALUES
('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'USER_CREATE', 'Create User', 'Create new users', 'USERS', TRUE),
('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'USER_READ', 'View User', 'View user details', 'USERS', TRUE),
('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'USER_UPDATE', 'Update User', 'Update user information', 'USERS', TRUE),
('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'USER_DELETE', 'Delete User', 'Delete users', 'USERS', TRUE);

-- =============================================
-- COMMENTS FOR DOCUMENTATION
-- =============================================

COMMENT ON SCHEMA core IS 'Core infrastructure schema for multi-tenant hospital management system';
COMMENT ON TABLE core.tenants IS 'Top-level organizational units (hospital groups/networks)';
COMMENT ON TABLE core.branches IS 'Individual hospital locations within tenants';
COMMENT ON TABLE core.users IS 'System users with authentication and authorization';
COMMENT ON TABLE core.roles IS 'System roles for role-based access control';
COMMENT ON TABLE core.permissions IS 'Fine-grained permissions for system access control';

COMMENT ON COLUMN core.users.password_hash IS 'bcrypt hashed password (60 characters)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN core.users.must_change_password IS 'Flag indicating if user must change password on first login';
COMMENT ON COLUMN core.roles.is_system_role IS 'System roles cannot be modified or deleted';
```

```sql
-- =============================================
-- CORE INFRASTRUCTURE DOMAIN
-- Foundation for multi-tenant hospital system
-- =============================================

-- Enable required extensions
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pgcrypto";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "pg_trgm";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "btree_gin";
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "btree_gist";

-- Create core schema
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS core;

-- Custom domain types
CREATE DOMAIN core.email_type AS VARCHAR(254) 
    CHECK (VALUE ~ '^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z|a-z]{2,}$');

CREATE DOMAIN core.phone_type AS VARCHAR(20) 
    CHECK (VALUE ~ '^\+?[1-9]\d{1,14}$');

CREATE DOMAIN core.currency_type AS NUMERIC(18,2);

CREATE DOMAIN core.percentage_type AS NUMERIC(5,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0 AND VALUE <= 100);

CREATE DOMAIN core.blood_group_type AS SMALLINT 
    CHECK (VALUE IN (1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8));
-- 1=A+, 2=A-, 3=B+, 4=B-, 5=AB+, 6=AB-, 7=O+, 8=O-

-- =============================================
-- TENANTS TABLE
-- Top-level organizational units
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.tenants (
    tenant_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    tenant_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    legal_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    tax_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- Encrypted in application layer
    address TEXT NOT NULL,
    contact_email core.email_type NOT NULL,
    contact_phone core.phone_type NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    activation_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    deactivation_date DATE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL,
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    
    -- Soft delete
    is_deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by UUID,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_tenant_code UNIQUE (tenant_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_activation_dates CHECK (
        (is_active = TRUE AND deactivation_date IS NULL) OR
        (is_active = FALSE AND deactivation_date IS NOT NULL)
    )
);

-- =============================================
-- BRANCHES TABLE
-- Individual hospital locations
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.branches (
    branch_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    branch_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    address TEXT NOT NULL,
    city VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    state VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    country VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    postal_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    phone core.phone_type NOT NULL,
    emergency_phone core.phone_type NOT NULL,
    is_24_hours BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    bed_capacity INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    license_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    accreditation_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    -- 1=Not Accredited, 2=NABH, 3=JCI, 4=ISO, 5=Other
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL,
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    
    -- Soft delete
    is_deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by UUID,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_branch_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_bed_capacity CHECK (bed_capacity >= 0)
);

-- =============================================
-- COUNTRIES TABLE
-- International country codes
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.countries (
    country_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    country_code CHAR(2) NOT NULL, -- ISO 3166-1 alpha-2
    country_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    iso_code CHAR(3) NOT NULL, -- ISO 3166-1 alpha-3
    phone_code VARCHAR(5) NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_country_code UNIQUE (country_code),
    CONSTRAINT uq_iso_code UNIQUE (iso_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- STATES TABLE
-- State/Province definitions
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.states (
    state_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    country_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.countries(country_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    state_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    state_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_state_code UNIQUE (country_id, state_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- CITIES TABLE
-- City definitions
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.cities (
    city_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    state_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.states(state_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    city_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    timezone VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_city_state UNIQUE (state_id, city_name)
);

-- =============================================
-- DEPARTMENTS TABLE
-- Organizational units within branches
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.departments (
    department_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    department_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    department_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    department_type SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    -- 1=Clinical, 2=Administrative, 3=Support, 4=Research
    parent_department_id UUID REFERENCES core.departments(department_id),
    cost_center_code VARCHAR(50),
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL,
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_department_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, department_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_parent_department CHECK (parent_department_id != department_id)
);

-- =============================================
-- DESIGNATIONS TABLE
-- Job titles and positions
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.designations (
    designation_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    designation_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    designation_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    job_grade VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    pay_band VARCHAR(20),
    min_salary core.currency_type,
    max_salary core.currency_type,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_designation_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, designation_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_salary_range CHECK (min_salary <= max_salary)
);

-- =============================================
-- SPECIALIZATIONS TABLE
-- Medical specialties
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.specializations (
    specialization_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    specialization_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    specialization_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    category SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    -- 1=Medical, 2=Surgical, 3=Diagnostic, 4=Allied Health
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_specialization_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, specialization_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- USERS TABLE
-- System users with authentication
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.users (
    user_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    username VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    password_hash BYTEA NOT NULL, -- bcrypt hashed
    email core.email_type NOT NULL,
    first_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    last_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    date_of_birth DATE NOT NULL,
    gender SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    -- 1=Male, 2=Female, 3=Other, 4=Unknown
    employee_id VARCHAR(50),
    department_id UUID REFERENCES core.departments(department_id),
    designation_id UUID REFERENCES core.designations(designation_id),
    specialization_id UUID REFERENCES core.specializations(specialization_id),
    license_number VARCHAR(100),
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    last_login_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    must_change_password BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    password_expiry_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT (CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '90 days'),
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL,
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    
    -- Soft delete
    is_deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by UUID,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_username UNIQUE (tenant_id, username),
    CONSTRAINT uq_employee_id UNIQUE (tenant_id, employee_id),
    CONSTRAINT chk_password_expiry CHECK (password_expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE),
    CONSTRAINT chk_age CHECK (date_of_birth <= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '18 years')
);

-- =============================================
-- ROLES TABLE
-- System roles
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.roles (
    role_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    role_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    role_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    role_description TEXT,
    is_system_role BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_role_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, role_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- PERMISSIONS TABLE
-- Fine-grained permissions
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.permissions (
    permission_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    permission_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    permission_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    permission_description TEXT,
    module VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    sub_module VARCHAR(50),
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_permission_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, permission_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- USER_ROLES TABLE
-- User to role assignment
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.user_roles (
    user_role_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    role_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.roles(role_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    assigned_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    valid_from DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    valid_until DATE,
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_user_role UNIQUE (user_id, role_id),
    CONSTRAINT chk_valid_dates CHECK (valid_until IS NULL OR valid_until > valid_from)
);

-- =============================================
-- ROLE_PERMISSIONS TABLE
-- Role to permission assignment
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE core.role_permissions (
    role_permission_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    role_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.roles(role_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    permission_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.permissions(permission_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    assigned_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    assigned_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_role_permission UNIQUE (role_id, permission_id)
);

-- =============================================
-- INDEXES FOR CORE INFRASTRUCTURE
-- =============================================

-- Tenants indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_tenants_active ON core.tenants(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_tenants_created ON core.tenants(created_at);

-- Branches indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_branches_tenant ON core.branches(tenant_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_branches_active ON core.branches(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_branches_location ON core.branches(country, state, city);

-- Departments indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_departments_branch ON core.departments(branch_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_departments_parent ON core.departments(parent_department_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_departments_active ON core.departments(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

-- Users indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_users_tenant ON core.users(tenant_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_users_department ON core.users(department_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_users_active ON core.users(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_users_name ON core.users(first_name, last_name);
CREATE INDEX idx_users_employee ON core.users(employee_id) WHERE employee_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_users_login ON core.users(last_login_at) WHERE last_login_at IS NOT NULL;

-- Role-Permission indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_role_perms_role ON core.role_permissions(role_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_role_perms_perm ON core.role_permissions(permission_id);

-- User-Role indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_user_roles_user ON core.user_roles(user_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_user_roles_role ON core.user_roles(role_id);

-- =============================================
-- AUDIT TRIGGER FUNCTION
-- =============================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.updated_at = NOW();
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Apply audit trigger to all tables with updated_at
CREATE TRIGGER trg_tenants_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON core.tenants 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_branches_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON core.branches 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_departments_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON core.departments 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_users_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON core.users 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

-- =============================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY POLICIES
-- =============================================

-- Enable RLS on all tables
ALTER TABLE core.tenants ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE core.branches ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE core.departments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE core.users ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE core.roles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE core.permissions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Tenant isolation policy
CREATE POLICY policy_tenant_isolation ON core.tenants
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_tenant_isolation ON core.branches
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_tenant_isolation ON core.departments
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_tenant_isolation ON core.users
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_tenant_isolation ON core.roles
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_tenant_isolation ON core.permissions
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

-- =============================================
-- INITIAL DATA (System roles and permissions)
-- =============================================

-- Insert system roles
INSERT INTO core.roles (tenant_id, role_code, role_name, role_description, is_system_role, is_active) VALUES
('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'SYS_ADMIN', 'System Administrator', 'Full system access', TRUE, TRUE),
('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'TENANT_ADMIN', 'Tenant Administrator', 'Tenant-level administration', TRUE, TRUE),
('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'HR_MANAGER', 'HR Manager', 'Human resources management', TRUE, TRUE);

-- Insert basic permissions

('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'USER_READ', 'View User', 'View user details', 'USERS', TRUE),
('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'USER_UPDATE', 'Update User', 'Update user information', 'USERS', TRUE),
('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'USER_DELETE', 'Delete User', 'Delete users', 'USERS', TRUE);

-- =============================================
-- COMMENTS FOR DOCUMENTATION
-- =============================================

COMMENT ON SCHEMA core IS 'Core infrastructure schema for multi-tenant hospital management system';
COMMENT ON TABLE core.tenants IS 'Top-level organizational units (hospital groups/networks)';
COMMENT ON TABLE core.branches IS 'Individual hospital locations within tenants';
COMMENT ON TABLE core.users IS 'System users with authentication and authorization';
COMMENT ON TABLE core.roles IS 'System roles for role-based access control';
COMMENT ON TABLE core.permissions IS 'Fine-grained permissions for system access control';

COMMENT ON COLUMN core.users.password_hash IS 'bcrypt hashed password (60 characters)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN core.users.must_change_password IS 'Flag indicating if user must change password on first login';
COMMENT ON COLUMN core.roles.is_system_role IS 'System roles cannot be modified or deleted';
```

## Domain 1 Implementation Complete

✅ Core Infrastructure Domain DDL generated with:

- 12 Tables with complete constraints and relationships
- Custom domain types for validation
- Indexes for performance optimization
- Row Level Security policies for multi-tenancy
- Audit triggers for automatic timestamp updates
- Initial data for system roles and permissions
- Comprehensive comments for documentation

Key Features Implemented:

- Multi-tenancy with tenant isolation
- Complete audit trails (created_at, updated_at, soft delete)
- Role-based access control foundation
