I'll proceed with Domain 2: Patient Management DDL generation. This is the critical foundation for all clinical operations.

## Domain 2: Patient Management DDL

sql

```sql
-- =============================================
-- PATIENT MANAGEMENT DOMAIN
-- Central patient repository with demographics
-- =============================================

-- Create patient management schema
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS patient_management;

-- Custom domain types for patient management
CREATE DOMAIN patient_management.mrn_type AS VARCHAR(20);
CREATE DOMAIN patient_management.national_id_type AS VARCHAR(100); -- Encrypted storage
CREATE DOMAIN patient_management.blood_group_type AS SMALLINT 
    CHECK (VALUE IN (1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8));
-- 1=A+, 2=A-, 3=B+, 4=B-, 5=AB+, 6=AB-, 7=O+, 8=O-

-- =============================================
-- PATIENTS TABLE
-- Core patient demographic information
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patients (
    patient_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    mrn patient_management.mrn_type NOT NULL,
    first_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    middle_name VARCHAR(100),
    last_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    date_of_birth DATE NOT NULL,
    gender SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (gender IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Male, 2=Female, 3=Other, 4=Unknown
    blood_group patient_management.blood_group_type,
    marital_status SMALLINT CHECK (marital_status IN (1,2,3,4,5)),
    -- 1=Single, 2=Married, 3=Divorced, 4=Widowed, 5=Separated
    nationality UUID REFERENCES core.countries(country_id),
    ethnicity VARCHAR(100),
    preferred_language VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'English',
    religion VARCHAR(100),
    occupation VARCHAR(100),
    education_level VARCHAR(100),
    is_deceased BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    date_of_death DATE,
    cause_of_death TEXT,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,

    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    -- Soft delete
    is_deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT uq_mrn_tenant UNIQUE (tenant_id, mrn),
    CONSTRAINT chk_date_of_birth CHECK (date_of_birth <= CURRENT_DATE),
    CONSTRAINT chk_deceased_date CHECK (
        (is_deceased = TRUE AND date_of_death IS NOT NULL) OR
        (is_deceased = FALSE AND date_of_death IS NULL)
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_age CHECK (
        date_of_birth <= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '1 day'
    )
) PARTITION BY LIST (tenant_id);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_ADDRESSES TABLE
-- SCD Type 2 for address history
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_addresses (
    address_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    address_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (address_type IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Primary, 2=Secondary, 3=Temporary
    address_line1 VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    address_line2 VARCHAR(255),
    city VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    state VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    postal_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    country UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.countries(country_id),
    is_current BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    valid_from DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    valid_until DATE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_address FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT chk_valid_dates CHECK (valid_until IS NULL OR valid_until > valid_from)
);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_CONTACTS TABLE
-- Multiple contact methods
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_contacts (
    contact_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    contact_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (contact_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Mobile, 2=Home Phone, 3=Work Phone, 4=Email
    contact_value VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    is_primary BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_contact FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT chk_email_format CHECK (
        contact_type != 4 OR (contact_value ~ '^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z|a-z]{2,}$')
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_phone_format CHECK (
        contact_type IN (1,2,3) OR (contact_value ~ '^\+?[1-9]\d{1,14}$')
    )
);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_EMERGENCY_CONTACTS TABLE
-- Emergency contact information
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_emergency_contacts (
    emergency_contact_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    first_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    last_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    relationship VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    -- Spouse, Parent, Child, Sibling, Friend, Other
    contact_phone core.phone_type NOT NULL,
    contact_email core.email_type,
    priority SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (priority IN (1,2)),
    -- 1=Primary, 2=Secondary
    is_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_emergency FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_INSURANCE TABLE
-- Insurance coverage details
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_insurance (
    insurance_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    insurance_provider VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    policy_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    group_number VARCHAR(100),
    insurance_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (insurance_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Primary, 2=Secondary, 3=Tertiary, 4=Other
    effective_date DATE NOT NULL,
    expiration_date DATE NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    verification_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (verification_status IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Pending, 2=Verified, 3=Rejected
    copay_amount core.currency_type,
    deductible_amount core.currency_type,
    out_of_pocket_max core.currency_type,
    verification_notes TEXT,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_insurance FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT chk_insurance_dates CHECK (expiration_date > effective_date)
);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_CONSENTS TABLE
-- Consent management
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_consents (
    consent_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    consent_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (consent_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Treatment, 2=Research, 3=Data Sharing, 4=Marketing
    consent_version VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    granted BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    granted_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    revoked_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    consent_text TEXT NOT NULL,
    consent_summary TEXT,
    witness_id UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    is_active BOOLEAN GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        granted AND revoked_at IS NULL AND 
        (valid_until IS NULL OR valid_until > NOW())
    ) STORED,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_consent FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_IDENTIFIERS TABLE
-- Government and other identifiers
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_identifiers (
    identifier_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    identifier_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (identifier_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=National ID, 2=Passport, 3=Driver License, 4=Other
    identifier_value patient_management.national_id_type NOT NULL,
    issuing_country UUID REFERENCES core.countries(country_id),
    issue_date DATE,
    expiration_date DATE,
    is_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_identifier FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT chk_expiration_date CHECK (expiration_date IS NULL OR expiration_date > issue_date)
);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_ALLERGIES TABLE
-- Allergy information
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_allergies (
    allergy_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    allergen VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    allergy_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (allergy_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Drug, 2=Food, 3=Environmental, 4=Other
    severity SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (severity IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Mild, 2=Moderate, 3=Severe
    reaction TEXT NOT NULL,
    onset_date DATE NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_allergy FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT chk_onset_date CHECK (onset_date <= CURRENT_DATE)
);

-- =============================================
-- INDEXES FOR PATIENT MANAGEMENT
-- =============================================

-- Patients indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_tenant ON patient_management.patients(tenant_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_mrn ON patient_management.patients(mrn);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_name ON patient_management.patients(last_name, first_name);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_dob ON patient_management.patients(date_of_birth);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_active ON patient_management.patients(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

-- Patient addresses indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_addresses_patient ON patient_management.patient_addresses(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_addresses_current ON patient_management.patient_addresses(is_current) WHERE is_current = TRUE;

-- Patient contacts indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_contacts_patient ON patient_management.patient_contacts(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_contacts_primary ON patient_management.patient_contacts(is_primary) WHERE is_primary = TRUE;

-- Emergency contacts indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_emergency_contacts_patient ON patient_management.patient_emergency_contacts(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_emergency_contacts_priority ON patient_management.patient_emergency_contacts(priority);

-- Insurance indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_insurance_patient ON patient_management.patient_insurance(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_insurance_active ON patient_management.patient_insurance(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_insurance_expiry ON patient_management.patient_insurance(expiration_date);

-- Consent indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_consents_patient ON patient_management.patient_consents(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_consents_active ON patient_management.patient_consents(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

-- Identifier indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_identifiers_patient ON patient_management.patient_identifiers(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_identifiers_verified ON patient_management.patient_identifiers(is_verified) WHERE is_verified = TRUE;

-- Allergy indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_allergies_patient ON patient_management.patient_allergies(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_allergies_active ON patient_management.patient_allergies(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

-- =============================================
-- PARTITIONING FOR PATIENTS TABLE
-- =============================================

-- Create partitions for each tenant (example for first few tenants)
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patients_tenant1 
    PARTITION OF patient_management.patients 
    FOR VALUES IN ('tenant1-uuid-here');

CREATE TABLE patient_management.patients_tenant2 
    PARTITION OF patient_management.patients 
    FOR VALUES IN ('tenant2-uuid-here');

-- Default partition
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patients_default 
    PARTITION OF patient_management.patients 
    DEFAULT;

-- =============================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY POLICIES
-- =============================================

-- Enable RLS on all patient tables
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patients ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_addresses ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_contacts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_emergency_contacts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_insurance ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_consents ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_identifiers ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_allergies ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Tenant isolation policies
CREATE POLICY policy_patient_tenant_isolation ON patient_management.patients
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_patient_data_access ON patient_management.patient_addresses
    USING (EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM patient_management.patients p 
        WHERE p.patient_id = patient_addresses.patient_id 
        AND p.tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID
    ));

-- Similar policies for all other patient-related tables...

-- =============================================
-- AUDIT TRIGGERS
-- =============================================

CREATE TRIGGER trg_patients_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON patient_management.patients 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_patient_addresses_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON patient_management.patient_addresses 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_patient_contacts_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON patient_management.patient_contacts 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

-- Apply to all other tables...

-- =============================================
-- COMMENTS FOR DOCUMENTATION
-- =============================================

COMMENT ON SCHEMA patient_management IS 'Patient demographic and administrative data management';
COMMENT ON TABLE patient_management.patients IS 'Core patient demographic information with MRN';
COMMENT ON TABLE patient_management.patient_addresses IS 'Patient address history with SCD Type 2 tracking';
COMMENT ON TABLE patient_management.patient_contacts IS 'Multiple contact methods for patients';
COMMENT ON TABLE patient_management.patient_emergency_contacts IS 'Emergency contact information';

COMMENT ON COLUMN patient_management.patient_identifiers.identifier_value IS 'Encrypted storage of sensitive identification numbers';
COMMENT ON COLUMN patient_management.patients.mrn IS 'Medical Record Number - unique within tenant';
```

```sql
-- =============================================
-- PATIENT MANAGEMENT DOMAIN
-- Central patient repository with demographics
-- =============================================

-- Create patient management schema
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS patient_management;

-- Custom domain types for patient management
CREATE DOMAIN patient_management.mrn_type AS VARCHAR(20);
CREATE DOMAIN patient_management.national_id_type AS VARCHAR(100); -- Encrypted storage
CREATE DOMAIN patient_management.blood_group_type AS SMALLINT 
    CHECK (VALUE IN (1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8));
-- 1=A+, 2=A-, 3=B+, 4=B-, 5=AB+, 6=AB-, 7=O+, 8=O-

-- =============================================
-- PATIENTS TABLE
-- Core patient demographic information
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patients (
    patient_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    mrn patient_management.mrn_type NOT NULL,
    first_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    middle_name VARCHAR(100),
    last_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    date_of_birth DATE NOT NULL,
    gender SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (gender IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Male, 2=Female, 3=Other, 4=Unknown
    blood_group patient_management.blood_group_type,
    marital_status SMALLINT CHECK (marital_status IN (1,2,3,4,5)),
    -- 1=Single, 2=Married, 3=Divorced, 4=Widowed, 5=Separated
    nationality UUID REFERENCES core.countries(country_id),
    ethnicity VARCHAR(100),
    preferred_language VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'English',
    religion VARCHAR(100),
    occupation VARCHAR(100),
    education_level VARCHAR(100),
    is_deceased BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    date_of_death DATE,
    cause_of_death TEXT,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,

    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    -- Soft delete
    is_deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT uq_mrn_tenant UNIQUE (tenant_id, mrn),
    CONSTRAINT chk_date_of_birth CHECK (date_of_birth <= CURRENT_DATE),
    CONSTRAINT chk_deceased_date CHECK (
        (is_deceased = TRUE AND date_of_death IS NOT NULL) OR
        (is_deceased = FALSE AND date_of_death IS NULL)
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_age CHECK (
        date_of_birth <= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '1 day'
    )
) PARTITION BY LIST (tenant_id);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_ADDRESSES TABLE
-- SCD Type 2 for address history
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_addresses (
    address_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    address_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (address_type IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Primary, 2=Secondary, 3=Temporary
    address_line1 VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    address_line2 VARCHAR(255),
    city VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    state VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    postal_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    country UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.countries(country_id),
    is_current BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    valid_from DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    valid_until DATE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_address FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT chk_valid_dates CHECK (valid_until IS NULL OR valid_until > valid_from)
);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_CONTACTS TABLE
-- Multiple contact methods
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_contacts (
    contact_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    contact_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (contact_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Mobile, 2=Home Phone, 3=Work Phone, 4=Email
    contact_value VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    is_primary BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_contact FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT chk_email_format CHECK (
        contact_type != 4 OR (contact_value ~ '^[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z|a-z]{2,}$')
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_phone_format CHECK (
        contact_type IN (1,2,3) OR (contact_value ~ '^\+?[1-9]\d{1,14}$')
    )
);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_EMERGENCY_CONTACTS TABLE
-- Emergency contact information
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_emergency_contacts (
    emergency_contact_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    first_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    last_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    relationship VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    -- Spouse, Parent, Child, Sibling, Friend, Other
    contact_phone core.phone_type NOT NULL,
    contact_email core.email_type,
    priority SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (priority IN (1,2)),
    -- 1=Primary, 2=Secondary
    is_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_emergency FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_INSURANCE TABLE
-- Insurance coverage details
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_insurance (
    insurance_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    insurance_provider VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    policy_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    group_number VARCHAR(100),
    insurance_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (insurance_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Primary, 2=Secondary, 3=Tertiary, 4=Other
    effective_date DATE NOT NULL,
    expiration_date DATE NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    verification_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (verification_status IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Pending, 2=Verified, 3=Rejected
    copay_amount core.currency_type,
    deductible_amount core.currency_type,
    out_of_pocket_max core.currency_type,
    verification_notes TEXT,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_insurance FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT chk_insurance_dates CHECK (expiration_date > effective_date)
);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_CONSENTS TABLE
-- Consent management
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_consents (
    consent_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    consent_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (consent_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Treatment, 2=Research, 3=Data Sharing, 4=Marketing
    consent_version VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    granted BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    granted_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    revoked_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    consent_text TEXT NOT NULL,
    consent_summary TEXT,
    witness_id UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    is_active BOOLEAN GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        granted AND revoked_at IS NULL AND 
        (valid_until IS NULL OR valid_until > NOW())
    ) STORED,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_consent FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_IDENTIFIERS TABLE
-- Government and other identifiers
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_identifiers (
    identifier_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    identifier_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (identifier_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=National ID, 2=Passport, 3=Driver License, 4=Other
    identifier_value patient_management.national_id_type NOT NULL,
    issuing_country UUID REFERENCES core.countries(country_id),
    issue_date DATE,
    expiration_date DATE,
    is_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_identifier FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT chk_expiration_date CHECK (expiration_date IS NULL OR expiration_date > issue_date)
);

-- =============================================
-- PATIENT_ALLERGIES TABLE
-- Allergy information
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patient_allergies (
    allergy_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    allergen VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    allergy_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (allergy_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Drug, 2=Food, 3=Environmental, 4=Other
    severity SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (severity IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Mild, 2=Moderate, 3=Severe
    reaction TEXT NOT NULL,
    onset_date DATE NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_patient_allergy FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) 
        REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT chk_onset_date CHECK (onset_date <= CURRENT_DATE)
);

-- =============================================
-- INDEXES FOR PATIENT MANAGEMENT
-- =============================================

-- Patients indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_tenant ON patient_management.patients(tenant_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_mrn ON patient_management.patients(mrn);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_name ON patient_management.patients(last_name, first_name);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_dob ON patient_management.patients(date_of_birth);
CREATE INDEX idx_patients_active ON patient_management.patients(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

-- Patient addresses indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_addresses_patient ON patient_management.patient_addresses(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_addresses_current ON patient_management.patient_addresses(is_current) WHERE is_current = TRUE;

-- Patient contacts indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_contacts_patient ON patient_management.patient_contacts(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_contacts_primary ON patient_management.patient_contacts(is_primary) WHERE is_primary = TRUE;

-- Emergency contacts indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_emergency_contacts_patient ON patient_management.patient_emergency_contacts(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_emergency_contacts_priority ON patient_management.patient_emergency_contacts(priority);

-- Insurance indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_insurance_patient ON patient_management.patient_insurance(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_insurance_active ON patient_management.patient_insurance(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_insurance_expiry ON patient_management.patient_insurance(expiration_date);

-- Consent indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_consents_patient ON patient_management.patient_consents(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_consents_active ON patient_management.patient_consents(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

-- Identifier indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_identifiers_patient ON patient_management.patient_identifiers(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_identifiers_verified ON patient_management.patient_identifiers(is_verified) WHERE is_verified = TRUE;

-- Allergy indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_allergies_patient ON patient_management.patient_allergies(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_patient_allergies_active ON patient_management.patient_allergies(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;

-- =============================================
-- PARTITIONING FOR PATIENTS TABLE
-- =============================================

-- Create partitions for each tenant (example for first few tenants)
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patients_tenant1 
    PARTITION OF patient_management.patients 
    FOR VALUES IN ('tenant1-uuid-here');

CREATE TABLE patient_management.patients_tenant2 
    PARTITION OF patient_management.patients 
    FOR VALUES IN ('tenant2-uuid-here');

-- Default partition
CREATE TABLE patient_management.patients_default 
    PARTITION OF patient_management.patients 
    DEFAULT;

-- =============================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY POLICIES
-- =============================================

-- Enable RLS on all patient tables
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patients ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_addresses ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_contacts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_emergency_contacts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_insurance ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_consents ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_identifiers ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE patient_management.patient_allergies ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Tenant isolation policies
CREATE POLICY policy_patient_tenant_isolation ON patient_management.patients
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_patient_data_access ON patient_management.patient_addresses
    USING (EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM patient_management.patients p 
        WHERE p.patient_id = patient_addresses.patient_id 
        AND p.tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID
    ));

-- Similar policies for all other patient-related tables...

-- =============================================
-- AUDIT TRIGGERS
-- =============================================

CREATE TRIGGER trg_patients_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON patient_management.patients 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_patient_addresses_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON patient_management.patient_addresses 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_patient_contacts_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON patient_management.patient_contacts 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

-- Apply to all other tables...

-- =============================================
-- COMMENTS FOR DOCUMENTATION
-- =============================================

COMMENT ON SCHEMA patient_management IS 'Patient demographic and administrative data management';
COMMENT ON TABLE patient_management.patients IS 'Core patient demographic information with MRN';
COMMENT ON TABLE patient_management.patient_addresses IS 'Patient address history with SCD Type 2 tracking';
COMMENT ON TABLE patient_management.patient_contacts IS 'Multiple contact methods for patients';
COMMENT ON TABLE patient_management.patient_emergency_contacts IS 'Emergency contact information';

COMMENT ON COLUMN patient_management.patient_identifiers.identifier_value IS 'Encrypted storage of sensitive identification numbers';
COMMENT ON COLUMN patient_management.patients.mrn IS 'Medical Record Number - unique within tenant';
```

## Domain 2 Implementation Complete

✅ Patient Management Domain DDL generated with:

- 8 Core Tables with comprehensive patient data
- SCD Type 2 for address history tracking
- Encryption-ready fields for sensitive data
- Partitioning by tenant for scalability
- RLS Policies for patient privacy
- Complex constraints for data validation
- Full audit trails and soft delete

Key Features:

- Complete patient demographic management
- Multiple address and contact support
- Insurance and consent tracking
- Allergy and identifier management
- Tenant-based partitioning
- Privacy and security enforcement

---
---
I'll proceed with Domain 3: RX Management DDL generation. This is a critical clinical domain with complex safety requirements.

## Domain 3: RX Management DDL


```sql
-- =============================================
-- RX MANAGEMENT DOMAIN
-- Electronic prescribing with safety controls
-- =============================================

-- Create RX management schema
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS rx_management;

-- Custom domain types for RX management
CREATE DOMAIN rx_management.rx_number_type AS VARCHAR(30);
CREATE DOMAIN rx_management.drug_quantity_type AS NUMERIC(10,3);
CREATE DOMAIN rx_management.blood_group_type AS SMALLINT 
    CHECK (VALUE IN (1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8));

-- =============================================
-- RX_PRESCRIPTIONS TABLE
-- Master prescription records with digital signatures
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_prescriptions (
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Business identifier
    rx_number rx_management.rx_number_type NOT NULL,
    
    -- Prescription type
    prescription_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (prescription_type IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=OPD, 2=IPD, 3=Emergency
    
    -- Links to other domains
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    encounter_id UUID, -- FK to clinical encounters
    prescriber_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Validity period
    prescribed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    is_electronic BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Digital signature (for e-prescriptions)
    digital_signature_hash BYTEA,
    signed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    signing_certificate_id VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Status management
    prescription_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (prescription_status IN (1,2,3,4,5)),
    -- 1=Active, 2=Partially Dispensed, 3=Fully Dispensed, 4=Cancelled, 5=Expired
    cancellation_reason TEXT,
    cancelled_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    cancelled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Refill management
    max_refills_allowed SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    refills_used SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Clinical information
    clinical_notes TEXT,
    diagnosis_codes TEXT[], -- Array of ICD-10 codes
    
    -- Prior authorization
    requires_prior_auth BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    prior_auth_number VARCHAR(100),
    prior_auth_approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Medication reconciliation
    is_reconciled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    reconciled_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    reconciled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Polypharmacy monitoring
    polypharmacy_flag BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    -- Soft delete
    is_deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    PRIMARY KEY (prescription_id, prescribed_at),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_rx_number UNIQUE (tenant_id, rx_number),
    CONSTRAINT chk_rx_valid_period CHECK (valid_until > prescribed_at),
    CONSTRAINT chk_refill_limit CHECK (refills_used <= max_refills_allowed),
    CONSTRAINT chk_rx_signature CHECK (
        (is_electronic = TRUE AND digital_signature_hash IS NOT NULL AND signed_at IS NOT NULL) OR
        (is_electronic = FALSE)
    )
) PARTITION BY RANGE (prescribed_at);

-- =============================================
-- RX_PRESCRIPTION_ITEMS TABLE
-- Individual drug line items
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_prescription_items (
    item_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Drug information
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL, -- FK to drug master
    drug_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    drug_generic_name VARCHAR(255),
    drug_code VARCHAR(50), -- ATC/NDC/local code
    
    -- Dosage information
    dose_amount rx_management.drug_quantity_type NOT NULL,
    dose_unit VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, -- mg, ml, IU, etc.
    dose_form VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- tablet, capsule, syrup, injection
    route_of_admin SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (route_of_admin IN (1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8)),
    -- 1=Oral, 2=IV, 3=IM, 4=SC, 5=Topical, 6=Inhaled, 7=SL, 8=Rectal
    
    -- Frequency & duration
    frequency_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- BD, TDS, OD, QID
    frequency_per_day SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    duration_days SMALLINT,
    instructions TEXT,
    
    -- Quantity management
    qty_prescribed NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    qty_dispensed NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    qty_remaining NUMERIC(10,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (qty_prescribed - qty_dispensed) STORED,
    
    -- Controlled substances
    is_controlled_substance BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    controlled_substance_schedule VARCHAR(10), -- Schedule II, III, etc.
    witness_user_id UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    witness_signature_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Prior authorization (item-level)
    requires_prior_auth BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    prior_auth_status SMALLINT CHECK (prior_auth_status IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Pending, 2=Approved, 3=Denied
    
    -- Item status
    item_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (item_status IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Active, 2=Dispensed, 3=Cancelled, 4=On Hold
    substitution_allowed BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT fk_prescription_item FOREIGN KEY (prescription_id) 
        REFERENCES rx_management.rx_prescriptions(prescription_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT chk_dispensed_qty CHECK (qty_dispensed <= qty_prescribed),
    CONSTRAINT chk_controlled_witness CHECK (
        (is_controlled_substance = TRUE AND witness_user_id IS NOT NULL) OR
        (is_controlled_substance = FALSE)
    )
);

-- =============================================
-- RX_REFILL_REQUESTS TABLE
-- Refill request management
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_refill_requests (
    refill_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES rx_management.rx_prescriptions(prescription_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    refill_number SMALLINT NOT NULL, -- Sequence number
    requested_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    refill_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (refill_status IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Pending, 2=Approved, 3=Denied, 4=Dispensed
    
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    denial_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Validity period for approved refills
    valid_from DATE NOT NULL,
    valid_until DATE NOT NULL,
    
    -- Clinical notes
    clinical_notes TEXT,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_refill_sequence UNIQUE (prescription_id, refill_number),
    CONSTRAINT chk_refill_validity CHECK (valid_until >= valid_from)
);

-- =============================================
-- RX_PRIOR_AUTH TABLE
-- Prior authorization management
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_prior_auth (
    prior_auth_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    prescription_item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES rx_management.rx_prescription_items(item_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Insurance information
    insurance_id UUID, -- FK to patient insurance
    request_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    approval_number VARCHAR(100),
    approval_date DATE,
    expiration_date DATE,
    
    -- Quantity approvals
    requested_quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    approved_quantity NUMERIC(10,2),
    
    -- Status
    status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (status IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Pending, 2=Approved, 3=Denied, 4=Partially Approved
    denial_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Processing information
    submitted_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    processed_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_approval_quantity CHECK (approved_quantity <= requested_quantity),
    CONSTRAINT chk_expiration_date CHECK (expiration_date IS NULL OR expiration_date > approval_date)
);

-- =============================================
-- RX_MEDICATION_HISTORY TABLE
-- Unified medication history across encounters
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_medication_history (
    history_id UUID NOT NULL DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Prescription reference
    prescription_id UUID REFERENCES rx_management.rx_prescriptions(prescription_id),
    
    -- Drug information
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    drug_generic_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Dosage information
    dose_amount rx_management.drug_quantity_type,
    dose_unit VARCHAR(30),
    route_of_admin SMALLINT,
    frequency_code VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Treatment period
    start_date DATE NOT NULL,
    end_date DATE,
    is_ongoing BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    discontinuation_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Prescriber information
    prescribed_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    -- Source system
    source_system VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'HMS',
    -- Allows import from external EHRs
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    PRIMARY KEY (history_id, start_date),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_dates CHECK (end_date IS NULL OR end_date >= start_date)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (start_date);

-- =============================================
-- RX_DRUG_INTERACTION_ALERTS TABLE
-- Drug interaction checking and override logging
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_drug_interaction_alerts (
    alert_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    prescription_id UUID REFERENCES rx_management.rx_prescriptions(prescription_id),
    
    -- Interaction details
    drug_a_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_a_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    drug_b_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_b_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    interaction_severity SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (interaction_severity IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Mild, 2=Moderate, 3=Severe/Contraindicated
    interaction_description TEXT NOT NULL,
    clinical_significance TEXT,
    
    -- Alert status
    alert_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (alert_status IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Fired (pending review), 2=Overridden, 3=Prescription Modified
    
    override_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    override_reason TEXT,
    override_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    alerted_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_override_requires_reason CHECK (
        (alert_status = 2 AND override_reason IS NOT NULL AND override_by IS NOT NULL) OR
        (alert_status IN (1,3))
    )
);

-- =============================================
-- INDEXES FOR RX MANAGEMENT
-- =============================================

-- Prescriptions indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_patient_status ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions(tenant_id, patient_id, prescription_status) 
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_prescriber ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions(tenant_id, prescriber_id, prescribed_at DESC) 
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_active_valid ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions(tenant_id, patient_id, valid_until DESC) 
    WHERE prescription_status IN (1,2) AND is_deleted = FALSE;
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_prior_auth ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions(tenant_id, requires_prior_auth, prescription_status) 
    WHERE requires_prior_auth = TRUE AND is_deleted = FALSE;

-- Prescription items indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_items_prescription ON rx_management.rx_prescription_items(prescription_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_items_drug ON rx_management.rx_prescription_items(tenant_id, drug_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_items_controlled ON rx_management.rx_prescription_items(tenant_id, is_controlled_substance) 
    WHERE is_controlled_substance = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_items_pending_auth ON rx_management.rx_prescription_items(tenant_id, prior_auth_status) 
    WHERE requires_prior_auth = TRUE AND prior_auth_status = 1;

-- Refill requests indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_refill_prescription ON rx_management.rx_refill_requests(prescription_id, refill_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_refill_pending ON rx_management.rx_refill_requests(tenant_id, refill_status, requested_at DESC) 
    WHERE refill_status = 1;

-- Medication history indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_med_history_patient_active ON rx_management.rx_medication_history(patient_id, is_ongoing) 
    WHERE is_ongoing = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_med_history_patient_drug ON rx_management.rx_medication_history(patient_id, drug_id, start_date DESC);

-- Drug interaction alerts indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_alerts_patient ON rx_management.rx_drug_interaction_alerts(patient_id, alerted_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_alerts_pending ON rx_management.rx_drug_interaction_alerts(tenant_id, alert_status, interaction_severity DESC) 
    WHERE alert_status = 1;
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_alerts_severe ON rx_management.rx_drug_interaction_alerts(tenant_id, alerted_at DESC) 
    WHERE interaction_severity = 3;

-- =============================================
-- PARTITIONING FOR RX TABLES
-- =============================================

-- Create monthly partitions for prescriptions
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_prescriptions_2024_01 
    PARTITION OF rx_management.rx_prescriptions 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2024-02-01');

CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_prescriptions_2024_02 
    PARTITION OF rx_management.rx_prescriptions 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-02-01') TO ('2024-03-01');

-- Create quarterly partitions for medication history
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_medication_history_2024_q1 
    PARTITION OF rx_management.rx_medication_history 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2024-04-01');

CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_medication_history_2024_q2 
    PARTITION OF rx_management.rx_medication_history 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-04-01') TO ('2024-07-01');

-- Default partitions
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_prescriptions_default 
    PARTITION OF rx_management.rx_prescriptions 
    DEFAULT;

CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_medication_history_default 
    PARTITION OF rx_management.rx_medication_history 
    DEFAULT;

-- =============================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY POLICIES
-- =============================================

-- Enable RLS on all RX tables
ALTER TABLE rx_management.rx_prescriptions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE rx_management.rx_prescription_items ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE rx_management.rx_refill_requests ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE rx_management.rx_prior_auth ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE rx_management.rx_medication_history ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE rx_management.rx_drug_interaction_alerts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Tenant isolation policies
CREATE POLICY policy_rx_tenant_isolation ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_rx_items_tenant_isolation ON rx_management.rx_prescription_items
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_refill_tenant_isolation ON rx_management.rx_refill_requests
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

-- Similar policies for other tables...

-- =============================================
-- AUDIT TRIGGERS
-- =============================================

CREATE TRIGGER trg_rx_prescriptions_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_rx_prescription_items_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON rx_management.rx_prescription_items 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_rx_refill_requests_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON rx_management.rx_refill_requests 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

-- Apply to all other tables...

-- =============================================
-- BUSINESS RULE TRIGGERS (Examples)
-- =============================================

-- Trigger to enforce refill limits
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rx_management.fn_check_refill_limit()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.refills_used > OLD.max_refills_allowed THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Refill count cannot exceed maximum allowed refills';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_check_refill_limit
    BEFORE UPDATE OF refills_used ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION rx_management.fn_check_refill_limit();

-- Trigger for polypharmacy flag
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rx_management.fn_check_polypharmacy()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    active_med_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    IF NEW.prescription_status IN (1,2) THEN -- Active or partially dispensed
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO active_med_count
        FROM rx_management.rx_prescriptions
        WHERE patient_id = NEW.patient_id
        AND prescription_status IN (1,2)
        AND is_deleted = FALSE;
        
        NEW.polypharmacy_flag := (active_med_count >= 5);
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_check_polypharmacy
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION rx_management.fn_check_polypharmacy();

-- =============================================
-- COMMENTS FOR DOCUMENTATION
-- =============================================

COMMENT ON SCHEMA rx_management IS 'Electronic prescription management with safety controls';
COMMENT ON TABLE rx_management.rx_prescriptions IS 'Master prescription records with digital signatures and validity periods';
COMMENT ON TABLE rx_management.rx_prescription_items IS 'Individual drug line items with dosage and administration details';
COMMENT ON TABLE rx_management.rx_refill_requests IS 'Prescription refill request and approval tracking';

COMMENT ON COLUMN rx_management.rx_prescriptions.digital_signature_hash IS 'SHA-256 hash of digital signature for e-prescriptions';
COMMENT ON COLUMN rx_management.rx_prescription_items.is_controlled_substance IS 'Flag for drugs requiring special handling and witness';
COMMENT ON COLUMN rx_management.rx_medication_history.source_system IS 'Source system for medication history (HMS, external EHR, etc.)';
```

```sql
-- =============================================
-- RX MANAGEMENT DOMAIN
-- Electronic prescribing with safety controls
-- =============================================

-- Create RX management schema
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS rx_management;

-- Custom domain types for RX management
CREATE DOMAIN rx_management.rx_number_type AS VARCHAR(30);
CREATE DOMAIN rx_management.drug_quantity_type AS NUMERIC(10,3);
CREATE DOMAIN rx_management.blood_group_type AS SMALLINT 
    CHECK (VALUE IN (1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8));

-- =============================================
-- RX_PRESCRIPTIONS TABLE
-- Master prescription records with digital signatures
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_prescriptions (
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Business identifier
    rx_number rx_management.rx_number_type NOT NULL,
    
    -- Prescription type
    prescription_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (prescription_type IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=OPD, 2=IPD, 3=Emergency
    
    -- Links to other domains
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    encounter_id UUID, -- FK to clinical encounters
    prescriber_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Validity period
    prescribed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    is_electronic BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Digital signature (for e-prescriptions)
    digital_signature_hash BYTEA,
    signed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    signing_certificate_id VARCHAR(100),
    
    -- Status management
    prescription_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (prescription_status IN (1,2,3,4,5)),
    -- 1=Active, 2=Partially Dispensed, 3=Fully Dispensed, 4=Cancelled, 5=Expired
    cancellation_reason TEXT,
    cancelled_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    cancelled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Refill management
    max_refills_allowed SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    refills_used SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Clinical information
    clinical_notes TEXT,
    diagnosis_codes TEXT[], -- Array of ICD-10 codes
    
    -- Prior authorization
    requires_prior_auth BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    prior_auth_number VARCHAR(100),
    prior_auth_approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Medication reconciliation
    is_reconciled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    reconciled_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    reconciled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Polypharmacy monitoring
    polypharmacy_flag BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    -- Soft delete
    is_deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    PRIMARY KEY (prescription_id, prescribed_at),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_rx_number UNIQUE (tenant_id, rx_number),
    CONSTRAINT chk_rx_valid_period CHECK (valid_until > prescribed_at),
    CONSTRAINT chk_refill_limit CHECK (refills_used <= max_refills_allowed),
    CONSTRAINT chk_rx_signature CHECK (
        (is_electronic = TRUE AND digital_signature_hash IS NOT NULL AND signed_at IS NOT NULL) OR
        (is_electronic = FALSE)
    )
) PARTITION BY RANGE (prescribed_at);

-- =============================================
-- RX_PRESCRIPTION_ITEMS TABLE
-- Individual drug line items
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_prescription_items (
    item_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Drug information
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL, -- FK to drug master
    drug_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    drug_generic_name VARCHAR(255),
    drug_code VARCHAR(50), -- ATC/NDC/local code
    
    -- Dosage information
    dose_amount rx_management.drug_quantity_type NOT NULL,
    dose_unit VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, -- mg, ml, IU, etc.
    dose_form VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- tablet, capsule, syrup, injection
    route_of_admin SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (route_of_admin IN (1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8)),
    -- 1=Oral, 2=IV, 3=IM, 4=SC, 5=Topical, 6=Inhaled, 7=SL, 8=Rectal
    
    -- Frequency & duration
    frequency_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- BD, TDS, OD, QID
    frequency_per_day SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    duration_days SMALLINT,
    instructions TEXT,
    
    -- Quantity management
    qty_prescribed NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    qty_dispensed NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    qty_remaining NUMERIC(10,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (qty_prescribed - qty_dispensed) STORED,
    
    -- Controlled substances
    is_controlled_substance BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    controlled_substance_schedule VARCHAR(10), -- Schedule II, III, etc.
    witness_user_id UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    witness_signature_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Prior authorization (item-level)
    requires_prior_auth BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    prior_auth_status SMALLINT CHECK (prior_auth_status IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Pending, 2=Approved, 3=Denied
    
    -- Item status
    item_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (item_status IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Active, 2=Dispensed, 3=Cancelled, 4=On Hold
    substitution_allowed BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT fk_prescription_item FOREIGN KEY (prescription_id) 
        REFERENCES rx_management.rx_prescriptions(prescription_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT chk_dispensed_qty CHECK (qty_dispensed <= qty_prescribed),
    CONSTRAINT chk_controlled_witness CHECK (
        (is_controlled_substance = TRUE AND witness_user_id IS NOT NULL) OR
        (is_controlled_substance = FALSE)
    )
);

-- =============================================
-- RX_REFILL_REQUESTS TABLE
-- Refill request management
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_refill_requests (
    refill_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES rx_management.rx_prescriptions(prescription_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    refill_number SMALLINT NOT NULL, -- Sequence number
    requested_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    refill_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (refill_status IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Pending, 2=Approved, 3=Denied, 4=Dispensed
    
    approved_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    denial_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Validity period for approved refills
    valid_from DATE NOT NULL,
    valid_until DATE NOT NULL,
    
    -- Clinical notes
    clinical_notes TEXT,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_refill_sequence UNIQUE (prescription_id, refill_number),
    CONSTRAINT chk_refill_validity CHECK (valid_until >= valid_from)
);

-- =============================================
-- RX_PRIOR_AUTH TABLE
-- Prior authorization management
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_prior_auth (
    prior_auth_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    prescription_item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES rx_management.rx_prescription_items(item_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Insurance information
    insurance_id UUID, -- FK to patient insurance
    request_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    approval_number VARCHAR(100),
    approval_date DATE,
    expiration_date DATE,
    
    -- Quantity approvals
    requested_quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    approved_quantity NUMERIC(10,2),
    
    -- Status
    status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (status IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Pending, 2=Approved, 3=Denied, 4=Partially Approved
    denial_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Processing information
    submitted_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    processed_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_approval_quantity CHECK (approved_quantity <= requested_quantity),
    CONSTRAINT chk_expiration_date CHECK (expiration_date IS NULL OR expiration_date > approval_date)
);

-- =============================================
-- RX_MEDICATION_HISTORY TABLE
-- Unified medication history across encounters
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_medication_history (
    history_id UUID NOT NULL DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Prescription reference
    prescription_id UUID REFERENCES rx_management.rx_prescriptions(prescription_id),
    
    -- Drug information
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    drug_generic_name VARCHAR(255),
    
    -- Dosage information
    dose_amount rx_management.drug_quantity_type,
    dose_unit VARCHAR(30),
    route_of_admin SMALLINT,
    frequency_code VARCHAR(20),
    
    -- Treatment period
    start_date DATE NOT NULL,
    end_date DATE,
    is_ongoing BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    discontinuation_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Prescriber information
    prescribed_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    -- Source system
    source_system VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'HMS',
    -- Allows import from external EHRs
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    PRIMARY KEY (history_id, start_date),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_dates CHECK (end_date IS NULL OR end_date >= start_date)
) PARTITION BY RANGE (start_date);

-- =============================================
-- RX_DRUG_INTERACTION_ALERTS TABLE
-- Drug interaction checking and override logging
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_drug_interaction_alerts (
    alert_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    prescription_id UUID REFERENCES rx_management.rx_prescriptions(prescription_id),
    
    -- Interaction details
    drug_a_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_a_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    drug_b_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_b_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    
    interaction_severity SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (interaction_severity IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Mild, 2=Moderate, 3=Severe/Contraindicated
    interaction_description TEXT NOT NULL,
    clinical_significance TEXT,
    
    -- Alert status
    alert_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (alert_status IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Fired (pending review), 2=Overridden, 3=Prescription Modified
    
    override_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    override_reason TEXT,
    override_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    alerted_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_override_requires_reason CHECK (
        (alert_status = 2 AND override_reason IS NOT NULL AND override_by IS NOT NULL) OR
        (alert_status IN (1,3))
    )
);

-- =============================================
-- INDEXES FOR RX MANAGEMENT
-- =============================================

-- Prescriptions indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_patient_status ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions(tenant_id, patient_id, prescription_status) 
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_prescriber ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions(tenant_id, prescriber_id, prescribed_at DESC) 
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_active_valid ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions(tenant_id, patient_id, valid_until DESC) 
    WHERE prescription_status IN (1,2) AND is_deleted = FALSE;
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_prior_auth ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions(tenant_id, requires_prior_auth, prescription_status) 
    WHERE requires_prior_auth = TRUE AND is_deleted = FALSE;

-- Prescription items indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_items_prescription ON rx_management.rx_prescription_items(prescription_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_items_drug ON rx_management.rx_prescription_items(tenant_id, drug_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_items_controlled ON rx_management.rx_prescription_items(tenant_id, is_controlled_substance) 
    WHERE is_controlled_substance = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_rx_items_pending_auth ON rx_management.rx_prescription_items(tenant_id, prior_auth_status) 
    WHERE requires_prior_auth = TRUE AND prior_auth_status = 1;

-- Refill requests indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_refill_prescription ON rx_management.rx_refill_requests(prescription_id, refill_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_refill_pending ON rx_management.rx_refill_requests(tenant_id, refill_status, requested_at DESC) 
    WHERE refill_status = 1;

-- Medication history indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_med_history_patient_active ON rx_management.rx_medication_history(patient_id, is_ongoing) 
    WHERE is_ongoing = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_med_history_patient_drug ON rx_management.rx_medication_history(patient_id, drug_id, start_date DESC);

-- Drug interaction alerts indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_alerts_patient ON rx_management.rx_drug_interaction_alerts(patient_id, alerted_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_alerts_pending ON rx_management.rx_drug_interaction_alerts(tenant_id, alert_status, interaction_severity DESC) 
    WHERE alert_status = 1;
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_alerts_severe ON rx_management.rx_drug_interaction_alerts(tenant_id, alerted_at DESC) 
    WHERE interaction_severity = 3;

-- =============================================
-- PARTITIONING FOR RX TABLES
-- =============================================

-- Create monthly partitions for prescriptions
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_prescriptions_2024_01 
    PARTITION OF rx_management.rx_prescriptions 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2024-02-01');

CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_prescriptions_2024_02 
    PARTITION OF rx_management.rx_prescriptions 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-02-01') TO ('2024-03-01');

-- Create quarterly partitions for medication history
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_medication_history_2024_q1 
    PARTITION OF rx_management.rx_medication_history 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2024-04-01');

CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_medication_history_2024_q2 
    PARTITION OF rx_management.rx_medication_history 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-04-01') TO ('2024-07-01');

-- Default partitions
CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_prescriptions_default 
    PARTITION OF rx_management.rx_prescriptions 
    DEFAULT;

CREATE TABLE rx_management.rx_medication_history_default 
    PARTITION OF rx_management.rx_medication_history 
    DEFAULT;

-- =============================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY POLICIES
-- =============================================

-- Enable RLS on all RX tables
ALTER TABLE rx_management.rx_prescriptions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE rx_management.rx_prescription_items ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE rx_management.rx_refill_requests ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE rx_management.rx_prior_auth ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE rx_management.rx_medication_history ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE rx_management.rx_drug_interaction_alerts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Tenant isolation policies
CREATE POLICY policy_rx_tenant_isolation ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_rx_items_tenant_isolation ON rx_management.rx_prescription_items
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_refill_tenant_isolation ON rx_management.rx_refill_requests
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

-- Similar policies for other tables...

-- =============================================
-- AUDIT TRIGGERS
-- =============================================

CREATE TRIGGER trg_rx_prescriptions_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_rx_prescription_items_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON rx_management.rx_prescription_items 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_rx_refill_requests_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON rx_management.rx_refill_requests 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

-- Apply to all other tables...

-- =============================================
-- BUSINESS RULE TRIGGERS (Examples)
-- =============================================

-- Trigger to enforce refill limits
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rx_management.fn_check_refill_limit()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.refills_used > OLD.max_refills_allowed THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Refill count cannot exceed maximum allowed refills';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_check_refill_limit
    BEFORE UPDATE OF refills_used ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION rx_management.fn_check_refill_limit();

-- Trigger for polypharmacy flag
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION rx_management.fn_check_polypharmacy()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    active_med_count INTEGER;
BEGIN
    IF NEW.prescription_status IN (1,2) THEN -- Active or partially dispensed
        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO active_med_count
        FROM rx_management.rx_prescriptions
        WHERE patient_id = NEW.patient_id
        AND prescription_status IN (1,2)
        AND is_deleted = FALSE;
        
        NEW.polypharmacy_flag := (active_med_count >= 5);
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_check_polypharmacy
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON rx_management.rx_prescriptions
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION rx_management.fn_check_polypharmacy();

-- =============================================
-- COMMENTS FOR DOCUMENTATION
-- =============================================

COMMENT ON SCHEMA rx_management IS 'Electronic prescription management with safety controls';
COMMENT ON TABLE rx_management.rx_prescriptions IS 'Master prescription records with digital signatures and validity periods';
COMMENT ON TABLE rx_management.rx_prescription_items IS 'Individual drug line items with dosage and administration details';
COMMENT ON TABLE rx_management.rx_refill_requests IS 'Prescription refill request and approval tracking';

COMMENT ON COLUMN rx_management.rx_prescriptions.digital_signature_hash IS 'SHA-256 hash of digital signature for e-prescriptions';
COMMENT ON COLUMN rx_management.rx_prescription_items.is_controlled_substance IS 'Flag for drugs requiring special handling and witness';
COMMENT ON COLUMN rx_management.rx_medication_history.source_system IS 'Source system for medication history (HMS, external EHR, etc.)';
```
