## Domain 4: Reagent Management DDL

sql

```sql
-- =============================================
-- REAGENT MANAGEMENT DOMAIN
-- Laboratory reagent tracking with quality control
-- =============================================

-- Create reagent management schema
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS reagent_management;

-- Custom domain types for reagent management
CREATE DOMAIN reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type AS NUMERIC(10,4);
CREATE DOMAIN reagent_management.temperature_c_type AS NUMERIC(4,1) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN -30 AND 60);
CREATE DOMAIN reagent_management.percentage_type AS NUMERIC(5,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0 AND VALUE <= 100);

-- =============================================
-- REAGENT_MASTER TABLE
-- Master catalog of laboratory reagents
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_master (
    reagent_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    reagent_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    reagent_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    reagent_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (reagent_type IN (1,2,3,4,5)),
    -- 1=Assay Reagent, 2=QC Material, 3=Calibrator, 4=Diluent, 5=Wash Solution
    
    -- Analyzer compatibility
    analyzer_id UUID, -- FK to lab analyzers
    analyzer_compatible TEXT[], -- Compatible analyzer models
    test_ids UUID[], -- Tests this reagent is used for
    
    -- Inventory rules
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, -- ml, test-count, kit, etc.
    reorder_level reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    reorder_quantity reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    lead_time_days SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 7,
    
    -- Storage requirements
    storage_temperature_min reagent_management.temperature_c_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 2,
    storage_temperature_max reagent_management.temperature_c_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 8,
    requires_refrigeration BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    requires_dark_storage BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    hazmat_classification VARCHAR(50), -- Biohazardous/chemical classification
    
    -- Status
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    -- Soft delete
    is_deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_reagent_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, reagent_code) 
        WHERE is_deleted = FALSE
);

-- =============================================
-- REAGENT_LOTS TABLE
-- Individual reagent lots with expiry tracking
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_lots (
    lot_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    reagent_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES reagent_management.reagent_master(reagent_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    lot_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    manufacturer_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    supplier_id UUID, -- FK to suppliers
    
    -- Date management
    manufacture_date DATE,
    expiry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    received_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    opened_date DATE,
    open_stability_days SMALLINT, -- Stability after opening
    
    -- Generated expiry dates
    in_use_expiry_date DATE GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE WHEN opened_date IS NOT NULL AND open_stability_days IS NOT NULL
             THEN opened_date + open_stability_days
             ELSE NULL
        END
    ) STORED,
    
    effective_expiry_date DATE GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE
            WHEN opened_date IS NOT NULL AND open_stability_days IS NOT NULL
            THEN LEAST(expiry_date, opened_date + open_stability_days)
            ELSE expiry_date
        END
    ) STORED,
    
    -- Quantity management
    quantity_received reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type NOT NULL,
    quantity_available reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type NOT NULL,
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    
    -- QC Status (gate before clinical use)
    qc_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (qc_status IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Pending QC, 2=QC Passed, 3=QC Failed, 4=Released for Use
    
    qc_verified_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    qc_verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    qc_failure_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Lot status
    lot_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (lot_status IN (1,2,3,4,5)),
    -- 1=Quarantine, 2=Released, 3=In Use, 4=Exhausted, 5=Recalled/Discarded
    
    recall_reason TEXT,
    discarded_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    discarded_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    -- Storage location
    storage_location VARCHAR(100),
    refrigerator_id UUID, -- FK to asset equipment
    
    -- Cost information
    unit_cost core.currency_type,
    currency_code VARCHAR(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USD',
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_lot_number UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, reagent_id, lot_number),
    CONSTRAINT chk_lot_expiry CHECK (expiry_date > COALESCE(manufacture_date, '1900-01-01'::DATE)),
    CONSTRAINT chk_lot_qty CHECK (quantity_available <= quantity_received),
    CONSTRAINT chk_qc_gate CHECK (
        (lot_status = 4 AND qc_status = 4) OR (lot_status != 4)
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_expiry_block CHECK (
        NOT (lot_status IN (2,3,4) AND effective_expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE)
    )
);

-- =============================================
-- REAGENT_QC_LOGS TABLE
-- Westgard QC rules tracking
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs (
    qc_log_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    lot_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES reagent_management.reagent_lots(lot_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    analyzer_id UUID,
    qc_level SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (qc_level IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Level 1 (Low), 2=Level 2 (Normal), 3=Level 3 (High)
    
    -- Measurement values
    measured_value NUMERIC(15,6) NOT NULL,
    unit VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    mean_value NUMERIC(15,6) NOT NULL,
    sd_value NUMERIC(15,6) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Generated CV%
    cv_percent reagent_management.percentage_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE WHEN mean_value != 0
             THEN ROUND((sd_value / mean_value * 100)::NUMERIC, 2)
             ELSE NULL
        END
    ) STORED,
    
    -- Westgard Rules Evaluation
    westgard_1_2s BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,  -- Warning: 1 value > 2SD
    westgard_1_3s BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,  -- Rejection: 1 value > 3SD
    westgard_2_2s BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,  -- Rejection: 2 consecutive > 2SD
    westgard_r_4s BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,  -- Rejection: range > 4SD
    westgard_4_1s BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,  -- Warning: 4 consecutive > 1SD
    westgard_10x BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,  -- Rejection: 10 on same side of mean
    
    -- Overall QC decision
    qc_passed BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    failure_rules TEXT[], -- Which Westgard rules fired
    action_taken TEXT,
    
    -- Clinical blocking
    clinical_use_blocked BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    blocked_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    unblocked_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    unblocked_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Performed by
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    performed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- =============================================
-- REAGENT_USAGE_LOGS TABLE
-- Reagent consumption tracking
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs (
    usage_id UUID NOT NULL DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    lot_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES reagent_management.reagent_lots(lot_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Traceability
    lab_result_id UUID, -- FK to lab_results
    lab_order_id UUID, -- FK to lab_orders
    analyzer_id UUID,
    
    -- Usage details
    quantity_used reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type NOT NULL CHECK (quantity_used > 0),
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    unit_cost core.currency_type,
    
    -- Generated total cost
    total_cost core.currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (quantity_used * COALESCE(unit_cost, 0)) STORED,
    
    used_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    used_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    PRIMARY KEY (usage_id, used_at),
    
    CONSTRAINT fk_usage_lot FOREIGN KEY (lot_id) 
        REFERENCES reagent_management.reagent_lots(lot_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
) PARTITION BY RANGE (used_at);

-- =============================================
-- REAGENT_CALIBRATION_LOGS TABLE
-- Analyzer calibration tracking
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_calibration_logs (
    calibration_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    analyzer_id UUID NOT NULL,
    calibrator_lot_id UUID REFERENCES reagent_management.reagent_lots(lot_id),
    
    calibration_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (calibration_type IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Daily startup, 2=New lot verification, 3=Corrective
    
    calibration_result SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (calibration_result IN (1,2)),
    -- 1=Passed, 2=Failed
    
    failure_reason TEXT,
    corrective_action TEXT,
    
    -- Clinical hold
    analyzer_held BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    held_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    performed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ, -- Calibration validity window
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- =============================================
-- REAGENT_INVENTORY_TRANSACTIONS TABLE
-- Stock movement tracking
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_inventory_transactions (
    transaction_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    lot_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES reagent_management.reagent_lots(lot_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    transaction_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (transaction_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Receipt, 2=Issue, 3=Adjustment, 4=Wastage
    
    quantity reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type NOT NULL,
    reference_number VARCHAR(100), -- PO, Issue number, etc.
    reason TEXT, -- For adjustments/wastage
    
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    performed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    -- Generated balance
    balance_after reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE transaction_type
            WHEN 1 THEN quantity -- Receipt
            WHEN 2 THEN -quantity -- Issue
            WHEN 3 THEN quantity -- Adjustment (could be + or - but typically absolute)
            WHEN 4 THEN -quantity -- Wastage
            ELSE 0
        END
    ) STORED
);

-- =============================================
-- INDEXES FOR REAGENT MANAGEMENT
-- =============================================

-- Reagent master indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_master_type ON reagent_management.reagent_master(tenant_id, branch_id, reagent_type) 
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_analyzer ON reagent_management.reagent_master USING GIN(analyzer_compatible) 
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

-- Reagent lots indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_lots_reagent ON reagent_management.reagent_lots(reagent_id, lot_status, effective_expiry_date ASC);
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_lots_expiry_alert ON reagent_management.reagent_lots(tenant_id, branch_id, effective_expiry_date ASC) 
    WHERE lot_status IN (2,3);
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_lots_qc_pending ON reagent_management.reagent_lots(tenant_id, qc_status) 
    WHERE qc_status = 1;
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_lots_fefo ON reagent_management.reagent_lots(reagent_id, effective_expiry_date ASC, quantity_available) 
    WHERE lot_status IN (2,3) AND quantity_available > 0;

-- QC logs indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_qc_lot ON reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs(lot_id, performed_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_qc_failures ON reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs(tenant_id, branch_id, performed_at DESC) 
    WHERE qc_passed = FALSE;
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_qc_blocked ON reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs(tenant_id, clinical_use_blocked, blocked_until) 
    WHERE clinical_use_blocked = TRUE;

-- Usage logs indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_usage_lot ON reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs(lot_id, used_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_usage_result ON reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs(lab_result_id, used_at DESC) 
    WHERE lab_result_id IS NOT NULL;

-- Calibration logs indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_calibration_analyzer ON reagent_management.reagent_calibration_logs(analyzer_id, performed_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_calibration_failures ON reagent_management.reagent_calibration_logs(tenant_id, calibration_result, performed_at DESC) 
    WHERE calibration_result = 2;

-- =============================================
-- PARTITIONING FOR USAGE LOGS
-- =============================================

-- Create monthly partitions for usage logs
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_usage_2024_01 
    PARTITION OF reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2024-02-01');

CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_usage_2024_02 
    PARTITION OF reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-02-01') TO ('2024-03-01');

-- Default partition
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_usage_default 
    PARTITION OF reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs 
    DEFAULT;

-- =============================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY POLICIES
-- =============================================

-- Enable RLS on all reagent tables
ALTER TABLE reagent_management.reagent_master ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE reagent_management.reagent_lots ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE reagent_management.reagent_calibration_logs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE reagent_management.reagent_inventory_transactions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Tenant isolation policies
CREATE POLICY policy_reagent_tenant_isolation ON reagent_management.reagent_master
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_reagent_lots_tenant_isolation ON reagent_management.reagent_lots
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_reagent_qc_tenant_isolation ON reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

-- Similar policies for other tables...

-- =============================================
-- AUDIT TRIGGERS
-- =============================================

CREATE TRIGGER trg_reagent_master_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON reagent_management.reagent_master 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_reagent_lots_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON reagent_management.reagent_lots 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_reagent_qc_logs_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

-- Apply to all other tables...

-- =============================================
-- BUSINESS RULE TRIGGERS
-- =============================================

-- Trigger to enforce QC gate before clinical use
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION reagent_management.fn_enforce_qc_gate()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.lot_status = 4 THEN -- Released for Use
        IF NEW.qc_status != 4 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Lot cannot be released for clinical use without QC approval';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_enforce_qc_gate
    BEFORE UPDATE OF lot_status ON reagent_management.reagent_lots
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION reagent_management.fn_enforce_qc_gate();

-- Trigger to block expired reagent usage
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION reagent_management.fn_block_expired_reagent()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM reagent_management.reagent_lots rl
        WHERE rl.lot_id = NEW.lot_id
        AND rl.effective_expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Cannot use expired reagent lot';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_block_expired_reagent
    BEFORE INSERT ON reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION reagent_management.fn_block_expired_reagent();

-- =============================================
-- COMMENTS FOR DOCUMENTATION
-- =============================================

COMMENT ON SCHEMA reagent_management IS 'Laboratory reagent management with quality control and traceability';
COMMENT ON TABLE reagent_management.reagent_master IS 'Master catalog of laboratory reagents with analyzer compatibility';
COMMENT ON TABLE reagent_management.reagent_lots IS 'Individual reagent lots with expiry tracking and QC status';
COMMENT ON TABLE reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs IS 'Westgard QC rules evaluation and quality control logging';

COMMENT ON COLUMN reagent_management.reagent_lots.effective_expiry_date IS 'Minimum of lot expiry and in-use expiry for FEFO enforcement';
COMMENT ON COLUMN reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs.westgard_1_3s IS 'Westgard 1:3s rule violation (rejection rule)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs.lab_result_id IS 'Link to specific lab result for full traceability';
```

```sql
-- =============================================
-- REAGENT MANAGEMENT DOMAIN
-- Laboratory reagent tracking with quality control
-- =============================================

-- Create reagent management schema
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS reagent_management;

-- Custom domain types for reagent management
CREATE DOMAIN reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type AS NUMERIC(10,4);
CREATE DOMAIN reagent_management.temperature_c_type AS NUMERIC(4,1) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN -30 AND 60);
CREATE DOMAIN reagent_management.percentage_type AS NUMERIC(5,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0 AND VALUE <= 100);

-- =============================================
-- REAGENT_MASTER TABLE
-- Master catalog of laboratory reagents
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_master (
    reagent_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    reagent_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    reagent_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    reagent_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (reagent_type IN (1,2,3,4,5)),
    -- 1=Assay Reagent, 2=QC Material, 3=Calibrator, 4=Diluent, 5=Wash Solution
    
    -- Analyzer compatibility
    analyzer_id UUID, -- FK to lab analyzers
    analyzer_compatible TEXT[], -- Compatible analyzer models
    test_ids UUID[], -- Tests this reagent is used for
    
    -- Inventory rules
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, -- ml, test-count, kit, etc.
    reorder_level reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    reorder_quantity reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    lead_time_days SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 7,
    
    -- Storage requirements
    storage_temperature_min reagent_management.temperature_c_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 2,
    storage_temperature_max reagent_management.temperature_c_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 8,
    requires_refrigeration BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    requires_dark_storage BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    hazmat_classification VARCHAR(50), -- Biohazardous/chemical classification
    
    -- Status
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    -- Soft delete
    is_deleted BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    deleted_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_reagent_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, reagent_code) 
        WHERE is_deleted = FALSE
);

-- =============================================
-- REAGENT_LOTS TABLE
-- Individual reagent lots with expiry tracking
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_lots (
    lot_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    reagent_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES reagent_management.reagent_master(reagent_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    lot_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    manufacturer_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    supplier_id UUID, -- FK to suppliers
    
    -- Date management
    manufacture_date DATE,
    expiry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    received_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    opened_date DATE,
    open_stability_days SMALLINT, -- Stability after opening
    
    -- Generated expiry dates
    in_use_expiry_date DATE GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE WHEN opened_date IS NOT NULL AND open_stability_days IS NOT NULL
             THEN opened_date + open_stability_days
             ELSE NULL
        END
    ) STORED,
    
    effective_expiry_date DATE GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE
            WHEN opened_date IS NOT NULL AND open_stability_days IS NOT NULL
            THEN LEAST(expiry_date, opened_date + open_stability_days)
            ELSE expiry_date
        END
    ) STORED,
    
    -- Quantity management
    quantity_received reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type NOT NULL,
    quantity_available reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type NOT NULL,
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    
    -- QC Status (gate before clinical use)
    qc_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (qc_status IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Pending QC, 2=QC Passed, 3=QC Failed, 4=Released for Use
    
    qc_verified_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    qc_verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    qc_failure_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Lot status
    lot_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (lot_status IN (1,2,3,4,5)),
    -- 1=Quarantine, 2=Released, 3=In Use, 4=Exhausted, 5=Recalled/Discarded
    
    recall_reason TEXT,
    discarded_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    discarded_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    -- Storage location
    storage_location VARCHAR(100),
    refrigerator_id UUID, -- FK to asset equipment
    
    -- Cost information
    unit_cost core.currency_type,
    currency_code VARCHAR(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USD',
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_lot_number UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, reagent_id, lot_number),
    CONSTRAINT chk_lot_expiry CHECK (expiry_date > COALESCE(manufacture_date, '1900-01-01'::DATE)),
    CONSTRAINT chk_lot_qty CHECK (quantity_available <= quantity_received),
    CONSTRAINT chk_qc_gate CHECK (
        (lot_status = 4 AND qc_status = 4) OR (lot_status != 4)
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_expiry_block CHECK (
        NOT (lot_status IN (2,3,4) AND effective_expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE)
    )
);

-- =============================================
-- REAGENT_QC_LOGS TABLE
-- Westgard QC rules tracking
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs (
    qc_log_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    lot_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES reagent_management.reagent_lots(lot_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    analyzer_id UUID,
    qc_level SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (qc_level IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Level 1 (Low), 2=Level 2 (Normal), 3=Level 3 (High)
    
    -- Measurement values
    measured_value NUMERIC(15,6) NOT NULL,
    unit VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    mean_value NUMERIC(15,6) NOT NULL,
    sd_value NUMERIC(15,6) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Generated CV%
    cv_percent reagent_management.percentage_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE WHEN mean_value != 0
             THEN ROUND((sd_value / mean_value * 100)::NUMERIC, 2)
             ELSE NULL
        END
    ) STORED,
    
    -- Westgard Rules Evaluation
    westgard_1_2s BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,  -- Warning: 1 value > 2SD
    westgard_1_3s BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,  -- Rejection: 1 value > 3SD
    westgard_2_2s BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,  -- Rejection: 2 consecutive > 2SD
    westgard_r_4s BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,  -- Rejection: range > 4SD
    westgard_4_1s BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,  -- Warning: 4 consecutive > 1SD
    westgard_10x BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,  -- Rejection: 10 on same side of mean
    
    -- Overall QC decision
    qc_passed BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    failure_rules TEXT[], -- Which Westgard rules fired
    action_taken TEXT,
    
    -- Clinical blocking
    clinical_use_blocked BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    blocked_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    unblocked_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    unblocked_reason TEXT,
    
    -- Performed by
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    performed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    verified_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- =============================================
-- REAGENT_USAGE_LOGS TABLE
-- Reagent consumption tracking
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs (
    usage_id UUID NOT NULL DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    lot_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES reagent_management.reagent_lots(lot_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Traceability
    lab_result_id UUID, -- FK to lab_results
    lab_order_id UUID, -- FK to lab_orders
    analyzer_id UUID,
    
    -- Usage details
    quantity_used reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type NOT NULL CHECK (quantity_used > 0),
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    unit_cost core.currency_type,
    
    -- Generated total cost
    total_cost core.currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (quantity_used * COALESCE(unit_cost, 0)) STORED,
    
    used_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    used_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    PRIMARY KEY (usage_id, used_at),
    
    CONSTRAINT fk_usage_lot FOREIGN KEY (lot_id) 
        REFERENCES reagent_management.reagent_lots(lot_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
) PARTITION BY RANGE (used_at);

-- =============================================
-- REAGENT_CALIBRATION_LOGS TABLE
-- Analyzer calibration tracking
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_calibration_logs (
    calibration_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    analyzer_id UUID NOT NULL,
    calibrator_lot_id UUID REFERENCES reagent_management.reagent_lots(lot_id),
    
    calibration_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (calibration_type IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Daily startup, 2=New lot verification, 3=Corrective
    
    calibration_result SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (calibration_result IN (1,2)),
    -- 1=Passed, 2=Failed
    
    failure_reason TEXT,
    corrective_action TEXT,
    
    -- Clinical hold
    analyzer_held BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    held_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    performed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ, -- Calibration validity window
    
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- =============================================
-- REAGENT_INVENTORY_TRANSACTIONS TABLE
-- Stock movement tracking
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_inventory_transactions (
    transaction_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    lot_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES reagent_management.reagent_lots(lot_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    transaction_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (transaction_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Receipt, 2=Issue, 3=Adjustment, 4=Wastage
    
    quantity reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type NOT NULL,
    reference_number VARCHAR(100), -- PO, Issue number, etc.
    reason TEXT, -- For adjustments/wastage
    
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    performed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    -- Generated balance
    balance_after reagent_management.reagent_quantity_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE transaction_type
            WHEN 1 THEN quantity -- Receipt
            WHEN 2 THEN -quantity -- Issue
            WHEN 3 THEN quantity -- Adjustment (could be + or - but typically absolute)
            WHEN 4 THEN -quantity -- Wastage
            ELSE 0
        END
    ) STORED
);

-- =============================================
-- INDEXES FOR REAGENT MANAGEMENT
-- =============================================

-- Reagent master indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_master_type ON reagent_management.reagent_master(tenant_id, branch_id, reagent_type) 
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_analyzer ON reagent_management.reagent_master USING GIN(analyzer_compatible) 
    WHERE is_deleted = FALSE;

-- Reagent lots indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_lots_reagent ON reagent_management.reagent_lots(reagent_id, lot_status, effective_expiry_date ASC);
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_lots_expiry_alert ON reagent_management.reagent_lots(tenant_id, branch_id, effective_expiry_date ASC) 
    WHERE lot_status IN (2,3);
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_lots_qc_pending ON reagent_management.reagent_lots(tenant_id, qc_status) 
    WHERE qc_status = 1;
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_lots_fefo ON reagent_management.reagent_lots(reagent_id, effective_expiry_date ASC, quantity_available) 
    WHERE lot_status IN (2,3) AND quantity_available > 0;

-- QC logs indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_qc_lot ON reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs(lot_id, performed_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_qc_failures ON reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs(tenant_id, branch_id, performed_at DESC) 
    WHERE qc_passed = FALSE;
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_qc_blocked ON reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs(tenant_id, clinical_use_blocked, blocked_until) 
    WHERE clinical_use_blocked = TRUE;

-- Usage logs indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_usage_lot ON reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs(lot_id, used_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_reagent_usage_result ON reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs(lab_result_id, used_at DESC) 
    WHERE lab_result_id IS NOT NULL;

-- Calibration logs indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_calibration_analyzer ON reagent_management.reagent_calibration_logs(analyzer_id, performed_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_calibration_failures ON reagent_management.reagent_calibration_logs(tenant_id, calibration_result, performed_at DESC) 
    WHERE calibration_result = 2;

-- =============================================
-- PARTITIONING FOR USAGE LOGS
-- =============================================

-- Create monthly partitions for usage logs
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_usage_2024_01 
    PARTITION OF reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2024-02-01');

CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_usage_2024_02 
    PARTITION OF reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs 
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-02-01') TO ('2024-03-01');

-- Default partition
CREATE TABLE reagent_management.reagent_usage_default 
    PARTITION OF reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs 
    DEFAULT;

-- =============================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY POLICIES
-- =============================================

-- Enable RLS on all reagent tables
ALTER TABLE reagent_management.reagent_master ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE reagent_management.reagent_lots ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE reagent_management.reagent_calibration_logs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE reagent_management.reagent_inventory_transactions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Tenant isolation policies
CREATE POLICY policy_reagent_tenant_isolation ON reagent_management.reagent_master
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_reagent_lots_tenant_isolation ON reagent_management.reagent_lots
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_reagent_qc_tenant_isolation ON reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

-- Similar policies for other tables...

-- =============================================
-- AUDIT TRIGGERS
-- =============================================

CREATE TRIGGER trg_reagent_master_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON reagent_management.reagent_master 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_reagent_lots_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON reagent_management.reagent_lots 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_reagent_qc_logs_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

-- Apply to all other tables...

-- =============================================
-- BUSINESS RULE TRIGGERS
-- =============================================

-- Trigger to enforce QC gate before clinical use
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION reagent_management.fn_enforce_qc_gate()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.lot_status = 4 THEN -- Released for Use
        IF NEW.qc_status != 4 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Lot cannot be released for clinical use without QC approval';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_enforce_qc_gate
    BEFORE UPDATE OF lot_status ON reagent_management.reagent_lots
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION reagent_management.fn_enforce_qc_gate();

-- Trigger to block expired reagent usage
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION reagent_management.fn_block_expired_reagent()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM reagent_management.reagent_lots rl
        WHERE rl.lot_id = NEW.lot_id
        AND rl.effective_expiry_date < CURRENT_DATE
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Cannot use expired reagent lot';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_block_expired_reagent
    BEFORE INSERT ON reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION reagent_management.fn_block_expired_reagent();

-- =============================================
-- COMMENTS FOR DOCUMENTATION
-- =============================================

COMMENT ON SCHEMA reagent_management IS 'Laboratory reagent management with quality control and traceability';
COMMENT ON TABLE reagent_management.reagent_master IS 'Master catalog of laboratory reagents with analyzer compatibility';
COMMENT ON TABLE reagent_management.reagent_lots IS 'Individual reagent lots with expiry tracking and QC status';
COMMENT ON TABLE reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs IS 'Westgard QC rules evaluation and quality control logging';

COMMENT ON COLUMN reagent_management.reagent_lots.effective_expiry_date IS 'Minimum of lot expiry and in-use expiry for FEFO enforcement';
COMMENT ON COLUMN reagent_management.reagent_qc_logs.westgard_1_3s IS 'Westgard 1:3s rule violation (rejection rule)';
COMMENT ON COLUMN reagent_management.reagent_usage_logs.lab_result_id IS 'Link to specific lab result for full traceability';
```

- QC Enforcement with Westgard rules implementation

- Lot Traceability to individual test results
- Temperature Monitoring with validation
- Business Rule Triggers for quality gates
- Partitioning for high-volume usage data

- Expiry date validation triggers

## Domain 5: Kitchen/Nutrition Management DDL

sql

```sql
-- =============================================
-- KITCHEN/NUTRITION MANAGEMENT DOMAIN
-- Patient dietary services with allergy safety
-- =============================================

-- Create kitchen/nutrition schema
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS kitchen_management;

-- Custom domain types for kitchen management
CREATE DOMAIN kitchen_management.temperature_c_type AS NUMERIC(4,1) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN -30 AND 60);
CREATE DOMAIN kitchen_management.percentage_type AS NUMERIC(5,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0 AND VALUE <= 100);

-- =============================================
-- MENU_CATEGORIES TABLE
-- Meal category organization
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.menu_categories (
    category_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    category_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    -- Breakfast, Lunch, Dinner, Snacks, Therapeutic
    serving_time_start TIME NOT NULL,
    serving_time_end TIME NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    display_order SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_category_name UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, category_name),
    CONSTRAINT chk_serving_times CHECK (serving_time_end > serving_time_start)
);

-- =============================================
-- MENU_ITEMS TABLE
-- Dish master with nutritional information
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.menu_items (
    item_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    category_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.menu_categories(category_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    item_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    item_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    portion_size VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Nutritional information
    calories NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    protein_g NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    carbs_g NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    fat_g NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    fiber_g NUMERIC(6,1),
    sodium_mg NUMERIC(6,1),
    sugar_g NUMERIC(6,1),
    
    -- Dietary restrictions
    is_vegetarian BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_vegan BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_gluten_free BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_halal BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_kosher BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Preparation details
    preparation_time_min SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    holding_temp_min kitchen_management.temperature_c_type NOT NULL,
    holding_temp_max kitchen_management.temperature_c_type NOT NULL,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_item_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, item_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_holding_temp CHECK (holding_temp_max > holding_temp_min)
);

-- =============================================
-- MENU_ITEM_ALLERGENS TABLE
-- Allergen information for menu items
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.menu_item_allergens (
    item_allergen_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    menu_item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.menu_items(item_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    allergen_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    -- Common allergens: MILK, EGGS, FISH, SHELLFISH, TREENUTS, PEANUTS, WHEAT, SOY
    severity SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (severity IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Contains, 2=May Contain, 3=Processed in same facility
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_item_allergen UNIQUE (menu_item_id, allergen_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- DIET_TYPES TABLE
-- Therapeutic diet definitions
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.diet_types (
    diet_type_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    diet_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    diet_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    -- General, Diabetic, Renal, Cardiac, Low-Sodium, etc.
    description TEXT,
    
    -- Nutritional ranges
    calorie_range_min NUMERIC(6,1),
    calorie_range_max NUMERIC(6,1),
    protein_range_min NUMERIC(6,1),
    protein_range_max NUMERIC(6,1),
    sodium_range_max NUMERIC(6,1),
    fluid_restriction_max_ml NUMERIC(6,1),
    
    -- Allergen restrictions
    allowed_allergens TEXT[],
    restricted_allergens TEXT[],
    
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_diet_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, diet_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_calorie_range CHECK (calorie_range_max >= calorie_range_min),
    CONSTRAINT chk_protein_range CHECK (protein_range_max >= protein_range_min)
);

-- =============================================
-- DIET_ORDERS TABLE
-- Patient-specific diet prescriptions
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.diet_orders (
    diet_order_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Patient and admission links
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL, -- FK to IPD admissions
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    diet_type_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.diet_types(diet_type_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Order details
    start_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    end_date DATE,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    is_nil_by_mouth BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Allergen safety
    allergen_check_passed BOOLEAN,
    allergen_checked_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    allergen_checked_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Doctor approval
    doctor_approved_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    doctor_approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Modifications
    special_instructions TEXT,
    texture_modification SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (texture_modification IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Normal, 2=Minced, 3=Pureed
    
    fluid_restriction_ml NUMERIC(6,1),
    
    -- Order status
    order_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (order_status IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Active, 2=On Hold, 3=Completed, 4=Cancelled
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_dates CHECK (end_date IS NULL OR end_date >= start_date)
);

-- =============================================
-- MEAL_PLANS TABLE
-- Daily meal assignments
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.meal_plans (
    meal_plan_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    diet_order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.diet_orders(diet_order_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    plan_date DATE NOT NULL,
    meal_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (meal_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Breakfast, 2=Lunch, 3=Dinner, 4=Snack
    
    menu_item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.menu_items(item_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    planned_quantity SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    actual_quantity SMALLINT,
    
    -- Generated nutritional values
    planned_calories NUMERIC(6,1) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        planned_quantity * (SELECT calories FROM kitchen_management.menu_items WHERE item_id = menu_item_id)
    ) STORED,
    planned_protein NUMERIC(6,1) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        planned_quantity * (SELECT protein_g FROM kitchen_management.menu_items WHERE item_id = menu_item_id)
    ) STORED,
    planned_carbs NUMERIC(6,1) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        planned_quantity * (SELECT carbs_g FROM kitchen_management.menu_items WHERE item_id = menu_item_id)
    ) STORED,
    planned_fat NUMERIC(6,1) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        planned_quantity * (SELECT fat_g FROM kitchen_management.menu_items WHERE item_id = menu_item_id)
    ) STORED,
    
    is_served BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    served_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_daily_meal UNIQUE (diet_order_id, plan_date, meal_type)
);

-- =============================================
-- MEAL_DELIVERIES TABLE
-- Actual meal delivery tracking
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.meal_deliveries (
    delivery_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    diet_order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.diet_orders(diet_order_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    meal_plan_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.meal_plans(meal_plan_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    delivered_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    delivered_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    received_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    temperature_at_delivery kitchen_management.temperature_c_type,
    is_accepted BOOLEAN,
    rejection_reason TEXT,
    
    delivery_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (delivery_status IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Prepared, 2=In Transit, 3=Delivered, 4=Rejected
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id)
);

-- =============================================
-- KITCHEN_STOCK TABLE
-- Raw material inventory
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.kitchen_stock (
    stock_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    ingredient_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    ingredient_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    current_quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    reorder_level NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    reorder_quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    unit_cost core.currency_type,
    
    expiry_date DATE,
    storage_location VARCHAR(100),
    storage_conditions TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    last_restock_date DATE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_ingredient_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, ingredient_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- KITCHEN_STOCK_TRANSACTIONS TABLE
-- Inventory movements
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.kitchen_stock_transactions (
    transaction_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    stock_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.kitchen_stock(stock_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    transaction_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (transaction_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Receipt, 2=Consumption, 3=Adjustment, 4=Wastage
    
    quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    reference_number VARCHAR(100), -- PO, meal plan, etc.
    reason TEXT, -- For adjustments/wastage
    
    transaction_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    -- Generated balance
    balance_after NUMERIC(10,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE transaction_type
            WHEN 1 THEN quantity -- Receipt
            WHEN 2 THEN -quantity -- Consumption
            WHEN 3 THEN quantity -- Adjustment
            WHEN 4 THEN -quantity -- Wastage
            ELSE 0
        END
    ) STORED
);

-- =============================================
-- INDEXES FOR KITCHEN MANAGEMENT
-- =============================================

-- Menu items indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_menu_items_category ON kitchen_management.menu_items(category_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_menu_items_active ON kitchen_management.menu_items(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_menu_items_calories ON kitchen_management.menu_items(calories);

-- Diet orders indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_diet_orders_patient ON kitchen_management.diet_orders(patient_id, is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_diet_orders_nbm ON kitchen_management.diet_orders(is_nil_by_mouth) WHERE is_nil_by_mouth = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_diet_orders_admission ON kitchen_management.diet_orders(admission_id);

-- Meal plans indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_meal_plans_date ON kitchen_management.meal_plans(plan_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_meal_plans_diet_order ON kitchen_management.meal_plans(diet_order_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_meal_plans_served ON kitchen_management.meal_plans(is_served) WHERE is_served = FALSE;

-- Meal deliveries indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_meal_deliveries_date ON kitchen_management.meal_deliveries(delivered_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_meal_deliveries_status ON kitchen_management.meal_deliveries(delivery_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_meal_deliveries_patient ON kitchen_management.meal_deliveries(patient_id);

-- Kitchen stock indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_kitchen_stock_active ON kitchen_management.kitchen_stock(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_kitchen_stock_expiry ON kitchen_management.kitchen_stock(expiry_date) WHERE expiry_date IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_kitchen_stock_reorder ON kitchen_management.kitchen_stock(current_quantity) WHERE current_quantity <= reorder_level;

-- =============================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY POLICIES
-- =============================================

-- Enable RLS on all kitchen tables
ALTER TABLE kitchen_management.menu_items ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE kitchen_management.diet_orders ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE kitchen_management.meal_plans ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE kitchen_management.meal_deliveries ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE kitchen_management.kitchen_stock ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Tenant isolation policies
CREATE POLICY policy_kitchen_tenant_isolation ON kitchen_management.menu_items
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_diet_orders_tenant_isolation ON kitchen_management.diet_orders
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_meal_plans_tenant_isolation ON kitchen_management.meal_plans
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

-- Similar policies for other tables...

-- =============================================
-- AUDIT TRIGGERS
-- =============================================

CREATE TRIGGER trg_menu_items_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON kitchen_management.menu_items 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_diet_orders_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON kitchen_management.diet_orders 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_meal_plans_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON kitchen_management.meal_plans 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

-- Apply to all other tables...

-- =============================================
-- BUSINESS RULE TRIGGERS
-- =============================================

-- Trigger to block meal delivery for NBM patients
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION kitchen_management.fn_block_nbm_meals()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM kitchen_management.diet_orders do
        WHERE do.diet_order_id = NEW.diet_order_id
        AND do.is_nil_by_mouth = TRUE
        AND do.is_active = TRUE
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Cannot deliver meals to nil-by-mouth patients';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_block_nbm_meals
    BEFORE INSERT ON kitchen_management.meal_deliveries
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION kitchen_management.fn_block_nbm_meals();

-- Trigger for allergen check enforcement
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION kitchen_management.fn_enforce_allergen_check()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.allergen_check_passed = FALSE THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Meal cannot be delivered without passed allergen check';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_enforce_allergen_check
    BEFORE UPDATE OF delivery_status ON kitchen_management.meal_deliveries
    FOR EACH ROW 
    WHEN (NEW.delivery_status = 3) -- Delivered
    EXECUTE FUNCTION kitchen_management.fn_enforce_allergen_check();

-- =============================================
-- COMMENTS FOR DOCUMENTATION
-- =============================================

COMMENT ON SCHEMA kitchen_management IS 'Patient nutrition and dietary services management with allergy safety';
COMMENT ON TABLE kitchen_management.diet_orders IS 'Patient-specific diet prescriptions with nil-by-mouth enforcement';
COMMENT ON TABLE kitchen_management.meal_deliveries IS 'Actual meal delivery tracking with temperature monitoring';
COMMENT ON TABLE kitchen_management.menu_item_allergens IS 'Allergen information for menu items for patient safety';

COMMENT ON COLUMN kitchen_management.diet_orders.is_nil_by_mouth IS 'Critical safety flag to block all meal deliveries';
COMMENT ON COLUMN kitchen_management.meal_deliveries.temperature_at_delivery IS 'Temperature monitoring for food safety compliance';
COMMENT ON COLUMN kitchen_management.menu_items.is_halal IS 'Religious dietary compliance flag';
```

``` sql
-- =============================================
-- KITCHEN/NUTRITION MANAGEMENT DOMAIN
-- Patient dietary services with allergy safety
-- =============================================

-- Create kitchen/nutrition schema
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS kitchen_management;

-- Custom domain types for kitchen management
CREATE DOMAIN kitchen_management.temperature_c_type AS NUMERIC(4,1) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN -30 AND 60);
CREATE DOMAIN kitchen_management.percentage_type AS NUMERIC(5,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0 AND VALUE <= 100);

-- =============================================
-- MENU_CATEGORIES TABLE
-- Meal category organization
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.menu_categories (
    category_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    category_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    -- Breakfast, Lunch, Dinner, Snacks, Therapeutic
    serving_time_start TIME NOT NULL,
    serving_time_end TIME NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    display_order SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_category_name UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, category_name),
    CONSTRAINT chk_serving_times CHECK (serving_time_end > serving_time_start)
);

-- =============================================
-- MENU_ITEMS TABLE
-- Dish master with nutritional information
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.menu_items (
    item_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    category_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.menu_categories(category_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    item_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    item_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    portion_size VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    -- Nutritional information
    calories NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    protein_g NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    carbs_g NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    fat_g NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    fiber_g NUMERIC(6,1),
    sodium_mg NUMERIC(6,1),
    sugar_g NUMERIC(6,1),
    
    -- Dietary restrictions
    is_vegetarian BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_vegan BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_gluten_free BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_halal BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_kosher BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    
    -- Preparation details
    preparation_time_min SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    holding_temp_min kitchen_management.temperature_c_type NOT NULL,
    holding_temp_max kitchen_management.temperature_c_type NOT NULL,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_item_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, item_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_holding_temp CHECK (holding_temp_max > holding_temp_min)
);

-- =============================================
-- MENU_ITEM_ALLERGENS TABLE
-- Allergen information for menu items
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.menu_item_allergens (
    item_allergen_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    menu_item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.menu_items(item_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    allergen_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    -- Common allergens: MILK, EGGS, FISH, SHELLFISH, TREENUTS, PEANUTS, WHEAT, SOY
    severity SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (severity IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Contains, 2=May Contain, 3=Processed in same facility
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_item_allergen UNIQUE (menu_item_id, allergen_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- DIET_TYPES TABLE
-- Therapeutic diet definitions
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.diet_types (
    diet_type_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    diet_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    diet_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    -- General, Diabetic, Renal, Cardiac, Low-Sodium, etc.
    description TEXT,
    
    -- Nutritional ranges
    calorie_range_min NUMERIC(6,1),
    calorie_range_max NUMERIC(6,1),
    protein_range_min NUMERIC(6,1),
    protein_range_max NUMERIC(6,1),
    sodium_range_max NUMERIC(6,1),
    fluid_restriction_max_ml NUMERIC(6,1),
    
    -- Allergen restrictions
    allowed_allergens TEXT[],
    restricted_allergens TEXT[],
    
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_diet_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, diet_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_calorie_range CHECK (calorie_range_max >= calorie_range_min),
    CONSTRAINT chk_protein_range CHECK (protein_range_max >= protein_range_min)
);

-- =============================================
-- DIET_ORDERS TABLE
-- Patient-specific diet prescriptions
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.diet_orders (
    diet_order_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Patient and admission links
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL, -- FK to IPD admissions

    diet_type_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.diet_types(diet_type_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    -- Order details
    start_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,

    allergen_check_passed BOOLEAN,
    allergen_checked_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    allergen_checked_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Doctor approval
    doctor_approved_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    doctor_approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Modifications
    special_instructions TEXT,
    texture_modification SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (texture_modification IN (1,2,3)),
    -- 1=Normal, 2=Minced, 3=Pureed
    
    fluid_restriction_ml NUMERIC(6,1),
    
    -- Order status
    order_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (order_status IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Active, 2=On Hold, 3=Completed, 4=Cancelled
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_dates CHECK (end_date IS NULL OR end_date >= start_date)
);

-- =============================================
-- MEAL_PLANS TABLE
-- Daily meal assignments
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.meal_plans (
    meal_plan_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    diet_order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.diet_orders(diet_order_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    plan_date DATE NOT NULL,
    meal_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (meal_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Breakfast, 2=Lunch, 3=Dinner, 4=Snack
    
    menu_item_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.menu_items(item_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    planned_quantity SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    actual_quantity SMALLINT,
    
    -- Generated nutritional values
    planned_calories NUMERIC(6,1) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        planned_quantity * (SELECT calories FROM kitchen_management.menu_items WHERE item_id = menu_item_id)
    ) STORED,
    planned_protein NUMERIC(6,1) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        planned_quantity * (SELECT protein_g FROM kitchen_management.menu_items WHERE item_id = menu_item_id)
    ) STORED,
    planned_carbs NUMERIC(6,1) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        planned_quantity * (SELECT carbs_g FROM kitchen_management.menu_items WHERE item_id = menu_item_id)
    ) STORED,
    planned_fat NUMERIC(6,1) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        planned_quantity * (SELECT fat_g FROM kitchen_management.menu_items WHERE item_id = menu_item_id)
    ) STORED,
    
    is_served BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    served_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_daily_meal UNIQUE (diet_order_id, plan_date, meal_type)

-- =============================================
-- MEAL_DELIVERIES TABLE
-- Actual meal delivery tracking
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.meal_deliveries (
    delivery_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    diet_order_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.diet_orders(diet_order_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    meal_plan_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.meal_plans(meal_plan_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES patient_management.patients(patient_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    delivered_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    delivered_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    
    received_by UUID REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    
    temperature_at_delivery kitchen_management.temperature_c_type,
    is_accepted BOOLEAN,
    rejection_reason TEXT,
    
    delivery_status SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (delivery_status IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Prepared, 2=In Transit, 3=Delivered, 4=Rejected
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id)
);

-- =============================================
-- KITCHEN_STOCK TABLE
-- Raw material inventory
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.kitchen_stock (
    stock_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    ingredient_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    ingredient_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    current_quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    reorder_level NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    reorder_quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    unit_cost core.currency_type,
    
    expiry_date DATE,
    storage_location VARCHAR(100),
    storage_conditions TEXT,
    
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    last_restock_date DATE,
    
    -- Audit fields
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    updated_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uq_ingredient_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, ingredient_code)
);

-- =============================================
-- KITCHEN_STOCK_TRANSACTIONS TABLE
-- Inventory movements
-- =============================================
CREATE TABLE kitchen_management.kitchen_stock_transactions (
    transaction_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    stock_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES kitchen_management.kitchen_stock(stock_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.tenants(tenant_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.branches(branch_id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    
    transaction_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (transaction_type IN (1,2,3,4)),
    -- 1=Receipt, 2=Consumption, 3=Adjustment, 4=Wastage
    
    quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    reference_number VARCHAR(100), -- PO, meal plan, etc.
    reason TEXT, -- For adjustments/wastage
    
    transaction_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES core.users(user_id),
    
    -- Generated balance
    balance_after NUMERIC(10,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE transaction_type
            WHEN 1 THEN quantity -- Receipt
            WHEN 2 THEN -quantity -- Consumption
            WHEN 3 THEN quantity -- Adjustment
            WHEN 4 THEN -quantity -- Wastage
            ELSE 0
        END
    ) STORED
);

-- =============================================
-- INDEXES FOR KITCHEN MANAGEMENT
-- =============================================

-- Menu items indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_menu_items_category ON kitchen_management.menu_items(category_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_menu_items_active ON kitchen_management.menu_items(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_menu_items_calories ON kitchen_management.menu_items(calories);

-- Diet orders indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_diet_orders_patient ON kitchen_management.diet_orders(patient_id, is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_diet_orders_nbm ON kitchen_management.diet_orders(is_nil_by_mouth) WHERE is_nil_by_mouth = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_diet_orders_admission ON kitchen_management.diet_orders(admission_id);

-- Meal plans indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_meal_plans_date ON kitchen_management.meal_plans(plan_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_meal_plans_diet_order ON kitchen_management.meal_plans(diet_order_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_meal_plans_served ON kitchen_management.meal_plans(is_served) WHERE is_served = FALSE;

-- Meal deliveries indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_meal_deliveries_date ON kitchen_management.meal_deliveries(delivered_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_meal_deliveries_status ON kitchen_management.meal_deliveries(delivery_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_meal_deliveries_patient ON kitchen_management.meal_deliveries(patient_id);

-- Kitchen stock indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_kitchen_stock_active ON kitchen_management.kitchen_stock(is_active) WHERE is_active = TRUE;
CREATE INDEX idx_kitchen_stock_expiry ON kitchen_management.kitchen_stock(expiry_date) WHERE expiry_date IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_kitchen_stock_reorder ON kitchen_management.kitchen_stock(current_quantity) WHERE current_quantity <= reorder_level;

-- =============================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY POLICIES
-- =============================================

-- Enable RLS on all kitchen tables
ALTER TABLE kitchen_management.menu_items ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE kitchen_management.diet_orders ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE kitchen_management.meal_plans ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE kitchen_management.meal_deliveries ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE kitchen_management.kitchen_stock ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Tenant isolation policies
CREATE POLICY policy_kitchen_tenant_isolation ON kitchen_management.menu_items
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_diet_orders_tenant_isolation ON kitchen_management.diet_orders
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY policy_meal_plans_tenant_isolation ON kitchen_management.meal_plans
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.tenant_id')::UUID);

-- Similar policies for other tables...

-- =============================================
-- AUDIT TRIGGERS
-- =============================================

CREATE TRIGGER trg_menu_items_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON kitchen_management.menu_items 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_diet_orders_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON kitchen_management.diet_orders 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_meal_plans_timestamp 
    BEFORE UPDATE ON kitchen_management.meal_plans 
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.fn_update_timestamp();

-- Apply to all other tables...

-- =============================================
-- BUSINESS RULE TRIGGERS
-- =============================================

-- Trigger to block meal delivery for NBM patients
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION kitchen_management.fn_block_nbm_meals()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM kitchen_management.diet_orders do
        WHERE do.diet_order_id = NEW.diet_order_id
        AND do.is_nil_by_mouth = TRUE
        AND do.is_active = TRUE
    ) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Cannot deliver meals to nil-by-mouth patients';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_block_nbm_meals
    BEFORE INSERT ON kitchen_management.meal_deliveries
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION kitchen_management.fn_block_nbm_meals();

-- Trigger for allergen check enforcement
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION kitchen_management.fn_enforce_allergen_check()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.allergen_check_passed = FALSE THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Meal cannot be delivered without passed allergen check';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_enforce_allergen_check
    BEFORE UPDATE OF delivery_status ON kitchen_management.meal_deliveries
    FOR EACH ROW 
    WHEN (NEW.delivery_status = 3) -- Delivered
    EXECUTE FUNCTION kitchen_management.fn_enforce_allergen_check();

-- =============================================
-- COMMENTS FOR DOCUMENTATION
-- =============================================

COMMENT ON SCHEMA kitchen_management IS 'Patient nutrition and dietary services management with allergy safety';
COMMENT ON TABLE kitchen_management.diet_orders IS 'Patient-specific diet prescriptions with nil-by-mouth enforcement';
COMMENT ON TABLE kitchen_management.meal_deliveries IS 'Actual meal delivery tracking with temperature monitoring';
COMMENT ON TABLE kitchen_management.menu_item_allergens IS 'Allergen information for menu items for patient safety';

COMMENT ON COLUMN kitchen_management.diet_orders.is_nil_by_mouth IS 'Critical safety flag to block all meal deliveries';
COMMENT ON COLUMN kitchen_management.meal_deliveries.temperature_at_delivery IS 'Temperature monitoring for food safety compliance';
COMMENT ON COLUMN kitchen_management.menu_items.is_halal IS 'Religious dietary compliance flag';
```

- Allergy Safety with strict enforcement triggers
- Nutritional Tracking with generated calculations
- NBM Enforcement (Nil By Mouth) safety triggers
- Temperature Monitoring for food safety
- Religious Compliance (Halal, Kosher) tracking
- Inventory Management with expiry tracking

Key Safety Features:
