 Domain :  8 Blood Bank

Here's the PostgreSQL implementation for the Blood Bank domain:



```sql
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN blood_group_type VARCHAR(3) 
    CHECK (VALUE IN ('A+', 'A-', 'B+', 'B-', 'AB+', 'AB-', 'O+', 'O-'));

CREATE DOMAIN temperature_c_type NUMERIC(4,1) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN -50 AND 50);

CREATE DOMAIN phone_type VARCHAR(20);
CREATE DOMAIN email_type VARCHAR(255);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE donation_status_type AS ENUM (
    'completed', 'deferred', 'stopped'
);

CREATE TYPE unit_status_type AS ENUM (
    'quarantine', 'available', 'reserved', 'issued', 'transfused', 'discarded', 'expired'
);

CREATE TYPE tti_status_type AS ENUM (
    'pending', 'negative', 'positive'
);

CREATE TYPE test_result_type AS ENUM (
    'negative', 'positive', 'indeterminate'
);

CREATE TYPE priority_type AS ENUM (
    'routine', 'urgent', 'emergency'
);

CREATE TYPE reaction_severity_type AS ENUM (
    'mild', 'moderate', 'severe'
);

CREATE TYPE outcome_type AS ENUM (
    'resolved', 'ongoing', 'serious'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE blood_donor_master (
    donor_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    donor_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    first_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    last_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    date_of_birth DATE NOT NULL,
    gender SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (gender BETWEEN 1 AND 3),
    blood_group blood_group_type,
    contact_phone phone_type,
    contact_email email_type,
    address TEXT,
    nationality UUID,
    donor_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (donor_type BETWEEN 1 AND 3),
    total_donations SMALLINT DEFAULT 0,
    last_donation_date DATE,
    next_eligible_date DATE,
    is_active_donor BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    permanent_deferral BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    deferral_reason TEXT,
    emergency_contact VARCHAR(255),
    emergency_phone phone_type,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (nationality) REFERENCES countries(country_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_donor_age CHECK (
        date_of_birth <= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '18 years'
    )
);

CREATE TABLE blood_donations (
    donation_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    donor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    donation_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    donation_date DATE NOT NULL,
    donation_time TIME NOT NULL,
    donation_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (donation_type BETWEEN 1 AND 2),
    collection_volume_ml NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    collection_site VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    phlebotomist_id UUID NOT NULL,
    donor_weight_kg NUMERIC(5,1),
    donor_hemoglobin NUMERIC(4,1),
    donor_bp_systolic SMALLINT,
    donor_bp_diastolic SMALLINT,
    donor_temperature temperature_c_type,
    eligibility_questions JSONB,
    donation_status donation_status_type NOT NULL,
    deferral_reason TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (donor_id) REFERENCES blood_donor_master(donor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (phlebotomist_id) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_collection_volume CHECK (
        collection_volume_ml BETWEEN 350 AND 550
    )
);

CREATE TABLE blood_units (
    unit_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    donation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    unit_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    component_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (component_type BETWEEN 1 AND 6),
    blood_group blood_group_type NOT NULL,
    volume_ml NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    collection_date DATE NOT NULL,
    expiry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    storage_temp_min temperature_c_type NOT NULL,
    storage_temp_max temperature_c_type NOT NULL,
    tti_status tti_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    unit_status unit_status_type DEFAULT 'quarantine',
    is_voluntary BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    issued_to_patient_id UUID,
    issued_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    issued_by UUID,
    transfusion_id UUID,
    current_location VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    special_testing TEXT[],
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (donation_id) REFERENCES blood_donations(donation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (issued_to_patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (issued_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_storage_temp CHECK (
        storage_temp_min < storage_temp_max
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_expiry_date CHECK (
        expiry_date > collection_date
    )
);

CREATE TABLE blood_unit_components (
    component_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    donation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    component_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (component_type BETWEEN 1 AND 6),
    volume_ml NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    separation_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    separated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    expiry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    unit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (donation_id) REFERENCES blood_donations(donation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (separated_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES blood_units(unit_id)
);

CREATE TABLE blood_tti_tests (
    tti_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    donation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    test_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (test_type BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    test_method VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    test_kit_lot VARCHAR(100),
    test_date DATE NOT NULL,
    test_result test_result_type NOT NULL,
    result_value VARCHAR(100),
    tested_by UUID NOT NULL,
    verified_by UUID,
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (donation_id) REFERENCES blood_donations(donation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tested_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE blood_requests (
    request_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID,
    requested_by UUID NOT NULL,
    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    priority priority_type NOT NULL,
    component_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (component_type BETWEEN 1 AND 6),
    quantity_units SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (quantity_units > 0),
    blood_group blood_group_type NOT NULL,
    clinical_indication TEXT,
    hemoglobin_level NUMERIC(4,1),
    platelet_count NUMERIC(6,1),
    request_status SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (request_status BETWEEN 1 AND 4),
    cancelled_reason TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (requested_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE blood_crossmatches (
    crossmatch_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    unit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    request_id UUID NOT NULL,
    abo_rh_compatible BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    antibody_screen_result SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (antibody_screen_result BETWEEN 1 AND 2),
    crossmatch_method VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    crossmatch_result SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (crossmatch_result BETWEEN 1 AND 2),
    incompatibility_reason TEXT,
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL,
    performed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    is_emergency BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES blood_units(unit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (request_id) REFERENCES blood_requests(request_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (performed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_valid_until CHECK (
        valid_until > performed_at
    )
);

CREATE TABLE blood_issuances (
    issuance_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    unit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    request_id UUID NOT NULL,
    issued_by UUID NOT NULL,
    issued_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    received_by UUID NOT NULL,
    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    transport_temp temperature_c_type,
    issue_notes TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES blood_units(unit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (request_id) REFERENCES blood_requests(request_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (issued_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (received_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE blood_transfusions (
    transfusion_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    unit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID,
    started_by UUID NOT NULL,
    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    volume_transfused_ml NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    transfusion_duration_min SMALLINT,
    vital_signs_pre JSONB,
    vital_signs_post JSONB,
    transfusion_status SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (transfusion_status BETWEEN 1 AND 4),
    stopped_reason TEXT,
    witnessed_by UUID,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES blood_units(unit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (started_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (witnessed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_transfusion_volume CHECK (
        volume_transfused_ml > 0
    )
);

CREATE TABLE transfusion_reactions (
    reaction_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    transfusion_id UUID NOT NULL,
    unit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    reaction_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (reaction_type BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    reaction_severity reaction_severity_type NOT NULL,
    onset_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    symptoms TEXT[] NOT NULL,
    treatment_given TEXT,
    outcome outcome_type,
    reported_by UUID NOT NULL,
    reported_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    investigated_by UUID,
    investigation_notes TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (transfusion_id) REFERENCES blood_transfusions(transfusion_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES blood_units(unit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (reported_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (investigated_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE blood_temperature_logs (
    temp_log_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    unit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    refrigerator_id UUID NOT NULL,
    temperature_c temperature_c_type NOT NULL,
    logged_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    is_in_range BOOLEAN GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        temperature_c BETWEEN 
        (SELECT storage_temp_min FROM blood_units WHERE unit_id = blood_temperature_logs.unit_id) 
        AND 
        (SELECT storage_temp_max FROM blood_units WHERE unit_id = blood_temperature_logs.unit_id)
    ) STORED,
    out_of_range_duration_min SMALLINT DEFAULT 0,
    alert_generated BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    alert_acknowledged_by UUID,
    alert_acknowledged_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES blood_units(unit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (refrigerator_id) REFERENCES equipment(equipment_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (alert_acknowledged_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE blood_discards (
    discard_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    unit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    discard_reason SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (discard_reason BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    discard_date DATE NOT NULL,
    discarded_by UUID NOT NULL,
    witness_id UUID NOT NULL,
    documentation TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES blood_units(unit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (discarded_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (witness_id) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_blood_units_status ON blood_units(blood_group, component_type, unit_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_blood_units_expiry ON blood_units(expiry_date) WHERE unit_status IN ('available', 'quarantine');
CREATE INDEX idx_blood_units_available ON blood_units(unit_status) WHERE unit_status = 'available';

CREATE INDEX idx_blood_donations_donor ON blood_donations(donor_id, donation_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_blood_donations_date ON blood_donations(donation_date);

CREATE INDEX idx_blood_tti_tests_donation ON blood_tti_tests(donation_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_blood_tti_tests_result ON blood_tti_tests(test_result);

CREATE INDEX idx_blood_crossmatches_unit ON blood_crossmatches(unit_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_blood_crossmatches_patient ON blood_crossmatches(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_blood_crossmatches_validity ON blood_crossmatches(valid_until);

CREATE INDEX idx_blood_temperature_logs_unit ON blood_temperature_logs(unit_id, logged_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_blood_temperature_logs_time ON blood_temperature_logs(logged_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_blood_temperature_logs_range ON blood_temperature_logs(is_in_range);

CREATE INDEX idx_blood_transfusions_patient ON blood_transfusions(patient_id, started_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_blood_transfusions_unit ON blood_transfusions(unit_id);

-- Partitioning for large tables
CREATE TABLE blood_temperature_logs_y2024 PARTITION OF blood_temperature_logs
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

CREATE TABLE blood_units_y2024 PARTITION OF blood_units
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_donor_stats()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.donation_status = 'completed' THEN
        UPDATE blood_donor_master
        SET 
            total_donations = total_donations + 1,
            last_donation_date = NEW.donation_date,
            next_eligible_date = NEW.donation_date + INTERVAL '3 months'
        WHERE donor_id = NEW.donor_id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_donor_stats
    AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON blood_donations
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_donor_stats();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_unit_expiry()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE AND NEW.unit_status NOT IN ('discarded', 'expired') THEN
        NEW.unit_status = 'expired';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_unit_expiry
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON blood_units
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION check_unit_expiry();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION prevent_issued_unit_modification()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF OLD.unit_status IN ('issued', 'transfused') AND NEW.unit_status != OLD.unit_status THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Cannot modify issued or transfused units';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_prevent_issued_unit_modification
    BEFORE UPDATE ON blood_units
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION prevent_issued_unit_modification();

-- RLS Policies (example)
CREATE POLICY blood_bank_tenant_isolation ON blood_units
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY donor_privacy ON blood_donor_master
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);
```

``` sql
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN blood_group_type VARCHAR(3) 
    CHECK (VALUE IN ('A+', 'A-', 'B+', 'B-', 'AB+', 'AB-', 'O+', 'O-'));

CREATE DOMAIN temperature_c_type NUMERIC(4,1) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN -50 AND 50);

CREATE DOMAIN phone_type VARCHAR(20);
CREATE DOMAIN email_type VARCHAR(255);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE donation_status_type AS ENUM (
    'completed', 'deferred', 'stopped'
);

CREATE TYPE unit_status_type AS ENUM (
    'quarantine', 'available', 'reserved', 'issued', 'transfused', 'discarded', 'expired'
);

CREATE TYPE tti_status_type AS ENUM (
    'pending', 'negative', 'positive'
);

CREATE TYPE test_result_type AS ENUM (
    'negative', 'positive', 'indeterminate'
);

CREATE TYPE priority_type AS ENUM (
    'routine', 'urgent', 'emergency'
);

CREATE TYPE reaction_severity_type AS ENUM (
    'mild', 'moderate', 'severe'
);

CREATE TYPE outcome_type AS ENUM (
    'resolved', 'ongoing', 'serious'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE blood_donor_master (
    donor_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    donor_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    first_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    last_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    date_of_birth DATE NOT NULL,
    gender SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (gender BETWEEN 1 AND 3),
    blood_group blood_group_type,
    contact_phone phone_type,
    contact_email email_type,
    address TEXT,
    nationality UUID,
    donor_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (donor_type BETWEEN 1 AND 3),
    total_donations SMALLINT DEFAULT 0,
    last_donation_date DATE,
    next_eligible_date DATE,
    is_active_donor BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    permanent_deferral BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    deferral_reason TEXT,
    emergency_contact VARCHAR(255),
    emergency_phone phone_type,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (nationality) REFERENCES countries(country_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_donor_age CHECK (
        date_of_birth <= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '18 years'
    )
);

CREATE TABLE blood_donations (
    donation_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    donor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    donation_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    donation_date DATE NOT NULL,
    donation_time TIME NOT NULL,
    donation_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (donation_type BETWEEN 1 AND 2),
    collection_volume_ml NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    collection_site VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    phlebotomist_id UUID NOT NULL,
    donor_weight_kg NUMERIC(5,1),
    donor_hemoglobin NUMERIC(4,1),
    donor_bp_systolic SMALLINT,
    donor_bp_diastolic SMALLINT,
    donor_temperature temperature_c_type,
    eligibility_questions JSONB,
    donation_status donation_status_type NOT NULL,
    deferral_reason TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (donor_id) REFERENCES blood_donor_master(donor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (phlebotomist_id) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_collection_volume CHECK (
        collection_volume_ml BETWEEN 350 AND 550
    )
);

CREATE TABLE blood_units (
    unit_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    donation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    unit_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    component_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (component_type BETWEEN 1 AND 6),
    blood_group blood_group_type NOT NULL,
    volume_ml NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    collection_date DATE NOT NULL,
    expiry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    storage_temp_min temperature_c_type NOT NULL,
    storage_temp_max temperature_c_type NOT NULL,
    tti_status tti_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    unit_status unit_status_type DEFAULT 'quarantine',
    is_voluntary BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    issued_to_patient_id UUID,
    issued_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    issued_by UUID,
    transfusion_id UUID,
    current_location VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    special_testing TEXT[],
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (donation_id) REFERENCES blood_donations(donation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (issued_to_patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (issued_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_storage_temp CHECK (
        storage_temp_min < storage_temp_max
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_expiry_date CHECK (
        expiry_date > collection_date
    )
);

CREATE TABLE blood_unit_components (
    component_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    donation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    component_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (component_type BETWEEN 1 AND 6),
    volume_ml NUMERIC(6,1) NOT NULL,
    separation_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    separated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    expiry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    unit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (donation_id) REFERENCES blood_donations(donation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (separated_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES blood_units(unit_id)
);

CREATE TABLE blood_tti_tests (
    tti_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    donation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    test_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (test_type BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    test_method VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    test_kit_lot VARCHAR(100),
    test_date DATE NOT NULL,
    test_result test_result_type NOT NULL,
    result_value VARCHAR(100),
    tested_by UUID NOT NULL,
    verified_by UUID,
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (donation_id) REFERENCES blood_donations(donation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tested_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE blood_requests (
    request_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID,
    requested_by UUID NOT NULL,
    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    priority priority_type NOT NULL,
    component_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (component_type BETWEEN 1 AND 6),
    quantity_units SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (quantity_units > 0),
    blood_group blood_group_type NOT NULL,
    clinical_indication TEXT,
    hemoglobin_level NUMERIC(4,1),
    platelet_count NUMERIC(6,1),
    request_status SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (request_status BETWEEN 1 AND 4),
    cancelled_reason TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (requested_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE blood_crossmatches (
    crossmatch_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    unit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    request_id UUID NOT NULL,
    abo_rh_compatible BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    antibody_screen_result SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (antibody_screen_result BETWEEN 1 AND 2),
    crossmatch_method VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    crossmatch_result SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (crossmatch_result BETWEEN 1 AND 2),
    incompatibility_reason TEXT,
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL,
    performed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    is_emergency BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES blood_units(unit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (request_id) REFERENCES blood_requests(request_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (performed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_valid_until CHECK (
        valid_until > performed_at
    )
);

CREATE TABLE blood_issuances (
    issuance_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    unit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    request_id UUID NOT NULL,
    issued_by UUID NOT NULL,
    issued_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    received_by UUID NOT NULL,
    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    transport_temp temperature_c_type,
    issue_notes TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES blood_units(unit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (request_id) REFERENCES blood_requests(request_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (issued_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (received_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE blood_transfusions (
    transfusion_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),

    FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES blood_units(unit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (started_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (witnessed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_transfusion_volume CHECK (
        volume_transfused_ml > 0
    )
);

CREATE TABLE transfusion_reactions (

    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),

    FOREIGN KEY (unit_id) REFERENCES blood_units(unit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (reported_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (investigated_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

        AND

    ) STORED,
    out_of_range_duration_min SMALLINT DEFAULT 0,

    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),

    FOREIGN KEY (refrigerator_id) REFERENCES equipment(equipment_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (alert_acknowledged_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_donor_stats()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.donation_status = 'completed' THEN
        UPDATE blood_donor_master
        SET 
            total_donations = total_donations + 1,
            last_donation_date = NEW.donation_date,
            next_eligible_date = NEW.donation_date + INTERVAL '3 months'
        WHERE donor_id = NEW.donor_id;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_donor_stats
    AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON blood_donations
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_donor_stats();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_unit_expiry()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE AND NEW.unit_status NOT IN ('discarded', 'expired') THEN
        NEW.unit_status = 'expired';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_unit_expiry
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON blood_units
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION check_unit_expiry();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION prevent_issued_unit_modification()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF OLD.unit_status IN ('issued', 'transfused') AND NEW.unit_status != OLD.unit_status THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Cannot modify issued or transfused units';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_prevent_issued_unit_modification
    BEFORE UPDATE ON blood_units
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION prevent_issued_unit_modification();

-- RLS Policies (example)
CREATE POLICY blood_bank_tenant_isolation ON blood_units
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY donor_privacy ON blood_donor_master
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);
```

This implementation includes:

1. Custom domains for data validation (blood groups, temperatures)
2. Enum types for standardized status values
3. Comprehensive foreign key relationships ensuring data integrity
4. Check constraints for business rule enforcement
5. Generated columns for automatic validation (temperature range)
6. Indexes optimized for common queries
7. Table partitioning for large tables (temperature logs, blood units)
8. Triggers for automatic updates (donor stats, expiry checks)
9. RLS policies for data security and tenant isolation

## Domain 9: Ambulance Management
Here's the PostgreSQL implementation for the Ambulance Management domain:


```sql
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN latitude_type NUMERIC(10,8) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN -90 AND 90);

CREATE DOMAIN longitude_type NUMERIC(11,8) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN -180 AND 180);

CREATE DOMAIN currency_type NUMERIC(12,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0);

CREATE DOMAIN phone_type VARCHAR(20);
CREATE DOMAIN email_type VARCHAR(255);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE vehicle_type_type AS ENUM (
    'bls', 'als', 'mobile_icu', 'air', 'neonatal'
);

CREATE TYPE vehicle_status_type AS ENUM (
    'available', 'dispatched', 'maintenance', 'retired'
);

CREATE TYPE fuel_type_type AS ENUM (
    'petrol', 'diesel', 'hybrid', 'electric'
);

CREATE TYPE equipment_condition_type AS ENUM (
    'excellent', 'good', 'needs_service', 'out_of_service'
);

CREATE TYPE staff_status_type AS ENUM (
    'available', 'on_trip', 'off_duty', 'training'
);

CREATE TYPE trip_type_type AS ENUM (
    'emergency', 'inter_facility', 'scheduled', 'discharge'
);

CREATE TYPE trip_status_type AS ENUM (
    'dispatched', 'en_route_pickup', 'at_pickup', 'transporting', 'completed', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE signal_quality_type AS ENUM (
    'excellent', 'good', 'poor', 'lost'
);

CREATE TYPE maintenance_type_type AS ENUM (
    'preventive', 'corrective', 'emergency'
);

CREATE TYPE maintenance_status_type AS ENUM (
    'scheduled', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'overdue'
);

CREATE TYPE billing_status_type AS ENUM (
    'draft', 'sent', 'paid', 'insurance_claim'
);

CREATE TYPE claim_status_type AS ENUM (
    'submitted', 'approved', 'rejected', 'partially_paid'
);

CREATE TYPE emergency_type_type AS ENUM (
    'medical', 'trauma', 'psychiatric', 'ob_gyn'
);

CREATE TYPE severity_type AS ENUM (
    'critical', 'serious', 'moderate', 'minor'
);

CREATE TYPE priority_type AS ENUM (
    'immediate', 'high', 'medium', 'low'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE ambulance_fleet (
    ambulance_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    vehicle_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    vehicle_type vehicle_type_type NOT NULL,
    make VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    model VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    year SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (year BETWEEN 1900 AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM CURRENT_DATE) + 1),
    vin VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    insurance_number VARCHAR(100),
    insurance_expiry DATE,
    capacity_patients SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (capacity_patients > 0),
    capacity_staff SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (capacity_staff > 0),
    odometer_reading NUMERIC(10,1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    fuel_type fuel_type_type NOT NULL,
    current_status vehicle_status_type DEFAULT 'available',
    base_location VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    gps_device_id VARCHAR(100),
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_insurance_expiry CHECK (
        insurance_expiry IS NULL OR insurance_expiry >= CURRENT_DATE
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_equipment (
    equipment_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    ambulance_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    equipment_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (equipment_type BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    equipment_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    serial_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    calibration_date DATE,
    next_calibration_date DATE,
    expiry_date DATE,
    current_condition equipment_condition_type DEFAULT 'excellent',
    last_service_date DATE,
    is_available BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (ambulance_id) REFERENCES ambulance_fleet(ambulance_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_calibration_dates CHECK (
        calibration_date IS NULL OR next_calibration_date IS NULL OR 
        next_calibration_date > calibration_date
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_staff (
    staff_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    user_id UUID NOT NULL,
    staff_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (staff_type BETWEEN 1 AND 4),
    certification_number VARCHAR(100),
    certification_type VARCHAR(100),
    certification_issue_date DATE,
    certification_expiry_date DATE,
    is_certification_valid BOOLEAN GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        certification_expiry_date IS NOT NULL AND 
        certification_expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE
    ) STORED,
    training_completed TEXT[],
    shift_schedule JSONB,
    current_status staff_status_type DEFAULT 'available',
    total_trips_completed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_certification_dates CHECK (
        certification_issue_date IS NULL OR certification_expiry_date IS NULL OR 
        certification_expiry_date > certification_issue_date
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_trips (
    trip_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    trip_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    ambulance_id UUID NOT NULL,
    driver_id UUID NOT NULL,
    paramedic_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID,
    call_received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    trip_type trip_type_type NOT NULL,
    pickup_address TEXT NOT NULL,
    pickup_lat latitude_type,
    pickup_lon longitude_type,
    pickup_geom GEOGRAPHY(POINT,4326),
    destination_address TEXT NOT NULL,
    destination_lat latitude_type,
    destination_lon longitude_type,
    destination_geom GEOGRAPHY(POINT,4326),
    dispatched_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    arrived_at_pickup TIMESTAMPTZ,
    departed_pickup_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    arrived_at_destination_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    trip_status trip_status_type DEFAULT 'dispatched',
    odometer_start NUMERIC(10,1),
    odometer_end NUMERIC(10,1),
    distance_km NUMERIC(8,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN odometer_start IS NOT NULL AND odometer_end IS NOT NULL 
            THEN odometer_end - odometer_start 
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    receiving_facility_id UUID,
    receiving_doctor_id UUID,
    handover_notes TEXT,
    estimated_arrival_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    eta_communicated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    eta_communicated_to VARCHAR(255),
    trip_billing_id UUID,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (ambulance_id) REFERENCES ambulance_fleet(ambulance_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (driver_id) REFERENCES ambulance_staff(staff_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (paramedic_id) REFERENCES ambulance_staff(staff_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (receiving_facility_id) REFERENCES facilities(facility_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (receiving_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_trip_timeline CHECK (
        call_received_at <= dispatched_at AND
        dispatched_at <= arrived_at_pickup AND
        arrived_at_pickup <= departed_pickup_at AND
        departed_pickup_at <= arrived_at_destination_at
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_odometer_values CHECK (
        odometer_start IS NULL OR odometer_end IS NULL OR 
        odometer_end >= odometer_start
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_gps_logs (
    gps_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    trip_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    ambulance_id UUID NOT NULL,
    latitude latitude_type NOT NULL,
    longitude longitude_type NOT NULL,
    location_geom GEOGRAPHY(POINT,4326) NOT NULL,
    speed_kmph NUMERIC(5,1) DEFAULT 0,
    heading_degrees SMALLINT CHECK (heading_degrees BETWEEN 0 AND 359),
    logged_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    signal_quality signal_quality_type DEFAULT 'good',
    device_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES ambulance_trips(trip_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (ambulance_id) REFERENCES ambulance_fleet(ambulance_id)
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_maintenance (
    maintenance_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    ambulance_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    maintenance_type maintenance_type_type NOT NULL,
    scheduled_date DATE NOT NULL,
    performed_date DATE,
    odometer_at_service NUMERIC(10,1) NOT NULL,
    service_provider VARCHAR(255),
    service_cost currency_type DEFAULT 0,
    work_performed TEXT[],
    parts_replaced TEXT[],
    next_service_date DATE,
    next_service_odometer NUMERIC(10,1),
    performed_by UUID,
    verified_by UUID,
    maintenance_status maintenance_status_type DEFAULT 'scheduled',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (ambulance_id) REFERENCES ambulance_fleet(ambulance_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (performed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_maintenance_dates CHECK (
        performed_date IS NULL OR performed_date >= scheduled_date
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_next_service CHECK (
        next_service_odometer IS NULL OR next_service_odometer > odometer_at_service
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_billing (
    billing_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    trip_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    billing_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    base_fare currency_type NOT NULL,
    distance_charge currency_type NOT NULL,
    time_charge currency_type NOT NULL,
    equipment_usage_charge currency_type DEFAULT 0,
    medication_charge currency_type DEFAULT 0,
    total_amount currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        base_fare + distance_charge + time_charge + 
        equipment_usage_charge + medication_charge
    ) STORED,
    insurance_covered currency_type DEFAULT 0,
    patient_payable currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        total_amount - insurance_covered
    ) STORED,
    billing_status billing_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    insurance_claim_number VARCHAR(100),
    claim_status claim_status_type,
    paid_date DATE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES ambulance_trips(trip_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_insurance_coverage CHECK (
        insurance_covered <= total_amount
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_dates CHECK (
        paid_date IS NULL OR paid_date >= billing_date
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_dispatch_log (
    dispatch_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    call_received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    caller_number phone_type NOT NULL,
    caller_name VARCHAR(100),
    caller_location VARCHAR(255),
    emergency_type emergency_type_type NOT NULL,
    emergency_severity severity_type NOT NULL,
    dispatch_priority priority_type NOT NULL,
    dispatched_ambulance_id UUID,
    dispatch_time_sec INTEGER,
    response_time_sec INTEGER,
    call_recording_ref VARCHAR(100),
    dispatcher_id UUID NOT NULL,
    dispatch_notes TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dispatched_ambulance_id) REFERENCES ambulance_fleet(ambulance_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dispatcher_id) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_fleet_status ON ambulance_fleet(current_status, vehicle_type) WHERE is_active = true;
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_fleet_location ON ambulance_fleet(base_location);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_fleet_gps ON ambulance_fleet(gps_device_id) WHERE gps_device_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_equipment_ambulance ON ambulance_equipment(ambulance_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_equipment_condition ON ambulance_equipment(current_condition) WHERE is_available = true;
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_equipment_calibration ON ambulance_equipment(next_calibration_date) WHERE next_calibration_date IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_staff_certification ON ambulance_staff(certification_expiry_date) WHERE is_certification_valid = false;
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_staff_status ON ambulance_staff(current_status, staff_type);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_staff_user ON ambulance_staff(user_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_trips_status ON ambulance_trips(trip_status, dispatched_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_trips_ambulance ON ambulance_trips(ambulance_id, trip_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_trips_patient ON ambulance_trips(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_trips_dates ON ambulance_trips(call_received_at, dispatched_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_trips_geo_pickup ON ambulance_trips USING GIST (pickup_geom);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_trips_geo_dest ON ambulance_trips USING GIST (destination_geom);

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_gps_trip ON ambulance_gps_logs(trip_id, logged_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_gps_ambulance ON ambulance_gps_logs(ambulance_id, logged_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_gps_geo ON ambulance_gps_logs USING GIST (location_geom);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_gps_time ON ambulance_gps_logs(logged_at);

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_maintenance_ambulance ON ambulance_maintenance(ambulance_id, maintenance_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_maintenance_dates ON ambulance_maintenance(scheduled_date, performed_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_maintenance_status ON ambulance_maintenance(maintenance_status) WHERE maintenance_status = 'overdue';

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_billing_trip ON ambulance_billing(trip_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_billing_patient ON ambulance_billing(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_billing_status ON ambulance_billing(billing_status, billing_date);

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_dispatch_time ON ambulance_dispatch_log(call_received_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_dispatch_priority ON ambulance_dispatch_log(dispatch_priority, emergency_severity);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_dispatch_ambulance ON ambulance_dispatch_log(dispatched_ambulance_id);

-- Partitioning for large tables
CREATE TABLE ambulance_gps_logs_y2024 PARTITION OF ambulance_gps_logs
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

CREATE TABLE ambulance_trips_y2024 PARTITION OF ambulance_trips
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_ambulance_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.trip_status IN ('dispatched', 'en_route_pickup', 'at_pickup', 'transporting') THEN
        UPDATE ambulance_fleet
        SET current_status = 'dispatched'
        WHERE ambulance_id = NEW.ambulance_id;
    ELSIF NEW.trip_status IN ('completed', 'cancelled') THEN
        UPDATE ambulance_fleet
        SET current_status = 'available'
        WHERE ambulance_id = NEW.ambulance_id;
    END IF;
    
    IF NEW.trip_status = 'completed' THEN
        UPDATE ambulance_staff
        SET total_trips_completed = total_trips_completed + 1
        WHERE staff_id IN (NEW.driver_id, NEW.paramedic_id);
        
        UPDATE ambulance_fleet
        SET odometer_reading = NEW.odometer_end
        WHERE ambulance_id = NEW.ambulance_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_ambulance_status
    AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON ambulance_trips
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_ambulance_status();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_maintenance_overdue()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.maintenance_status = 'scheduled' AND NEW.scheduled_date < CURRENT_DATE THEN
        NEW.maintenance_status = 'overdue';
    END IF;
    
    IF NEW.performed_date IS NOT NULL THEN
        NEW.maintenance_status = 'completed';
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_maintenance_overdue
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON ambulance_maintenance
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION check_maintenance_overdue();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION prevent_dispatch_unmaintained()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.current_status = 'maintenance' AND OLD.current_status != 'maintenance' THEN
        -- Cancel any active trips for this ambulance
        UPDATE ambulance_trips
        SET trip_status = 'cancelled'
        WHERE ambulance_id = NEW.ambulance_id 
        AND trip_status IN ('dispatched', 'en_route_pickup', 'at_pickup', 'transporting');
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_prevent_dispatch_unmaintained
    AFTER UPDATE ON ambulance_fleet
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION prevent_dispatch_unmaintained();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION validate_staff_certification()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.is_certification_valid = false AND NEW.current_status != 'off_duty' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Staff member with expired certification cannot be assigned to active duty';
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_validate_staff_certification
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON ambulance_staff
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION validate_staff_certification();

-- RLS Policies
CREATE POLICY ambulance_tenant_isolation ON ambulance_fleet
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY trips_privacy ON ambulance_trips
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY gps_data_access ON ambulance_gps_logs
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('dispatcher', 'fleet_manager', 'system_admin'));

-- View for available ambulances
CREATE VIEW available_ambulances AS
SELECT 
    af.ambulance_id,
    af.vehicle_number,
    af.vehicle_type,
    af.current_status,
    af.base_location,
    COUNT(ae.equipment_id) FILTER (WHERE ae.is_available = true) as available_equipment_count,
    af.odometer_reading,
    am.next_service_date,
    am.next_service_odometer
FROM ambulance_fleet af
LEFT JOIN ambulance_equipment ae ON af.ambulance_id = ae.ambulance_id
LEFT JOIN ambulance_maintenance am ON af.ambulance_id = am.ambulance_id 
    AND am.maintenance_status IN ('scheduled', 'overdue')
WHERE af.is_active = true
AND af.current_status = 'available'
AND (am.next_service_odometer IS NULL OR af.odometer_reading < am.next_service_odometer - 100)
GROUP BY af.ambulance_id, am.next_service_date, am.next_service_odometer;

-- View for staff certifications
CREATE VIEW staff_certification_status AS
SELECT 
    s.staff_id,
    u.first_name,
    u.last_name,
    s.staff_type,
    s.certification_type,
    s.certification_expiry_date,
    s.is_certification_valid,
    s.current_status,
    CASE 
        WHEN s.certification_expiry_date IS NOT NULL AND 
             s.certification_expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '30 days'
        THEN 'expiring_soon'
        WHEN s.is_certification_valid = false
        THEN 'expired'
        ELSE 'valid'
    END as certification_alert
FROM ambulance_staff s
JOIN users u ON s.user_id = u.user_id
WHERE s.certification_expiry_date IS NOT NULL;
```

```sql
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN latitude_type NUMERIC(10,8) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN -90 AND 90);

CREATE DOMAIN longitude_type NUMERIC(11,8) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN -180 AND 180);

CREATE DOMAIN currency_type NUMERIC(12,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0);

CREATE DOMAIN phone_type VARCHAR(20);
CREATE DOMAIN email_type VARCHAR(255);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE vehicle_type_type AS ENUM (
    'bls', 'als', 'mobile_icu', 'air', 'neonatal'
);

CREATE TYPE vehicle_status_type AS ENUM (
    'available', 'dispatched', 'maintenance', 'retired'
);

CREATE TYPE fuel_type_type AS ENUM (
    'petrol', 'diesel', 'hybrid', 'electric'
);

CREATE TYPE equipment_condition_type AS ENUM (
    'excellent', 'good', 'needs_service', 'out_of_service'
);

CREATE TYPE staff_status_type AS ENUM (
    'available', 'on_trip', 'off_duty', 'training'
);

CREATE TYPE trip_type_type AS ENUM (
    'emergency', 'inter_facility', 'scheduled', 'discharge'
);

CREATE TYPE trip_status_type AS ENUM (
    'dispatched', 'en_route_pickup', 'at_pickup', 'transporting', 'completed', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE signal_quality_type AS ENUM (
    'excellent', 'good', 'poor', 'lost'
);

CREATE TYPE maintenance_type_type AS ENUM (
    'preventive', 'corrective', 'emergency'
);

CREATE TYPE maintenance_status_type AS ENUM (
    'scheduled', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'overdue'
);

CREATE TYPE billing_status_type AS ENUM (
    'draft', 'sent', 'paid', 'insurance_claim'
);

CREATE TYPE claim_status_type AS ENUM (
    'submitted', 'approved', 'rejected', 'partially_paid'
);

CREATE TYPE emergency_type_type AS ENUM (
    'medical', 'trauma', 'psychiatric', 'ob_gyn'
);

CREATE TYPE severity_type AS ENUM (
    'critical', 'serious', 'moderate', 'minor'
);

CREATE TYPE priority_type AS ENUM (
    'immediate', 'high', 'medium', 'low'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE ambulance_fleet (
    ambulance_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    vehicle_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    vehicle_type vehicle_type_type NOT NULL,
    make VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    model VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    year SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (year BETWEEN 1900 AND EXTRACT(YEAR FROM CURRENT_DATE) + 1),
    vin VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    insurance_number VARCHAR(100),
    insurance_expiry DATE,
    capacity_patients SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (capacity_patients > 0),
    capacity_staff SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (capacity_staff > 0),
    odometer_reading NUMERIC(10,1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    fuel_type fuel_type_type NOT NULL,
    current_status vehicle_status_type DEFAULT 'available',
    base_location VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    gps_device_id VARCHAR(100),
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_insurance_expiry CHECK (
        insurance_expiry IS NULL OR insurance_expiry >= CURRENT_DATE
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_equipment (
    equipment_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    ambulance_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    equipment_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (equipment_type BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
    equipment_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    serial_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    calibration_date DATE,
    next_calibration_date DATE,
    expiry_date DATE,
    current_condition equipment_condition_type DEFAULT 'excellent',
    last_service_date DATE,
    is_available BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (ambulance_id) REFERENCES ambulance_fleet(ambulance_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_calibration_dates CHECK (
        calibration_date IS NULL OR next_calibration_date IS NULL OR 
        next_calibration_date > calibration_date
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_staff (
    staff_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    user_id UUID NOT NULL,
    staff_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (staff_type BETWEEN 1 AND 4),
    certification_number VARCHAR(100),
    certification_type VARCHAR(100),
    certification_issue_date DATE,
    certification_expiry_date DATE,
    is_certification_valid BOOLEAN GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        certification_expiry_date IS NOT NULL AND 
        certification_expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE
    ) STORED,
    training_completed TEXT[],
    shift_schedule JSONB,
    current_status staff_status_type DEFAULT 'available',
    total_trips_completed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_certification_dates CHECK (
        certification_issue_date IS NULL OR certification_expiry_date IS NULL OR 
        certification_expiry_date > certification_issue_date
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_trips (
    trip_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    trip_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    ambulance_id UUID NOT NULL,
    driver_id UUID NOT NULL,
    paramedic_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID,
    call_received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    trip_type trip_type_type NOT NULL,
    pickup_address TEXT NOT NULL,
    pickup_lat latitude_type,
    pickup_lon longitude_type,
    pickup_geom GEOGRAPHY(POINT,4326),
    destination_address TEXT NOT NULL,
    destination_lat latitude_type,
    destination_lon longitude_type,
    destination_geom GEOGRAPHY(POINT,4326),
    dispatched_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    arrived_at_pickup TIMESTAMPTZ,
    departed_pickup_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    arrived_at_destination_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    trip_status trip_status_type DEFAULT 'dispatched',
    odometer_start NUMERIC(10,1),
    odometer_end NUMERIC(10,1),
    distance_km NUMERIC(8,2) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN odometer_start IS NOT NULL AND odometer_end IS NOT NULL 
            THEN odometer_end - odometer_start 
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    receiving_facility_id UUID,
    receiving_doctor_id UUID,
    handover_notes TEXT,
    estimated_arrival_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    eta_communicated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    eta_communicated_to VARCHAR(255),
    trip_billing_id UUID,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (ambulance_id) REFERENCES ambulance_fleet(ambulance_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (driver_id) REFERENCES ambulance_staff(staff_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (paramedic_id) REFERENCES ambulance_staff(staff_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (receiving_facility_id) REFERENCES facilities(facility_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (receiving_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_trip_timeline CHECK (
        call_received_at <= dispatched_at AND
        dispatched_at <= arrived_at_pickup AND
        arrived_at_pickup <= departed_pickup_at AND
        departed_pickup_at <= arrived_at_destination_at
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_odometer_values CHECK (
        odometer_start IS NULL OR odometer_end IS NULL OR 
        odometer_end >= odometer_start
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_gps_logs (
    gps_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    trip_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    ambulance_id UUID NOT NULL,
    latitude latitude_type NOT NULL,
    longitude longitude_type NOT NULL,
    location_geom GEOGRAPHY(POINT,4326) NOT NULL,
    speed_kmph NUMERIC(5,1) DEFAULT 0,
    heading_degrees SMALLINT CHECK (heading_degrees BETWEEN 0 AND 359),
    logged_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    signal_quality signal_quality_type DEFAULT 'good',
    device_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES ambulance_trips(trip_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (ambulance_id) REFERENCES ambulance_fleet(ambulance_id)
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_maintenance (
    maintenance_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    ambulance_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    maintenance_type maintenance_type_type NOT NULL,
    scheduled_date DATE NOT NULL,
    performed_date DATE,
    odometer_at_service NUMERIC(10,1) NOT NULL,
    service_provider VARCHAR(255),
    service_cost currency_type DEFAULT 0,
    work_performed TEXT[],
    parts_replaced TEXT[],
    next_service_date DATE,
    next_service_odometer NUMERIC(10,1),
    performed_by UUID,
    verified_by UUID,
    maintenance_status maintenance_status_type DEFAULT 'scheduled',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (ambulance_id) REFERENCES ambulance_fleet(ambulance_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (performed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_maintenance_dates CHECK (
        performed_date IS NULL OR performed_date >= scheduled_date
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_next_service CHECK (
        next_service_odometer IS NULL OR next_service_odometer > odometer_at_service
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_billing (
    billing_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    trip_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    billing_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    base_fare currency_type NOT NULL,
    distance_charge currency_type NOT NULL,
    time_charge currency_type NOT NULL,
    equipment_usage_charge currency_type DEFAULT 0,
    medication_charge currency_type DEFAULT 0,
    total_amount currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        base_fare + distance_charge + time_charge + 
        equipment_usage_charge + medication_charge
    ) STORED,
    insurance_covered currency_type DEFAULT 0,
    patient_payable currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        total_amount - insurance_covered
    ) STORED,
    billing_status billing_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    insurance_claim_number VARCHAR(100),
    claim_status claim_status_type,
    paid_date DATE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (trip_id) REFERENCES ambulance_trips(trip_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_insurance_coverage CHECK (
        insurance_covered <= total_amount
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_dates CHECK (
        paid_date IS NULL OR paid_date >= billing_date
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ambulance_dispatch_log (
    dispatch_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    call_received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    caller_number phone_type NOT NULL,
    caller_name VARCHAR(100),
    caller_location VARCHAR(255),
    emergency_type emergency_type_type NOT NULL,
    emergency_severity severity_type NOT NULL,
    dispatch_priority priority_type NOT NULL,
    dispatched_ambulance_id UUID,
    dispatch_time_sec INTEGER,
    response_time_sec INTEGER,
    call_recording_ref VARCHAR(100),
    dispatcher_id UUID NOT NULL,
    dispatch_notes TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dispatched_ambulance_id) REFERENCES ambulance_fleet(ambulance_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dispatcher_id) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_fleet_status ON ambulance_fleet(current_status, vehicle_type) WHERE is_active = true;
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_fleet_location ON ambulance_fleet(base_location);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_fleet_gps ON ambulance_fleet(gps_device_id) WHERE gps_device_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_equipment_ambulance ON ambulance_equipment(ambulance_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_equipment_condition ON ambulance_equipment(current_condition) WHERE is_available = true;
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_equipment_calibration ON ambulance_equipment(next_calibration_date) WHERE next_calibration_date IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_staff_certification ON ambulance_staff(certification_expiry_date) WHERE is_certification_valid = false;
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_staff_status ON ambulance_staff(current_status, staff_type);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_staff_user ON ambulance_staff(user_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_trips_status ON ambulance_trips(trip_status, dispatched_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_trips_ambulance ON ambulance_trips(ambulance_id, trip_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_trips_patient ON ambulance_trips(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_trips_dates ON ambulance_trips(call_received_at, dispatched_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_trips_geo_pickup ON ambulance_trips USING GIST (pickup_geom);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_trips_geo_dest ON ambulance_trips USING GIST (destination_geom);

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_gps_trip ON ambulance_gps_logs(trip_id, logged_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_gps_ambulance ON ambulance_gps_logs(ambulance_id, logged_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_gps_geo ON ambulance_gps_logs USING GIST (location_geom);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_gps_time ON ambulance_gps_logs(logged_at);

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_maintenance_ambulance ON ambulance_maintenance(ambulance_id, maintenance_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_maintenance_dates ON ambulance_maintenance(scheduled_date, performed_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_maintenance_status ON ambulance_maintenance(maintenance_status) WHERE maintenance_status = 'overdue';

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_billing_trip ON ambulance_billing(trip_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_billing_patient ON ambulance_billing(patient_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_billing_status ON ambulance_billing(billing_status, billing_date);

CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_dispatch_time ON ambulance_dispatch_log(call_received_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_dispatch_priority ON ambulance_dispatch_log(dispatch_priority, emergency_severity);
CREATE INDEX idx_ambulance_dispatch_ambulance ON ambulance_dispatch_log(dispatched_ambulance_id);

-- Partitioning for large tables
CREATE TABLE ambulance_gps_logs_y2024 PARTITION OF ambulance_gps_logs
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

CREATE TABLE ambulance_trips_y2024 PARTITION OF ambulance_trips
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_ambulance_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.trip_status IN ('dispatched', 'en_route_pickup', 'at_pickup', 'transporting') THEN
        UPDATE ambulance_fleet
        SET current_status = 'dispatched'
        WHERE ambulance_id = NEW.ambulance_id;
    ELSIF NEW.trip_status IN ('completed', 'cancelled') THEN
        UPDATE ambulance_fleet
        SET current_status = 'available'
        WHERE ambulance_id = NEW.ambulance_id;
    END IF;
    
    IF NEW.trip_status = 'completed' THEN
        UPDATE ambulance_staff
        SET total_trips_completed = total_trips_completed + 1
        WHERE staff_id IN (NEW.driver_id, NEW.paramedic_id);
        
        UPDATE ambulance_fleet
        SET odometer_reading = NEW.odometer_end
        WHERE ambulance_id = NEW.ambulance_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_ambulance_status
    AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON ambulance_trips
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_ambulance_status();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_maintenance_overdue()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.maintenance_status = 'scheduled' AND NEW.scheduled_date < CURRENT_DATE THEN
        NEW.maintenance_status = 'overdue';
    END IF;
    
    IF NEW.performed_date IS NOT NULL THEN
        NEW.maintenance_status = 'completed';
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_maintenance_overdue
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON ambulance_maintenance
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION check_maintenance_overdue();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION prevent_dispatch_unmaintained()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.current_status = 'maintenance' AND OLD.current_status != 'maintenance' THEN
        -- Cancel any active trips for this ambulance
        UPDATE ambulance_trips
        SET trip_status = 'cancelled'
        WHERE ambulance_id = NEW.ambulance_id 
        AND trip_status IN ('dispatched', 'en_route_pickup', 'at_pickup', 'transporting');
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_prevent_dispatch_unmaintained
    AFTER UPDATE ON ambulance_fleet
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION prevent_dispatch_unmaintained();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION validate_staff_certification()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.is_certification_valid = false AND NEW.current_status != 'off_duty' THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Staff member with expired certification cannot be assigned to active duty';
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_validate_staff_certification
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON ambulance_staff
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION validate_staff_certification();

-- RLS Policies
CREATE POLICY ambulance_tenant_isolation ON ambulance_fleet
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY trips_privacy ON ambulance_trips
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY gps_data_access ON ambulance_gps_logs
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('dispatcher', 'fleet_manager', 'system_admin'));

-- View for available ambulances
CREATE VIEW available_ambulances AS
SELECT 
    af.ambulance_id,
    af.vehicle_number,
    af.vehicle_type,
    af.current_status,
    af.base_location,
    COUNT(ae.equipment_id) FILTER (WHERE ae.is_available = true) as available_equipment_count,
    af.odometer_reading,
    am.next_service_date,
    am.next_service_odometer
FROM ambulance_fleet af
LEFT JOIN ambulance_equipment ae ON af.ambulance_id = ae.ambulance_id
LEFT JOIN ambulance_maintenance am ON af.ambulance_id = am.ambulance_id 
    AND am.maintenance_status IN ('scheduled', 'overdue')
WHERE af.is_active = true
AND af.current_status = 'available'
AND (am.next_service_odometer IS NULL OR af.odometer_reading < am.next_service_odometer - 100)
GROUP BY af.ambulance_id, am.next_service_date, am.next_service_odometer;

-- View for staff certifications
CREATE VIEW staff_certification_status AS
SELECT 
    s.staff_id,
    u.first_name,
    u.last_name,
    s.staff_type,
    s.certification_type,
    s.certification_expiry_date,
    s.is_certification_valid,
    s.current_status,
    CASE 
        WHEN s.certification_expiry_date IS NOT NULL AND 
             s.certification_expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '30 days'
        THEN 'expiring_soon'
        WHEN s.is_certification_valid = false
        THEN 'expired'
        ELSE 'valid'
    END as certification_alert
FROM ambulance_staff s
JOIN users u ON s.user_id = u.user_id
WHERE s.certification_expiry_date IS NOT NULL;
```

This implementation includes:

1. Spatial data support with PostGIS geography types for GPS tracking
2. Generated columns for automatic calculations (distance, certification validity, billing totals)
3. Complex check constraints for business rule enforcement
4. Comprehensive indexing including spatial indexes for geographic queries
5. Table partitioning for high-volume GPS logs and trips
6. Automated status updates through triggers
7. Maintenance enforcement to prevent dispatch of overdue vehicles
8. Certification validation to ensure staff qualifications
9. RLS policies for data security and tenant isolation
10. Useful views for operational monitoring
