---
## Domain 10: Clinical OPD (Outpatient Department)

Here's the PostgreSQL implementation for the Clinical OPD (Outpatient Department) domain:

sql

```
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN temperature_c_type NUMERIC(4,1) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN 25 AND 45);

CREATE DOMAIN percentage_type NUMERIC(5,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN 0 AND 100);

CREATE DOMAIN phone_type VARCHAR(20);
CREATE DOMAIN email_type VARCHAR(255);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE appointment_type_type AS ENUM (
    'new', 'follow_up', 'review', 'procedure'
);

CREATE TYPE appointment_status_type AS ENUM (
    'scheduled', 'checked_in', 'in_consultation', 'completed', 'cancelled', 'no_show'
);

CREATE TYPE priority_type AS ENUM (
    'routine', 'urgent', 'emergency'
);

CREATE TYPE visit_type_type AS ENUM (
    'scheduled', 'walk_in', 'emergency'
);

CREATE TYPE visit_status_type AS ENUM (
    'registered', 'triaged', 'waiting', 'in_consultation', 'completed'
);

CREATE TYPE payment_status_type AS ENUM (
    'pending', 'partially_paid', 'paid'
);

CREATE TYPE consultation_status_type AS ENUM (
    'draft', 'completed', 'signed'
);

CREATE TYPE diagnosis_type_type AS ENUM (
    'primary', 'secondary', 'provisional'
);

CREATE TYPE diagnosis_certainty_type AS ENUM (
    'confirmed', 'suspected', 'ruled_out'
);

CREATE TYPE severity_type AS ENUM (
    'mild', 'moderate', 'severe'
);

CREATE TYPE referral_status_type AS ENUM (
    'requested', 'accepted', 'completed', 'rejected'
);

CREATE TYPE follow_up_status_type AS ENUM (
    'pending', 'scheduled', 'completed', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE queue_status_type AS ENUM (
    'waiting', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'skipped'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE opd_appointments (
    appointment_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    department_id UUID NOT NULL,
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_date DATE NOT NULL,
    appointment_time TIME NOT NULL,
    appointment_type appointment_type_type NOT NULL,
    appointment_status appointment_status_type DEFAULT 'scheduled',
    reason_for_visit TEXT,
    priority priority_type DEFAULT 'routine',
    scheduled_by UUID NOT NULL,
    cancelled_reason TEXT,
    cancelled_by UUID,
    cancelled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    reminder_sent BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    reminder_sent_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (department_id) REFERENCES departments(department_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (scheduled_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (cancelled_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_appointment_date CHECK (
        appointment_date >= CURRENT_DATE
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_cancellation_consistency CHECK (
        (cancelled_reason IS NULL AND cancelled_by IS NULL AND cancelled_at IS NULL) OR
        (cancelled_reason IS NOT NULL AND cancelled_by IS NOT NULL AND cancelled_at IS NOT NULL)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_visits (
    visit_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID,
    visit_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    visit_type visit_type_type NOT NULL,
    visit_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    visit_time TIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIME,
    registration_time TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    triage_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    consultation_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completion_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    visit_status visit_status_type DEFAULT 'registered',
    payment_status payment_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    is_insurance_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES opd_appointments(appointment_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_visit_timeline CHECK (
        registration_time <= COALESCE(triage_time, registration_time) AND
        COALESCE(triage_time, registration_time) <= COALESCE(consultation_time, registration_time) AND
        COALESCE(consultation_time, registration_time) <= COALESCE(completion_time, registration_time)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_vital_signs (
    vital_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    visit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    temperature temperature_c_type,
    heart_rate SMALLINT CHECK (heart_rate BETWEEN 30 AND 250),
    blood_pressure_systolic SMALLINT CHECK (blood_pressure_systolic BETWEEN 60 AND 250),
    blood_pressure_diastolic SMALLINT CHECK (blood_pressure_diastolic BETWEEN 40 AND 150),
    respiratory_rate SMALLINT CHECK (respiratory_rate BETWEEN 8 AND 60),
    oxygen_saturation percentage_type,
    pain_score SMALLINT CHECK (pain_score BETWEEN 0 AND 10),
    height_cm NUMERIC(5,1) CHECK (height_cm BETWEEN 30 AND 250),
    weight_kg NUMERIC(5,1) CHECK (weight_kg BETWEEN 1 AND 300),
    bmi NUMERIC(4,1) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN height_cm IS NOT NULL AND height_cm > 0 AND weight_kg IS NOT NULL 
            THEN weight_kg / ((height_cm / 100) * (height_cm / 100))
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    captured_by UUID NOT NULL,
    captured_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    vital_location VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (visit_id) REFERENCES opd_visits(visit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (captured_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_blood_pressure CHECK (
        blood_pressure_systolic IS NULL OR blood_pressure_diastolic IS NULL OR 
        blood_pressure_systolic > blood_pressure_diastolic
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_consultations (
    consultation_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    visit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    subjective TEXT,
    objective TEXT,
    assessment TEXT,
    plan TEXT,
    consultation_start_time TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    consultation_end_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    duration_minutes SMALLINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN consultation_start_time IS NOT NULL AND consultation_end_time IS NOT NULL 
            THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (consultation_end_time - consultation_start_time)) / 60
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    consultation_status consultation_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    signed_by UUID,
    signed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    template_used VARCHAR(100),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (visit_id) REFERENCES opd_visits(visit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (signed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_consultation_times CHECK (
        consultation_start_time <= COALESCE(consultation_end_time, consultation_start_time)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_diagnoses (
    diagnosis_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    consultation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    diagnosis_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    diagnosis_description VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    diagnosis_type diagnosis_type_type NOT NULL,
    diagnosis_certainty diagnosis_certainty_type DEFAULT 'confirmed',
    onset_date DATE,
    is_chronic BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    affected_body_system VARCHAR(100),
    severity severity_type,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (consultation_id) REFERENCES opd_consultations(consultation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_onset_date CHECK (
        onset_date IS NULL OR onset_date <= CURRENT_DATE
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_prescriptions (
    opd_prescription_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    consultation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL,
    is_eprescription BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    printed_copy_provided BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    provided_by UUID NOT NULL,
    provided_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (consultation_id) REFERENCES opd_consultations(consultation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (prescription_id) REFERENCES rx_prescriptions(prescription_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (provided_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE opd_referrals (
    referral_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    consultation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    referring_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    referred_to_department UUID,
    referred_to_doctor_id UUID,
    external_facility_name VARCHAR(255),
    external_doctor_name VARCHAR(100),
    external_contact VARCHAR(255),
    referral_reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    referral_priority priority_type DEFAULT 'routine',
    referral_status referral_status_type DEFAULT 'requested',
    appointment_given BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    appointment_date DATE,
    feedback_received TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (consultation_id) REFERENCES opd_consultations(consultation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (referring_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (referred_to_department) REFERENCES departments(department_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (referred_to_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_referral_target CHECK (
        (referred_to_department IS NOT NULL OR referred_to_doctor_id IS NOT NULL) OR
        (external_facility_name IS NOT NULL)
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_appointment_date_consistency CHECK (
        appointment_given = false OR appointment_date IS NOT NULL
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_follow_ups (
    follow_up_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    consultation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    follow_up_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (follow_up_type BETWEEN 1 AND 3),
    recommended_interval_days SMALLINT CHECK (recommended_interval_days > 0),
    scheduled_date DATE,
    scheduled_time TIME,
    follow_up_reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    tests_to_review UUID[],
    status follow_up_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    created_by UUID NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (consultation_id) REFERENCES opd_consultations(consultation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_follow_up_scheduling CHECK (
        status != 'scheduled' OR (scheduled_date IS NOT NULL AND scheduled_time IS NOT NULL)
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_follow_up_date CHECK (
        scheduled_date IS NULL OR scheduled_date >= CURRENT_DATE
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_queue_management (
    queue_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    visit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    department_id UUID NOT NULL,
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    queue_number SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    queue_status queue_status_type DEFAULT 'waiting',
    wait_time_minutes INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN queue_status = 'completed' AND created_at IS NOT NULL AND completed_at IS NOT NULL
            THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (completed_at - created_at)) / 60
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    estimated_wait_time INTEGER,
    current_position SMALLINT,
    called_by UUID,
    called_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (visit_id) REFERENCES opd_visits(visit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (department_id) REFERENCES departments(department_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (called_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_queue_timeline CHECK (
        created_at <= COALESCE(called_at, created_at) AND
        COALESCE(called_at, created_at) <= COALESCE(completed_at, created_at)
    )
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_appointments_date_doctor ON opd_appointments(appointment_date, doctor_id, appointment_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_appointments_patient ON opd_appointments(patient_id, appointment_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_appointments_status ON opd_appointments(appointment_status) WHERE appointment_status = 'scheduled';
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_appointments_reminder ON opd_appointments(appointment_date, reminder_sent) WHERE reminder_sent = false;

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_visits_date ON opd_visits(visit_date, visit_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_visits_patient ON opd_visits(patient_id, visit_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_visits_appointment ON opd_visits(appointment_id) WHERE appointment_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_visits_status ON opd_visits(visit_status) WHERE visit_status != 'completed';

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_vital_signs_visit ON opd_vital_signs(visit_id, captured_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_vital_signs_patient ON opd_vital_signs(patient_id, captured_at);

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_consultations_visit ON opd_consultations(visit_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_consultations_doctor ON opd_consultations(doctor_id, consultation_start_time);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_consultations_status ON opd_consultations(consultation_status) WHERE consultation_status = 'draft';

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_diagnoses_consultation ON opd_diagnoses(consultation_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_diagnoses_code ON opd_diagnoses(diagnosis_code);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_diagnoses_patient ON opd_diagnoses(patient_id, onset_date);

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_prescriptions_consultation ON opd_prescriptions(consultation_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_prescriptions_patient ON opd_prescriptions(patient_id, provided_at);

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_referrals_consultation ON opd_referrals(consultation_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_referrals_status ON opd_referrals(referral_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_referrals_doctor ON opd_referrals(referring_doctor_id, created_at);

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_follow_ups_consultation ON opd_follow_ups(consultation_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_follow_ups_patient ON opd_follow_ups(patient_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_follow_ups_status ON opd_follow_ups(status) WHERE status = 'pending';

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_queue_visit ON opd_queue_management(visit_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_queue_department ON opd_queue_management(department_id, queue_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_queue_status ON opd_queue_management(queue_status) WHERE queue_status = 'waiting';
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_queue_doctor ON opd_queue_management(doctor_id, queue_status);

-- Partitioning
CREATE TABLE opd_visits_y2024 PARTITION OF opd_visits
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

CREATE TABLE opd_appointments_y2024 PARTITION OF opd_appointments
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_visit_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update visit status based on consultation completion
    IF NEW.consultation_status = 'completed' AND OLD.consultation_status != 'completed' THEN
        UPDATE opd_visits
        SET visit_status = 'completed',
            completion_time = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE visit_id = NEW.visit_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_visit_status
    AFTER UPDATE ON opd_consultations
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_visit_status();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_appointment_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update appointment status when visit is created from appointment
    IF NEW.appointment_id IS NOT NULL AND NEW.visit_status = 'registered' THEN
        UPDATE opd_appointments
        SET appointment_status = 'checked_in'
        WHERE appointment_id = NEW.appointment_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_appointment_status
    AFTER INSERT ON opd_visits
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_appointment_status();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION manage_queue_on_visit()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Add to queue when visit is registered
    IF NEW.visit_status = 'registered' THEN
        INSERT INTO opd_queue_management (
            tenant_id, branch_id, visit_id, patient_id, 
            department_id, doctor_id, queue_number, current_position
        )
        SELECT 
            NEW.tenant_id, NEW.branch_id, NEW.visit_id, NEW.patient_id,
            a.department_id, a.doctor_id,
            (SELECT COALESCE(MAX(queue_number), 0) + 1 
             FROM opd_queue_management 
             WHERE department_id = a.department_id AND visit_date = CURRENT_DATE),
            (SELECT COUNT(*) + 1 
             FROM opd_queue_management 
             WHERE department_id = a.department_id AND queue_status = 'waiting')
        FROM opd_appointments a
        WHERE a.appointment_id = NEW.appointment_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_manage_queue_on_visit
    AFTER INSERT ON opd_visits
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION manage_queue_on_visit();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_doctor_availability()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    existing_appointments INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Check for overlapping appointments for the same doctor
    IF NEW.appointment_status = 'scheduled' THEN
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        INTO existing_appointments
        FROM opd_appointments
        WHERE doctor_id = NEW.doctor_id
        AND appointment_date = NEW.appointment_date
        AND appointment_time BETWEEN NEW.appointment_time - INTERVAL '15 minutes' 
                             AND NEW.appointment_time + INTERVAL '15 minutes'
        AND appointment_status IN ('scheduled', 'checked_in', 'in_consultation')
        AND appointment_id != NEW.appointment_id;
        
        IF existing_appointments > 0 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Doctor has overlapping appointments';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_doctor_availability
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON opd_appointments
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION check_doctor_availability();

-- RLS Policies
CREATE POLICY opd_tenant_isolation ON opd_appointments
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY visits_privacy ON opd_visits
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY consultations_access ON opd_consultations
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('doctor', 'nurse', 'system_admin'));

-- Views
CREATE VIEW current_opd_queue AS
SELECT 
    q.queue_id,
    q.visit_id,
    q.patient_id,
    p.first_name,
    p.last_name,
    q.department_id,
    d.department_name,
    q.doctor_id,
    doc.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    doc.last_name as doctor_last_name,
    q.queue_number,
    q.queue_status,
    q.current_position,
    q.wait_time_minutes,
    q.estimated_wait_time,
    v.visit_type,
    v.visit_status
FROM opd_queue_management q
JOIN opd_visits v ON q.visit_id = v.visit_id
JOIN patients p ON q.patient_id = p.patient_id
JOIN departments d ON q.department_id = d.department_id
JOIN doctors doc ON q.doctor_id = doc.doctor_id
WHERE q.queue_status IN ('waiting', 'in_progress')
ORDER BY q.department_id, q.queue_number;

CREATE VIEW doctor_appointment_schedule AS
SELECT 
    a.appointment_id,
    a.appointment_date,
    a.appointment_time,
    a.appointment_type,
    a.appointment_status,
    a.priority,
    p.patient_id,
    p.first_name,
    p.last_name,
    p.phone_number,
    a.reason_for_visit,
    d.department_name,
    doc.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    doc.last_name as doctor_last_name
FROM opd_appointments a
JOIN patients p ON a.patient_id = p.patient_id
JOIN departments d ON a.department_id = d.department_id
JOIN doctors doc ON a.doctor_id = doc.doctor_id
WHERE a.appointment_date >= CURRENT_DATE
ORDER BY a.appointment_date, a.appointment_time;

CREATE VIEW patient_visit_history AS
SELECT 
    v.visit_id,
    v.visit_date,
    v.visit_type,
    v.visit_status,
    d.department_name,
    doc.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    doc.last_name as doctor_last_name,
    c.consultation_start_time,
    c.consultation_end_time,
    c.consultation_status
FROM opd_visits v
LEFT JOIN opd_appointments a ON v.appointment_id = a.appointment_id
LEFT JOIN departments d ON a.department_id = d.department_id
LEFT JOIN doctors doc ON a.doctor_id = doc.doctor_id
LEFT JOIN opd_consultations c ON v.visit_id = c.visit_id
ORDER BY v.visit_date DESC, v.visit_time DESC;
```

```
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN temperature_c_type NUMERIC(4,1) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN 25 AND 45);

CREATE DOMAIN percentage_type NUMERIC(5,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN 0 AND 100);

CREATE DOMAIN phone_type VARCHAR(20);
CREATE DOMAIN email_type VARCHAR(255);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE appointment_type_type AS ENUM (
    'new', 'follow_up', 'review', 'procedure'
);

CREATE TYPE appointment_status_type AS ENUM (
    'scheduled', 'checked_in', 'in_consultation', 'completed', 'cancelled', 'no_show'
);

CREATE TYPE priority_type AS ENUM (
    'routine', 'urgent', 'emergency'
);

CREATE TYPE visit_type_type AS ENUM (
    'scheduled', 'walk_in', 'emergency'
);

CREATE TYPE visit_status_type AS ENUM (
    'registered', 'triaged', 'waiting', 'in_consultation', 'completed'
);

CREATE TYPE payment_status_type AS ENUM (
    'pending', 'partially_paid', 'paid'
);

CREATE TYPE consultation_status_type AS ENUM (
    'draft', 'completed', 'signed'
);

CREATE TYPE diagnosis_type_type AS ENUM (
    'primary', 'secondary', 'provisional'
);

CREATE TYPE diagnosis_certainty_type AS ENUM (
    'confirmed', 'suspected', 'ruled_out'
);

CREATE TYPE severity_type AS ENUM (
    'mild', 'moderate', 'severe'
);

CREATE TYPE referral_status_type AS ENUM (
    'requested', 'accepted', 'completed', 'rejected'
);

CREATE TYPE follow_up_status_type AS ENUM (
    'pending', 'scheduled', 'completed', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE queue_status_type AS ENUM (
    'waiting', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'skipped'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE opd_appointments (
    appointment_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    department_id UUID NOT NULL,
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_date DATE NOT NULL,
    appointment_time TIME NOT NULL,
    appointment_type appointment_type_type NOT NULL,
    appointment_status appointment_status_type DEFAULT 'scheduled',
    reason_for_visit TEXT,
    priority priority_type DEFAULT 'routine',
    scheduled_by UUID NOT NULL,
    cancelled_reason TEXT,
    cancelled_by UUID,
    cancelled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    reminder_sent BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    reminder_sent_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (department_id) REFERENCES departments(department_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (scheduled_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (cancelled_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_appointment_date CHECK (
        appointment_date >= CURRENT_DATE
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_cancellation_consistency CHECK (
        (cancelled_reason IS NULL AND cancelled_by IS NULL AND cancelled_at IS NULL) OR
        (cancelled_reason IS NOT NULL AND cancelled_by IS NOT NULL AND cancelled_at IS NOT NULL)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_visits (
    visit_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID,
    visit_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    visit_type visit_type_type NOT NULL,
    visit_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    visit_time TIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIME,
    registration_time TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    triage_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    consultation_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completion_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    visit_status visit_status_type DEFAULT 'registered',
    payment_status payment_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    is_insurance_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES opd_appointments(appointment_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_visit_timeline CHECK (
        registration_time <= COALESCE(triage_time, registration_time) AND
        COALESCE(triage_time, registration_time) <= COALESCE(consultation_time, registration_time) AND
        COALESCE(consultation_time, registration_time) <= COALESCE(completion_time, registration_time)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_vital_signs (
    vital_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    visit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    temperature temperature_c_type,
    heart_rate SMALLINT CHECK (heart_rate BETWEEN 30 AND 250),
    blood_pressure_systolic SMALLINT CHECK (blood_pressure_systolic BETWEEN 60 AND 250),
    blood_pressure_diastolic SMALLINT CHECK (blood_pressure_diastolic BETWEEN 40 AND 150),
    respiratory_rate SMALLINT CHECK (respiratory_rate BETWEEN 8 AND 60),
    oxygen_saturation percentage_type,
    pain_score SMALLINT CHECK (pain_score BETWEEN 0 AND 10),
    height_cm NUMERIC(5,1) CHECK (height_cm BETWEEN 30 AND 250),
    weight_kg NUMERIC(5,1) CHECK (weight_kg BETWEEN 1 AND 300),
    bmi NUMERIC(4,1) GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN height_cm IS NOT NULL AND height_cm > 0 AND weight_kg IS NOT NULL 
            THEN weight_kg / ((height_cm / 100) * (height_cm / 100))
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    captured_by UUID NOT NULL,
    captured_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    vital_location VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (visit_id) REFERENCES opd_visits(visit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (captured_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_blood_pressure CHECK (
        blood_pressure_systolic IS NULL OR blood_pressure_diastolic IS NULL OR 
        blood_pressure_systolic > blood_pressure_diastolic
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_consultations (
    consultation_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    visit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    subjective TEXT,
    objective TEXT,
    assessment TEXT,
    plan TEXT,
    consultation_start_time TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    consultation_end_time TIMESTAMPTZ,
    duration_minutes SMALLINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN consultation_start_time IS NOT NULL AND consultation_end_time IS NOT NULL 
            THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (consultation_end_time - consultation_start_time)) / 60
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    consultation_status consultation_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    signed_by UUID,
    signed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    template_used VARCHAR(100),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (visit_id) REFERENCES opd_visits(visit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (signed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_consultation_times CHECK (
        consultation_start_time <= COALESCE(consultation_end_time, consultation_start_time)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_diagnoses (
    diagnosis_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    consultation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    diagnosis_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    diagnosis_description VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    diagnosis_type diagnosis_type_type NOT NULL,
    diagnosis_certainty diagnosis_certainty_type DEFAULT 'confirmed',
    onset_date DATE,
    is_chronic BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    affected_body_system VARCHAR(100),
    severity severity_type,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (consultation_id) REFERENCES opd_consultations(consultation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_onset_date CHECK (
        onset_date IS NULL OR onset_date <= CURRENT_DATE
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_prescriptions (
    opd_prescription_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    consultation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL,
    is_eprescription BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    printed_copy_provided BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    provided_by UUID NOT NULL,
    provided_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (consultation_id) REFERENCES opd_consultations(consultation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (prescription_id) REFERENCES rx_prescriptions(prescription_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (provided_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE opd_referrals (
    referral_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    consultation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    referring_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    referred_to_department UUID,
    referred_to_doctor_id UUID,
    external_facility_name VARCHAR(255),
    external_doctor_name VARCHAR(100),
    external_contact VARCHAR(255),
    referral_reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    referral_priority priority_type DEFAULT 'routine',
    referral_status referral_status_type DEFAULT 'requested',
    appointment_given BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    appointment_date DATE,
    feedback_received TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (consultation_id) REFERENCES opd_consultations(consultation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (referring_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (referred_to_department) REFERENCES departments(department_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (referred_to_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_referral_target CHECK (
        (referred_to_department IS NOT NULL OR referred_to_doctor_id IS NOT NULL) OR
        (external_facility_name IS NOT NULL)
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_appointment_date_consistency CHECK (
        appointment_given = false OR appointment_date IS NOT NULL
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_follow_ups (
    follow_up_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    consultation_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    follow_up_type SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (follow_up_type BETWEEN 1 AND 3),
    recommended_interval_days SMALLINT CHECK (recommended_interval_days > 0),
    scheduled_date DATE,
    scheduled_time TIME,
    follow_up_reason TEXT NOT NULL,
    tests_to_review UUID[],
    status follow_up_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    created_by UUID NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (consultation_id) REFERENCES opd_consultations(consultation_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_follow_up_scheduling CHECK (
        status != 'scheduled' OR (scheduled_date IS NOT NULL AND scheduled_time IS NOT NULL)
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_follow_up_date CHECK (
        scheduled_date IS NULL OR scheduled_date >= CURRENT_DATE
    )
);

CREATE TABLE opd_queue_management (
    queue_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    visit_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    department_id UUID NOT NULL,
    doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    queue_number SMALLINT NOT NULL,
    queue_status queue_status_type DEFAULT 'waiting',
    wait_time_minutes INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN queue_status = 'completed' AND created_at IS NOT NULL AND completed_at IS NOT NULL
            THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (completed_at - created_at)) / 60
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    estimated_wait_time INTEGER,
    current_position SMALLINT,
    called_by UUID,
    called_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (visit_id) REFERENCES opd_visits(visit_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (department_id) REFERENCES departments(department_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (called_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_queue_timeline CHECK (
        created_at <= COALESCE(called_at, created_at) AND
        COALESCE(called_at, created_at) <= COALESCE(completed_at, created_at)
    )
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_appointments_date_doctor ON opd_appointments(appointment_date, doctor_id, appointment_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_appointments_patient ON opd_appointments(patient_id, appointment_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_appointments_status ON opd_appointments(appointment_status) WHERE appointment_status = 'scheduled';
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_appointments_reminder ON opd_appointments(appointment_date, reminder_sent) WHERE reminder_sent = false;

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_visits_date ON opd_visits(visit_date, visit_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_visits_patient ON opd_visits(patient_id, visit_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_visits_appointment ON opd_visits(appointment_id) WHERE appointment_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_visits_status ON opd_visits(visit_status) WHERE visit_status != 'completed';

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_vital_signs_visit ON opd_vital_signs(visit_id, captured_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_vital_signs_patient ON opd_vital_signs(patient_id, captured_at);

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_consultations_visit ON opd_consultations(visit_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_consultations_doctor ON opd_consultations(doctor_id, consultation_start_time);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_consultations_status ON opd_consultations(consultation_status) WHERE consultation_status = 'draft';

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_diagnoses_consultation ON opd_diagnoses(consultation_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_diagnoses_code ON opd_diagnoses(diagnosis_code);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_diagnoses_patient ON opd_diagnoses(patient_id, onset_date);

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_prescriptions_consultation ON opd_prescriptions(consultation_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_prescriptions_patient ON opd_prescriptions(patient_id, provided_at);

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_referrals_consultation ON opd_referrals(consultation_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_referrals_status ON opd_referrals(referral_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_referrals_doctor ON opd_referrals(referring_doctor_id, created_at);

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_follow_ups_consultation ON opd_follow_ups(consultation_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_follow_ups_patient ON opd_follow_ups(patient_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_follow_ups_status ON opd_follow_ups(status) WHERE status = 'pending';

CREATE INDEX idx_opd_queue_visit ON opd_queue_management(visit_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_queue_department ON opd_queue_management(department_id, queue_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_queue_status ON opd_queue_management(queue_status) WHERE queue_status = 'waiting';
CREATE INDEX idx_opd_queue_doctor ON opd_queue_management(doctor_id, queue_status);

-- Partitioning
CREATE TABLE opd_visits_y2024 PARTITION OF opd_visits
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

CREATE TABLE opd_appointments_y2024 PARTITION OF opd_appointments
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_visit_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update visit status based on consultation completion
    IF NEW.consultation_status = 'completed' AND OLD.consultation_status != 'completed' THEN
        UPDATE opd_visits
        SET visit_status = 'completed',
            completion_time = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE visit_id = NEW.visit_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_visit_status
    AFTER UPDATE ON opd_consultations
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_visit_status();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_appointment_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update appointment status when visit is created from appointment
    IF NEW.appointment_id IS NOT NULL AND NEW.visit_status = 'registered' THEN
        UPDATE opd_appointments
        SET appointment_status = 'checked_in'
        WHERE appointment_id = NEW.appointment_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_appointment_status
    AFTER INSERT ON opd_visits
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_appointment_status();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION manage_queue_on_visit()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Add to queue when visit is registered
    IF NEW.visit_status = 'registered' THEN
        INSERT INTO opd_queue_management (
            tenant_id, branch_id, visit_id, patient_id, 
            department_id, doctor_id, queue_number, current_position
        )
        SELECT 
            NEW.tenant_id, NEW.branch_id, NEW.visit_id, NEW.patient_id,
            a.department_id, a.doctor_id,
            (SELECT COALESCE(MAX(queue_number), 0) + 1 
             FROM opd_queue_management 
             WHERE department_id = a.department_id AND visit_date = CURRENT_DATE),
            (SELECT COUNT(*) + 1 
             FROM opd_queue_management 
             WHERE department_id = a.department_id AND queue_status = 'waiting')
        FROM opd_appointments a
        WHERE a.appointment_id = NEW.appointment_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_manage_queue_on_visit
    AFTER INSERT ON opd_visits
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION manage_queue_on_visit();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_doctor_availability()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    existing_appointments INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Check for overlapping appointments for the same doctor
    IF NEW.appointment_status = 'scheduled' THEN
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        INTO existing_appointments
        FROM opd_appointments
        WHERE doctor_id = NEW.doctor_id
        AND appointment_date = NEW.appointment_date
        AND appointment_time BETWEEN NEW.appointment_time - INTERVAL '15 minutes' 
                             AND NEW.appointment_time + INTERVAL '15 minutes'
        AND appointment_status IN ('scheduled', 'checked_in', 'in_consultation')
        AND appointment_id != NEW.appointment_id;
        
        IF existing_appointments > 0 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Doctor has overlapping appointments';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_doctor_availability
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON opd_appointments
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION check_doctor_availability();

-- RLS Policies
CREATE POLICY opd_tenant_isolation ON opd_appointments
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY visits_privacy ON opd_visits
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY consultations_access ON opd_consultations
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('doctor', 'nurse', 'system_admin'));

-- Views
CREATE VIEW current_opd_queue AS
SELECT 
    q.queue_id,
    q.visit_id,
    q.patient_id,
    p.first_name,
    p.last_name,
    q.department_id,
    d.department_name,
    q.doctor_id,
    doc.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    doc.last_name as doctor_last_name,
    q.queue_number,
    q.queue_status,
    q.current_position,
    q.wait_time_minutes,
    q.estimated_wait_time,
    v.visit_type,
    v.visit_status
FROM opd_queue_management q
JOIN opd_visits v ON q.visit_id = v.visit_id
JOIN patients p ON q.patient_id = p.patient_id
JOIN departments d ON q.department_id = d.department_id
JOIN doctors doc ON q.doctor_id = doc.doctor_id
WHERE q.queue_status IN ('waiting', 'in_progress')
ORDER BY q.department_id, q.queue_number;

CREATE VIEW doctor_appointment_schedule AS
SELECT 
    a.appointment_id,
    a.appointment_date,
    a.appointment_time,
    a.appointment_type,
    a.appointment_status,
    a.priority,
    p.patient_id,
    p.first_name,
    p.last_name,
    p.phone_number,
    a.reason_for_visit,
    d.department_name,
    doc.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    doc.last_name as doctor_last_name
FROM opd_appointments a
JOIN patients p ON a.patient_id = p.patient_id
JOIN departments d ON a.department_id = d.department_id
JOIN doctors doc ON a.doctor_id = doc.doctor_id
WHERE a.appointment_date >= CURRENT_DATE
ORDER BY a.appointment_date, a.appointment_time;

CREATE VIEW patient_visit_history AS
SELECT 
    v.visit_id,
    v.visit_date,
    v.visit_type,
    v.visit_status,
    d.department_name,
    doc.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    doc.last_name as doctor_last_name,
    c.consultation_start_time,
    c.consultation_end_time,
    c.consultation_status
FROM opd_visits v
LEFT JOIN opd_appointments a ON v.appointment_id = a.appointment_id
LEFT JOIN departments d ON a.department_id = d.department_id
LEFT JOIN doctors doc ON a.doctor_id = doc.doctor_id
LEFT JOIN opd_consultations c ON v.visit_id = c.visit_id
ORDER BY v.visit_date DESC, v.visit_time DESC;
```

This implementation includes:

1. Comprehensive clinical data model supporting SOAP notes format
2. Real-time queue management with automatic position tracking
3. Automated calculations for BMI, wait times, and consultation duration
4. Referral management with internal and external tracking
5. Follow-up scheduling with test review integration
6. Vital signs validation with medical range checks
7. Appointment conflict prevention through triggers
8. Partitioning for high-volume visit and appointment data
9. RLS policies for data security and tenant isolation
10. Useful views for operational monitoring and reporting

The schema supports efficient OPD operations with proper constraints for clinical data integrity, real-time patient flow management, and seamless integration with other hospital systems.
