---
## Domain 11: Clinical IPD (Inpatient Department)

Here's the PostgreSQL implementation for the Clinical IPD (Inpatient Department) domain:


```sql
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN phone_type VARCHAR(20);
CREATE DOMAIN email_type VARCHAR(255);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE admission_type_type AS ENUM (
    'emergency', 'elective', 'transfer'
);

CREATE TYPE admission_source_type AS ENUM (
    'opd', 'er', 'other_hospital', 'direct'
);

CREATE TYPE admission_status_type AS ENUM (
    'admitted', 'transferred', 'discharged', 'absconded'
);

CREATE TYPE ward_type_type AS ENUM (
    'general', 'specialty', 'icu', 'step_down', 'isolation'
);

CREATE TYPE bed_type_type AS ENUM (
    'regular', 'icu', 'pediatric', 'bariatric', 'isolation'
);

CREATE TYPE bed_status_type AS ENUM (
    'available', 'occupied', 'cleaning', 'maintenance'
);

CREATE TYPE assignment_status_type AS ENUM (
    'active', 'completed'
);

CREATE TYPE transfer_reason_type AS ENUM (
    'clinical', 'bed_availability', 'patient_request'
);

CREATE TYPE transfer_status_type AS ENUM (
    'requested', 'approved', 'completed', 'rejected'
);

CREATE TYPE discharge_type_type AS ENUM (
    'routine', 'ama', 'transfer', 'expired'
);

CREATE TYPE discharge_disposition_type AS ENUM (
    'home', 'rehab', 'other_facility', 'hospice'
);

CREATE TYPE discharge_status_type AS ENUM (
    'planned', 'completed', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE round_type_type AS ENUM (
    'morning', 'evening', 'specialty', 'multidisciplinary'
);

CREATE TYPE round_status_type AS ENUM (
    'completed', 'pending'
);

CREATE TYPE nursing_note_type_type AS ENUM (
    'assessment', 'progress', 'incident', 'handover'
);

CREATE TYPE nursing_note_status_type AS ENUM (
    'draft', 'completed'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE ipd_admissions (
    admission_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    admission_date DATE NOT NULL,
    admission_time TIME NOT NULL,
    admission_type admission_type_type NOT NULL,
    admission_source admission_source_type NOT NULL,
    referring_doctor_id UUID,
    admitting_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    primary_diagnosis VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    secondary_diagnoses TEXT[],
    admission_notes TEXT,
    expected_length_of_stay_days SMALLINT CHECK (expected_length_of_stay_days > 0),
    admission_status admission_status_type DEFAULT 'admitted',
    is_insurance_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    insurance_authorization_number VARCHAR(100),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (referring_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admitting_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_admission_date CHECK (
        admission_date <= CURRENT_DATE
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_wards (
    ward_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    ward_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    ward_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    ward_type ward_type_type NOT NULL,
    specialty UUID,
    total_beds SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (total_beds > 0),
    available_beds SMALLINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        total_beds - occupied_beds
    ) STORED,
    occupied_beds SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    nurse_station_phone phone_type,
    charge_nurse_id UUID,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (specialty) REFERENCES departments(department_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (charge_nurse_id) REFERENCES nurses(nurse_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_bed_counts CHECK (
        occupied_beds <= total_beds AND available_beds >= 0
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_beds (
    bed_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    ward_id UUID NOT NULL,
    bed_number VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    bed_type bed_type_type NOT NULL,
    equipment_available TEXT[],
    current_status bed_status_type DEFAULT 'available',
    is_isolated BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    isolation_type VARCHAR(100),
    last_cleaned_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    next_maintenance_date DATE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (ward_id) REFERENCES ipd_wards(ward_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_bed_ward_number UNIQUE (ward_id, bed_number),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_maintenance_date CHECK (
        next_maintenance_date IS NULL OR next_maintenance_date >= CURRENT_DATE
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_bed_assignments (
    assignment_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL,
    bed_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    assignment_start TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    assignment_end TIMESTAMPTZ,
    assigned_by UUID NOT NULL,
    assignment_reason TEXT,
    assignment_status assignment_status_type DEFAULT 'active',
    duration_hours INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN assignment_start IS NOT NULL AND assignment_end IS NOT NULL 
            THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (assignment_end - assignment_start)) / 3600
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES ipd_admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (bed_id) REFERENCES ipd_beds(bed_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (assigned_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_assignment_times CHECK (
        assignment_start <= COALESCE(assignment_end, assignment_start)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_transfers (
    transfer_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    from_bed_id UUID NOT NULL,
    to_bed_id UUID NOT NULL,
    from_ward_id UUID NOT NULL,
    to_ward_id UUID NOT NULL,
    transfer_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    transfer_reason transfer_reason_type NOT NULL,
    transfer_notes TEXT,
    initiated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    accepted_by UUID,
    transfer_status transfer_status_type DEFAULT 'requested',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES ipd_admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (from_bed_id) REFERENCES ipd_beds(bed_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (to_bed_id) REFERENCES ipd_beds(b极id),
    FOREIGN KEY (from_ward_id) REFERENCES ipd_wards(ward_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (to_ward_id) REFERENCES ipd_wards(ward_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (initiated_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (accepted极by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_different_beds CHECK (
        from_bed_id != to_bed_id
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_discharges (
    discharge_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID极NOT NULL,
    discharge_date DATE NOT NULL,
    discharge_time TIME NOT NULL,
    discharge_type discharge_type_type NOT NULL,
    discharge_disposition discharge_disposition_type NOT NULL,
    discharge_summary TEXT NOT NULL,
    follow_up_instructions TEXT,
    discharge_medications JSONB,
    discharging_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    discharge_status discharge_status_type DEFAULT 'planned',
    bed_cleared_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    final_bill_generated BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES ipd_admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (discharging_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_discharge_date CHECK (
        discharge_date >= (SELECT admission_date FROM ipd_admissions WHERE admission_id = ipd_discharges.admission_id)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_rounds (
    round_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    round_type round_type_type NOT NULL,
    round_datetime TIMEST极AMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    attending_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    participants UUID[],
    subjective TEXT,
    objective TEXT,
    assessment TEXT,
    plan TEXT,
    orders_given JSONB,
    round_status round_status_type DEFAULT 'completed',
    signed_by UUID,
    signed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES ipd_admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (attending_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (signed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_nursing_notes (
    nursing_note_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    note_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
极    note_type nursing_note_type_type NOT NULL,
    vital_sign极s JSONB,
    intake_output JSONB,
    pain_assessment JSONB,
    skin_assessment JSONB,
    nursing_interventions TEXT[],
    patient_response TEXT,
    nurse_id UUID NOT NULL,
    note_status nursing_note_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES ipd_admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (nurse_id) REFERENCES nurses(nurse_id)
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_length_of_stay (
    los_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_date DATE NOT NULL,
    discharge_date DATE,
    actual_length_of_stay_days INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN admission_date IS NOT NULL AND discharge_date IS NOT NULL 
            THEN discharge_date - admission_date
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    expected_length_of_stay_days INTEGER,
    variance_days INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        actual_length_of_stay_days - expected_length_of_stay_days
    ) STORED,
    ward_type ward_type_type,
    diagnosis_group VARCHAR(100),
    drg_code VARCHAR(10),
    is_planned_admission BOOLEAN,
    readmission_within_30_days BOOLEAN,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES ipd_admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_discharge_date_consistency CHECK (
        discharge_date IS NULL OR discharge_date >= admission_date
    )
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_admissions_status ON ipd_admissions(admission_status, admission_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_admissions_patient ON ipd_admissions(patient_id, admission_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_admissions_doctor ON ipd_admissions(admitting_doctor_id, admission_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_admissions_active ON ipd_admissions(admission_status) WHERE admission_status = 'admitted';

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_beds_ward ON ipd_beds(ward_id, current_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_beds_status ON ipd_beds(current_status) WHERE current_status = 'available';
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_beds_type ON ipd_beds(bed_type, current_status);

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_bed_assignments_active ON ipd_bed_assignments(assignment_status, assignment_start);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_bed_assignments_admission ON ipd_bed_assignments(admission_id, assignment_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_bed_assignments_bed ON ipd_bed_assignments(bed_id, assignment_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_bed_assignments_current ON ipd_bed_assignments(assignment_status) WHERE assignment_status = 'active';

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_transfers_admission ON ipd_transfers(admission_id, transfer_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_transfers_status ON ipd_transfers(transfer_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_transfers_ward ON ipd_transfers(from_ward_id, to_ward_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_discharges_admission ON ipd_discharges(admission_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_discharges_date ON ip极d_discharges(discharge_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_discharges_status ON ipd_discharges(discharge_status);

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_rounds_admission ON ipd_rounds(admission_id, round_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_rounds_doctor ON ipd_rounds(attending_doctor_id, round_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_rounds_date ON ipd_rounds(round_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_nursing_notes_admission ON ipd_nursing_notes(admission_id, note_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_nursing_notes_nurse ON ipd_nursing_notes(nurse_id, note_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_nursing_notes_type ON ipd_nursing_notes(note_type, note_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_los_admission ON ipd_length_of_stay(admission_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_los_diagnosis ON ipd_length_of_stay(diagnosis_group, actual_length_of_stay_days);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_los_ward ON ipd_length_of_stay(ward_type, actual_length_of_stay_days);

-- Partitioning
CREATE TABLE ipd_admissions_y2024 PARTITION OF ipd_admissions
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

CREATE TABLE ipd_nursing_notes_y2024 PARTITION OF ipd_nursing_notes
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_bed_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update bed status when assignment is created or ended
    IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' AND NEW.assignment_status = 'active' THEN
        UPDATE ipd_beds
        SET current_status = 'occupied'
        WHERE bed_id = NEW.bed_id;
        
        UPDATE ipd_wards
        SET occupied_beds = occupied_beds + 1
        WHERE ward_id = (SELECT ward_id FROM ipd_beds WHERE bed_id = NEW.bed_id);
        
    ELSIF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND NEW.assignment_status = 'completed' AND OLD.assignment_status = 'active' THEN
        UPDATE ipd_beds
        SET current_status = 'cleaning'
        WHERE bed_id = NEW.bed_id;
        
        UPDATE ipd_wards
        SET occupied_beds = occupied_beds - 1
        WHERE ward_id = (SELECT ward_id FROM ipd_beds WHERE bed_id = NEW.bed_id);
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_bed_status
    AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON ipd_bed_assignments
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_bed_status();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION complete_previous_assignment()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Complete previous active assignment when new one is created
    IF NEW.assignment_status = 'active' THEN
        UPDATE ipd_bed_assignments
        SET assignment_status = 'completed',
            assignment_end = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE admission_id = NEW.admission_id
        AND assignment_status = 'active'
        AND assignment_id != NEW.assignment_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_complete_previous_assignment
    BEFORE INSERT ON ip极d_bed_assignments
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION complete_previous_assignment();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_admission_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update admission status when discharge is completed
    IF NEW.discharge_status = 'completed' AND OLD.discharge_status != 'completed' THEN
        UPDATE ipd_admissions
        SET admission_status = 'discharged',
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE admission_id = NEW.admission_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_admission_status
    AFTER UPDATE ON ipd_discharges
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_admission_status();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION prevent_bed_reassignment()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    current_assignment UUID;
BEGIN
    -- Prevent assigning occupied bed
    IF NEW.assignment_status = 'active' THEN
        SELECT assignment_id INTO current_assignment
        FROM ipd_bed_assignments
        WHERE bed_id = NEW.bed_id
        AND assignment_status = 'active'
        AND assignment_id != NEW.assignment_id;
        
        IF current_assignment IS NOT NULL THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Bed is currently occupied';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_prevent_bed_reassignment
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON ipd_bed_assignments
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION prevent_bed_reassignment();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_los_on_discharge()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Create LOS record when discharge is completed
    IF NEW.discharge_status = 'completed' AND OLD.discharge_status != 'completed' THEN
        INSERT INTO ipd_length_of_stay (
            tenant_id, branch_id, admission_id, patient_id,
            admission_date, discharge_date, expected_length_of_stay_days,
            ward_type, diagnosis_group, drg_code, is_planned_admission
        )
        SELECT 
            a.tenant_id, a.branch_id, a.admission_id, a.patient_id,
            a.admission_date, NEW.discharge_date, a.expected_length_of_stay_days,
            w.ward_type, a.primary_diagnosis, NULL, a.admission_type = 'elective'
        FROM ipd_admissions a
        LEFT JOIN ipd_bed_assignments ba ON a.admission_id = ba.admission_id
        LEFT JOIN ipd_beds b ON ba.bed_id = b.bed_id
        LEFT JOIN ipd_wards w ON b.ward_id = w.ward_id
        WHERE a.admission_id = NEW.admission_id
        AND ba.assignment_status = 'active'
        LIMIT 1;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_los_on_discharge
    AFTER UPDATE ON ipd_discharges
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_los_on_discharge();

-- RLS Policies
CREATE POLICY ipd_tenant_isolation ON ipd_admissions
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY beds_ward_access ON ipd_beds
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('nurse', 'doctor', 'ward_clerk', 'system_admin'));

CREATE POLICY clinical_notes_privacy ON ipd_nursing_notes
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('nurse', 'doctor', 'system_admin'));

-- Views
CREATE VIEW current_bed_occupancy AS
SELECT 
    w.ward_id,
    w.ward_name,
    w.ward_type,
    w.total_beds,
    w.occupied_beds,
    w.available_beds,
    COUNT(b.bed_id) FILTER (WHERE b.current_status = 'available') as available_beds_count,
    COUNT(b.bed_id) FILTER (WHERE b.current_status = 'occupied') as occupied_beds_count,
    COUNT(b.bed_id) FILTER (WHERE b.current_status = 'cleaning') as cleaning_beds_count,
    COUNT(b.bed_id) FILTER (WHERE b.current_status = 'maintenance') as maintenance_beds_count
FROM ipd_wards w
LEFT JOIN ipd_beds b ON w.ward_id = b.ward_id
WHERE w.is_active = true
GROUP BY w.ward_id, w.ward_name, w.ward_type, w.total_beds, w.occupied_beds, w.available_beds;

CREATE VIEW patient_bed_assignments AS
SELECT 
    ba.assignment_id,
    ba.admission_id,
    a.admission_number,
    p.patient_id,
    p.first_name,
    p.last_name,
    b.bed_id,
    b.bed_number,
    w.ward_id,
    w.ward_name,
    ba.assignment_start,
    ba.assignment_end,
    ba.assignment_status,
    ba.duration_hours,
    a.admitting_doctor_id,
    doc.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    doc.last_name as doctor_last_name
FROM ipd_bed_assignments ba
JOIN ipd_admissions a ON ba.admission_id = a.admission_id
JOIN patients p ON ba.patient_id = p.patient_id
JOIN ipd_beds b ON ba.bed_id = b.bed_id
JOIN ipd_wards w ON b.ward_id = w.ward_id
JOIN doctors doc ON a.admitting_doctor_id = doc.doctor_id
WHERE ba.assignment_status = 'active'
ORDER BY w.ward_name, b.bed_number;

CREATE VIEW discharge_summary_view AS
SELECT 
    d.discharge_id,
    a.admission_id,
    a.admission_number,
    p.patient_id,
    p.first_name,
    p.last_name,
    a.admission_date,
    a.admission_time,
    d.discharge_date,
    d.discharge_time,
    d.discharge_type,
    d.discharge_disposition,
    d.discharge_summary,
    a.primary_diagnosis,
    a.secondary_diagnoses,
    d.discharging_doctor_id,
    doc.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    doc.last_name as doctor_last_name,
    los.actual_length_of_stay_days,
    los.expected_length_of_stay_days,
    los.variance_days
FROM ipd_discharges d
JOIN ipd_admissions a ON d.admission_id = a.admission_id
JOIN patients p ON d.patient_id = p.patient_id
JOIN doctors doc ON d.discharging_doctor_id = doc.doctor_id
LEFT JOIN ipd_length_of_stay los ON a.admission_id = los.admission_id
WHERE d.discharge_status = 'completed';
```

```sql
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN phone_type VARCHAR(20);
CREATE DOMAIN email_type VARCHAR(255);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE admission_type_type AS ENUM (
    'emergency', 'elective', 'transfer'
);

CREATE TYPE admission_source_type AS ENUM (
    'opd', 'er', 'other_hospital', 'direct'
);

CREATE TYPE admission_status_type AS ENUM (
    'admitted', 'transferred', 'discharged', 'absconded'
);

CREATE TYPE ward_type_type AS ENUM (
    'general', 'specialty', 'icu', 'step_down', 'isolation'
);

CREATE TYPE bed_type_type AS ENUM (
    'regular', 'icu', 'pediatric', 'bariatric', 'isolation'
);

CREATE TYPE bed_status_type AS ENUM (
    'available', 'occupied', 'cleaning', 'maintenance'
);

CREATE TYPE assignment_status_type AS ENUM (
    'active', 'completed'
);

CREATE TYPE transfer_reason_type AS ENUM (
    'clinical', 'bed_availability', 'patient_request'
);

CREATE TYPE transfer_status_type AS ENUM (
    'requested', 'approved', 'completed', 'rejected'
);

CREATE TYPE discharge_type_type AS ENUM (
    'routine', 'ama', 'transfer', 'expired'
);

CREATE TYPE discharge_disposition_type AS ENUM (
    'home', 'rehab', 'other_facility', 'hospice'
);

CREATE TYPE discharge_status_type AS ENUM (
    'planned', 'completed', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE round_type_type AS ENUM (
    'morning', 'evening', 'specialty', 'multidisciplinary'
);

CREATE TYPE round_status_type AS ENUM (
    'completed', 'pending'
);

CREATE TYPE nursing_note_type_type AS ENUM (
    'assessment', 'progress', 'incident', 'handover'
);

CREATE TYPE nursing_note_status_type AS ENUM (
    'draft', 'completed'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE ipd_admissions (
    admission_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    admission_date DATE NOT NULL,
    admission_time TIME NOT NULL,
    admission_type admission_type_type NOT NULL,
    admission_source admission_source_type NOT NULL,
    referring_doctor_id UUID,
    admitting_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    primary_diagnosis VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    secondary_diagnoses TEXT[],
    admission_notes TEXT,
    expected_length_of_stay_days SMALLINT CHECK (expected_length_of_stay_days > 0),
    admission_status admission_status_type DEFAULT 'admitted',
    is_insurance_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    insurance_authorization_number VARCHAR(100),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (referring_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admitting_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_admission_date CHECK (
        admission_date <= CURRENT_DATE
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_wards (
    ward_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    ward_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    ward_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    ward_type ward_type_type NOT NULL,
    specialty UUID,
    total_beds SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (total_beds > 0),
    available_beds SMALLINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        total_beds - occupied_beds
    ) STORED,
    occupied_beds SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    nurse_station_phone phone_type,
    charge_nurse_id UUID,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (specialty) REFERENCES departments(department_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (charge_nurse_id) REFERENCES nurses(nurse_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_bed_counts CHECK (
        occupied_beds <= total_beds AND available_beds >= 0
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_beds (
    bed_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    ward_id UUID NOT NULL,
    bed_number VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    bed_type bed_type_type NOT NULL,
    equipment_available TEXT[],
    current_status bed_status_type DEFAULT 'available',
    is_isolated BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    isolation_type VARCHAR(100),
    last_cleaned_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    next_maintenance_date DATE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (ward_id) REFERENCES ipd_wards(ward_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_bed_ward_number UNIQUE (ward_id, bed_number),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_maintenance_date CHECK (
        next_maintenance_date IS NULL OR next_maintenance_date >= CURRENT_DATE
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_bed_assignments (
    assignment_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL,
    bed_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    assignment_start TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    assignment_end TIMESTAMPTZ,
    assigned_by UUID NOT NULL,
    assignment_reason TEXT,
    assignment_status assignment_status_type DEFAULT 'active',
    duration_hours INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN assignment_start IS NOT NULL AND assignment_end IS NOT NULL 
            THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (assignment_end - assignment_start)) / 3600
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES ipd_admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (bed_id) REFERENCES ipd_beds(bed_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (assigned_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_assignment_times CHECK (
        assignment_start <= COALESCE(assignment_end, assignment_start)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_transfers (
    transfer_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    from_bed_id UUID NOT NULL,
    to_bed_id UUID NOT NULL,
    from_ward_id UUID NOT NULL,
    to_ward_id UUID NOT NULL,
    transfer_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    transfer_reason transfer_reason_type NOT NULL,
    transfer_notes TEXT,
    initiated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    accepted_by UUID,
    transfer_status transfer_status_type DEFAULT 'requested',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES ipd_admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (from_bed_id) REFERENCES ipd_beds(bed_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (to_bed_id) REFERENCES ipd_beds(b极id),
    FOREIGN KEY (from_ward_id) REFERENCES ipd_wards(ward_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (to_ward_id) REFERENCES ipd_wards(ward_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (initiated_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (accepted极by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_different_beds CHECK (
        from_bed_id != to_bed_id
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_discharges (
    discharge_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID极NOT NULL,
    discharge_date DATE NOT NULL,
    discharge_time TIME NOT NULL,
    discharge_type discharge_type_type NOT NULL,
    discharge_disposition discharge_disposition_type NOT NULL,
    discharge_summary TEXT NOT NULL,
    follow_up_instructions TEXT,
    discharge_medications JSONB,
    discharging_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    discharge_status discharge_status_type DEFAULT 'planned',
    bed_cleared_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    final_bill_generated BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES ipd_admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (discharging_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_discharge_date CHECK (
        discharge_date >= (SELECT admission_date FROM ipd_admissions WHERE admission_id = ipd_discharges.admission_id)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_rounds (
    round_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    round_type round_type_type NOT NULL,
    round_datetime TIMEST极AMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    attending_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    participants UUID[],
    subjective TEXT,
    objective TEXT,
    assessment TEXT,
    plan TEXT,
    orders_given JSONB,
    round_status round_status_type DEFAULT 'completed',
    signed_by UUID,
    signed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES ipd_admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (attending_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (signed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_nursing_notes (
    nursing_note_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    note_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
极    note_type nursing_note_type_type NOT NULL,
    vital_sign极s JSONB,
    intake_output JSONB,
    pain_assessment JSONB,
    skin_assessment JSONB,
    nursing_interventions TEXT[],
    patient_response TEXT,
    nurse_id UUID NOT NULL,
    note_status nursing_note_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES ipd_admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (nurse_id) REFERENCES nurses(nurse_id)
);

CREATE TABLE ipd_length_of_stay (
    los_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    admission_date DATE NOT NULL,
    discharge_date DATE,
    actual_length_of_stay_days INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN admission_date IS NOT NULL AND discharge_date IS NOT NULL 
            THEN discharge_date - admission_date
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    expected_length_of_stay_days INTEGER,
    variance_days INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        actual_length_of_stay_days - expected_length_of_stay_days
    ) STORED,
    ward_type ward_type_type,
    diagnosis_group VARCHAR(100),
    drg_code VARCHAR(10),
    is_planned_admission BOOLEAN,
    readmission_within_30_days BOOLEAN,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (admission_id) REFERENCES ipd_admissions(admission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_discharge_date_consistency CHECK (
        discharge_date IS NULL OR discharge_date >= admission_date
    )
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_admissions_status ON ipd_admissions(admission_status, admission_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_admissions_patient ON ipd_admissions(patient_id, admission_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_admissions_doctor ON ipd_admissions(admitting_doctor_id, admission_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_admissions_active ON ipd_admissions(admission_status) WHERE admission_status = 'admitted';

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_beds_ward ON ipd_beds(ward_id, current_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_beds_status ON ipd_beds(current_status) WHERE current_status = 'available';
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_beds_type ON ipd_beds(bed_type, current_status);

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_bed_assignments_active ON ipd_bed_assignments(assignment_status, assignment_start);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_bed_assignments_admission ON ipd_bed_assignments(admission_id, assignment_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_bed_assignments_bed ON ipd_bed_assignments(bed_id, assignment_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_bed_assignments_current ON ipd_bed_assignments(assignment_status) WHERE assignment_status = 'active';

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_transfers_admission ON ipd_transfers(admission_id, transfer_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_transfers_status ON ipd_transfers(transfer_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_transfers_ward ON ipd_transfers(from_ward_id, to_ward_id);

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_discharges_admission ON ipd_discharges(admission_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_discharges_date ON ip极d_discharges(discharge_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_discharges_status ON ipd_discharges(discharge_status);

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_rounds_admission ON ipd_rounds(admission_id, round_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_rounds_doctor ON ipd_rounds(attending_doctor_id, round_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_rounds_date ON ipd_rounds(round_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_nursing_notes_admission ON ipd_nursing_notes(admission_id, note_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_nursing_notes_nurse ON ipd_nursing_notes(nurse_id, note_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_nursing_notes_type ON ipd_nursing_notes(note_type, note_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_los_admission ON ipd_length_of_stay(admission_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_los_diagnosis ON ipd_length_of_stay(diagnosis_group, actual_length_of_stay_days);
CREATE INDEX idx_ipd_los_ward ON ipd_length_of_stay(ward_type, actual_length_of_stay_days);

-- Partitioning
CREATE TABLE ipd_admissions_y2024 PARTITION OF ipd_admissions
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

CREATE TABLE ipd_nursing_notes_y2024 PARTITION OF ipd_nursing_notes
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_bed_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update bed status when assignment is created or ended
    IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' AND NEW.assignment_status = 'active' THEN
        UPDATE ipd_beds
        SET current_status = 'occupied'
        WHERE bed_id = NEW.bed_id;
        
        UPDATE ipd_wards
        SET occupied_beds = occupied_beds + 1
        WHERE ward_id = (SELECT ward_id FROM ipd_beds WHERE bed_id = NEW.bed_id);
        
    ELSIF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND NEW.assignment_status = 'completed' AND OLD.assignment_status = 'active' THEN
        UPDATE ipd_beds
        SET current_status = 'cleaning'
        WHERE bed_id = NEW.bed_id;
        
        UPDATE ipd_wards
        SET occupied_beds = occupied_beds - 1
        WHERE ward_id = (SELECT ward_id FROM ipd_beds WHERE bed_id = NEW.bed_id);
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_bed_status
    AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON ipd_bed_assignments
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_bed_status();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION complete_previous_assignment()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Complete previous active assignment when new one is created
    IF NEW.assignment_status = 'active' THEN
        UPDATE ipd_bed_assignments
        SET assignment_status = 'completed',
            assignment_end = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE admission_id = NEW.admission_id
        AND assignment_status = 'active'
        AND assignment_id != NEW.assignment_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_complete_previous_assignment
    BEFORE INSERT ON ip极d_bed_assignments
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION complete_previous_assignment();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_admission_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update admission status when discharge is completed
    IF NEW.discharge_status = 'completed' AND OLD.discharge_status != 'completed' THEN
        UPDATE ipd_admissions
        SET admission_status = 'discharged',
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE admission_id = NEW.admission_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_admission_status
    AFTER UPDATE ON ipd_discharges
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_admission_status();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION prevent_bed_reassignment()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    current_assignment UUID;
BEGIN
    -- Prevent assigning occupied bed
    IF NEW.assignment_status = 'active' THEN
        SELECT assignment_id INTO current_assignment
        FROM ipd_bed_assignments
        WHERE bed_id = NEW.bed_id
        AND assignment_status = 'active'
        AND assignment_id != NEW.assignment_id;
        
        IF current_assignment IS NOT NULL THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Bed is currently occupied';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_prevent_bed_reassignment
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON ipd_bed_assignments
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION prevent_bed_reassignment();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_los_on_discharge()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Create LOS record when discharge is completed
    IF NEW.discharge_status = 'completed' AND OLD.discharge_status != 'completed' THEN
        INSERT INTO ipd_length_of_stay (
            tenant_id, branch_id, admission_id, patient_id,
            admission_date, discharge_date, expected_length_of_stay_days,
            ward_type, diagnosis_group, drg_code, is_planned_admission
        )
        SELECT 
            a.tenant_id, a.branch_id, a.admission_id, a.patient_id,
            a.admission_date, NEW.discharge_date, a.expected_length_of_stay_days,
            w.ward_type, a.primary_diagnosis, NULL, a.admission_type = 'elective'
        FROM ipd_admissions a
        LEFT JOIN ipd_bed_assignments ba ON a.admission_id = ba.admission_id
        LEFT JOIN ipd_beds b ON ba.bed_id = b.bed_id
        LEFT JOIN ipd_wards w ON b.ward_id = w.ward_id
        WHERE a.admission_id = NEW.admission_id
        AND ba.assignment_status = 'active'
        LIMIT 1;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_los_on_discharge
    AFTER UPDATE ON ipd_discharges
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_los_on_discharge();

-- RLS Policies
CREATE POLICY ipd_tenant_isolation ON ipd_admissions
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY beds_ward_access ON ipd_beds
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('nurse', 'doctor', 'ward_clerk', 'system_admin'));

CREATE POLICY clinical_notes_privacy ON ipd_nursing_notes
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('nurse', 'doctor', 'system_admin'));

-- Views
CREATE VIEW current_bed_occupancy AS
SELECT 
    w.ward_id,
    w.ward_name,
    w.ward_type,
    w.total_beds,
    w.occupied_beds,
    w.available_beds,
    COUNT(b.bed_id) FILTER (WHERE b.current_status = 'available') as available_beds_count,
    COUNT(b.bed_id) FILTER (WHERE b.current_status = 'occupied') as occupied_beds_count,
    COUNT(b.bed_id) FILTER (WHERE b.current_status = 'cleaning') as cleaning_beds_count,
    COUNT(b.bed_id) FILTER (WHERE b.current_status = 'maintenance') as maintenance_beds_count
FROM ipd_wards w
LEFT JOIN ipd_beds b ON w.ward_id = b.ward_id
WHERE w.is_active = true
GROUP BY w.ward_id, w.ward_name, w.ward_type, w.total_beds, w.occupied_beds, w.available_beds;

CREATE VIEW patient_bed_assignments AS
SELECT 
    ba.assignment_id,
    ba.admission_id,
    a.admission_number,
    p.patient_id,
    p.first_name,
    p.last_name,
    b.bed_id,
    b.bed_number,
    w.ward_id,
    w.ward_name,
    ba.assignment_start,
    ba.assignment_end,
    ba.assignment_status,
    ba.duration_hours,
    a.admitting_doctor_id,
    doc.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    doc.last_name as doctor_last_name
FROM ipd_bed_assignments ba
JOIN ipd_admissions a ON ba.admission_id = a.admission_id
JOIN patients p ON ba.patient_id = p.patient_id
JOIN ipd_beds b ON ba.bed_id = b.bed_id
JOIN ipd_wards w ON b.ward_id = w.ward_id
JOIN doctors doc ON a.admitting_doctor_id = doc.doctor_id
WHERE ba.assignment_status = 'active'
ORDER BY w.ward_name, b.bed_number;

CREATE VIEW discharge_summary_view AS
SELECT 
    d.discharge_id,
    a.admission_id,
    a.admission_number,
    p.patient_id,
    p.first_name,
    p.last_name,
    a.admission_date,
    a.admission_time,
    d.discharge_date,
    d.discharge_time,
    d.discharge_type,
    d.discharge_disposition,
    d.discharge_summary,
    a.primary_diagnosis,
    a.secondary_diagnoses,
    d.discharging_doctor_id,
    doc.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    doc.last_name as doctor_last_name,
    los.actual_length_of_stay_days,
    los.expected_length_of_stay_days,
    los.variance_days
FROM ipd_discharges d
JOIN ipd_admissions a ON d.admission_id = a.admission_id
JOIN patients p ON d.patient_id = p.patient_id
JOIN doctors doc ON d.discharging_doctor_id = doc.doctor_id
LEFT JOIN ipd_length_of_stay los ON a.admission_id = los.admission_id
WHERE d.discharge_status = 'completed';
```

This implementation includes:

1. Comprehensive inpatient management with admission, bed assignment, and discharge tracking
2. Real-time bed occupancy monitoring with automatic status updates
3. Clinical documentation for ward rounds and nursing notes
4. Transfer management with audit trail between wards/beds
5. Length of stay analytics with variance tracking
6. Automated status transitions through triggers
7. Bed availability enforcement with conflict prevention
8. Partitioning for high-volume admission and nursing note data
9. RLS policies for data security and tenant isolation
10. Useful views for operational monitoring and reporting

The schema supports efficient IPD operations with proper constraints for clinical data integrity, real-time bed management, and comprehensive patient flow tracking throughout the inpatient stay.
