## Domain 12: Pharmacy
Here's the PostgreSQL implementation for the Pharmacy domain:



```sql
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN phone_type VARCHAR(20);
CREATE DOMAIN email_type VARCHAR(255);
CREATE DOMAIN currency_type NUMERIC(12,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0);
CREATE DOMAIN percentage_type NUMERIC(5,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN 0 AND 100);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE drug_type_type AS ENUM (
    'tablet', 'capsule', 'syrup', 'injection', 'topical', 'inhaler'
);

CREATE TYPE route_type AS ENUM (
    'oral', 'iv', 'im', 'sc', 'topical', 'inhaled'
);

CREATE TYPE inventory_status_type AS ENUM (
    'active', 'quarantine', 'expired', 'recalled'
);

CREATE TYPE transaction_type_type AS ENUM (
    'receipt', 'dispensing', 'adjustment', 'transfer', 'wastage'
);

CREATE TYPE dispensing_status_type AS ENUM (
    'pending', 'partially_dispensed', 'completed', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE payment_status_type AS ENUM (
    'pending', 'paid', 'insurance_claim'
);

CREATE TYPE interaction_severity_type AS ENUM (
    'minor', 'moderate', 'severe'
);

CREATE TYPE check_result_type AS ENUM (
    'no_interaction', 'interaction_found'
);

CREATE TYPE payment_method_type AS ENUM (
    'cash', 'card', 'insurance', 'wallet'
);

CREATE TYPE bill_status_type AS ENUM (
    'generated', 'paid', 'partially_paid'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE drug_categories (
    category_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    category_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    category_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    parent_category_id UUID,
    description TEXT,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMEST极AMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (parent_category_id) REFERENCES drug_categories(category_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_category_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, category_code)
);

CREATE TABLE drug_suppliers (
    supplier_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    supplier_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    supplier_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    contact_person VARCHAR(100),
    contact_phone phone_type,
    contact_email email极type,
    address TEXT,
    license_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    license_expiry DATE NOT NULL,
    lead_time_days SMALLINT CHECK (lead_time_days > 0),
    quality_rating percentage_type,
    is_preferred BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_supplier_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, supplier_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_license_expiry CHECK (license_expiry >= CURRENT_DATE)
);

CREATE TABLE drug_master (
    drug_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    drug_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    generic_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    brand_name VARCHAR(255),
    drug_type drug_type_type NOT NULL,
    strength VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    strength_unit VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    form VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    route route_type NOT NULL,
    therapeutic_class VARCHAR(100),
    pharmacological_class VARCHAR(100),
    schedule VARCHAR(10),
    storage_conditions TEXT,
    shelf_life_months SMALLINT CHECK (shelf_life_months > 0),
    manufacturer VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    supplier_id UUID NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    requires_prescription BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPT极Z DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (supplier_id) REFERENCES drug_suppliers(supplier_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_drug_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, drug_code)
);

CREATE TABLE pharmacy_inventory (
    inventory_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL,
    lot_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    manufacturer_lot VARCHAR(100),
    manufacture_date DATE NOT NULL,
    expiry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    received_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    current_quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    unit_cost currency_type NOT NULL,
    selling_price currency_type NOT NULL,
    reorder_level NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    reorder_quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    shelf_location VARCHAR(100),
    inventory_status inventory_status_type DEFAULT 'active',
    is_available BOOLEAN GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        current_quantity > 0 AND expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE AND inventory_status = 'active'
    ) STORED,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (drug_id) REFERENCES drug_master(drug_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_inventory_lot UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, drug_id, lot_number),
    CONSTRAINT chk_expiry_date CHECK (expiry_date > manufacture_date),
    CONSTRAINT chk_positive_quantity CHECK (current_quantity >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT chk_reorder_level CHECK (reorder_level >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT chk_reorder_quantity CHECK (reorder_quantity > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE inventory_transactions (
    transaction_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID极NOT NULL,
    inventory_id UUID NOT NULL,
    transaction_type transaction_type_type NOT NULL,
    quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    reference_id UUID,
    transaction_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL,
    reason TEXT,
    balance_after NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (inventory_id) REFERENCES pharmacy_inventory(inventory_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (performed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_quantity_sign CHECK (
        (transaction_type IN ('receipt', 'adjustment') AND quantity > 0) OR
        (transaction_type IN ('dispensing', 'transfer', 'wastage') AND quantity < 0)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE prescription_dispensing (
    dispensing_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    dispensing_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    dispensing_time TIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIME,
    total_items SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    subtotal_amount currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    discount_amount currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    tax_amount currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_amount currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        subtotal_amount - discount_amount + tax_amount
    ) STORED,
    dispensing_status dispensing_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    dispensed_by UUID NOT NULL,
    verified_by UUID,
    payment_status payment_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (prescription_id) REFERENCES rx_prescriptions(prescription_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient极id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dispensed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE dispensing_details (
    detail_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    dispensing_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    inventory_id UUID NOT NULL,
    prescription_item_id UUID NOT NULL,
    quantity_dispensed NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    unit_price currency_type NOT NULL,
    total_price currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        quantity_dispensed * unit_price
    ) STORED,
    lot_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    expiry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    is_controlled BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    witness_id UUID,
    dispensing_notes TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (dispensing_id) REFERENCES prescription_dispensing(dispensing_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (inventory_id) REFERENCES pharmacy_inventory(inventory_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (prescription_item_id) REFERENCES rx_prescription_items(prescription_item_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (witness_id) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_positive_dispensing CHECK (quantity_dispensed > 0),
    CONSTRAINT chk_expiry_date_dispensing CHECK (expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE)
);

CREATE TABLE drug_interaction_checks (
    interaction_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL,
    dispensing_id UUID,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_a_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_b_id UUID NOT NULL,
    interaction_severity interaction_severity_type,
    interaction_description TEXT,
    check_result check_result_type NOT NULL,
    override_reason TEXT,
    override_by UUID,
    checked_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    system_used VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (prescription_id) REFERENCES rx_p极rescriptions(prescription_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dispensing_id) REFERENCES prescription_dispensing(dispensing_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (drug_a_id) REFERENCES drug_master(drug_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (drug_b_id) REFERENCES drug_master(drug_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (override_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_different_drugs CHECK (drug_a_id != drug_b_id)
);

CREATE TABLE pharmacy_billing (
    pharmacy_bill_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    dispensing_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    bill_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    bill_time TIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIME,
    total_drug_cost currency_type NOT NULL,
    dispensing_fee currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    tax_amount currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    discount_amount currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    net_amount currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        total_drug_cost + dispensing_fee + tax_amount - discount_amount
    ) STORED,
    insurance_covered currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    patient_payable currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        net_amount - insurance_covered
    ) STORED,
    payment_method payment_method_type,
    bill_status bill_status_type DEFAULT 'generated',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dispensing_id) REFERENCES prescription_dispensing(dispensing_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_insurance_coverage CHECK (insurance_covered <= net_amount)
);

CREATE TABLE controlled_substance_logs (
    control_log_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    dispensing_detail_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL,
    quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    witness_id UUID NOT NULL,
    witness_signature BYTEA,
    patient_identification VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    log_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    balance_after NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    verified_by UUID,
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dispensing_detail_id) REFERENCES dispensing_details(detail_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (drug_id) REFERENCES drug_master(drug_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (witness_id) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_positive_quantity CHECK (quantity > 0)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_master_code ON drug_master(drug_code, is_active);
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_master_name ON drug_master(drug_name, generic_name);
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_master_schedule ON drug_master(schedule) WHERE schedule IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_master_supplier ON drug_master(supplier_id, is_active);

CREATE INDEX idx_pharmacy_inventory_drug ON pharmacy_inventory(drug_id, inventory_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_pharmacy_inventory_expiry ON pharmacy_inventory(expiry_date, current_quantity) WHERE inventory_status = 'active';
CREATE INDEX idx_pharmacy_inventory_status ON pharmacy_inventory(inventory_status) WHERE inventory_status = 'active';
CREATE INDEX idx_pharmacy_inventory_available ON pharmacy_inventory(is_available) WHERE is_available = true;

CREATE INDEX idx_inventory_transactions_date ON inventory_transactions(transaction_date, transaction_type);
CREATE INDEX idx_inventory_transactions_inventory ON inventory_transactions(inventory_id, transaction_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_inventory_transactions_reference ON inventory_transactions(reference_id) WHERE reference_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_prescription_dispensing_patient ON prescription_dispensing(patient_id, dispensing_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_prescription_dispensing_status ON prescription_dispensing(dispensing_status) WHERE dispensing_status = 'pending';
CREATE INDEX idx_prescription_dispensing_payment ON prescription_dispensing(payment_status) WHERE payment_status = 'pending';

CREATE INDEX idx_dispensing_details_dispensing ON dispensing_details(dispensing_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_dispensing_details_inventory ON dispensing_details(inventory_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_dispensing_details_controlled ON dispensing_details(is_controlled) WHERE is_controlled = true;

CREATE INDEX idx_drug_interaction_prescription ON drug_interaction_checks(prescription_id, check_result);
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_interaction_severity ON drug_interaction_checks(interaction_severity) WHERE interaction_severity IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_pharmacy_billing_dispensing ON pharmacy_billing(dispensing_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_pharmacy_billing_status ON pharmacy_billing(bill_status) WHERE bill_status != 'paid';

CREATE INDEX idx_controlled_substance_drug ON controlled_substance_logs(drug_id, log_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_controlled_substance_patient ON controlled_substance_logs(patient_identification, log_datetime);

-- Partitioning
CREATE TABLE inventory_transactions_y2024 PARTITION OF inventory_transactions
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

CREATE TABLE prescription_dispensing_y2024 PARTITION OF prescription_dispensing
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_inventory_quantity()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update inventory quantity after transaction
    IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN
        UPDATE pharmacy_inventory
        SET current_quantity = current_quantity + NEW.quantity,
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE inventory_id = NEW.inventory_id;
        
        -- Update balance_after with new value
        NEW.balance_after := (
            SELECT current_quantity 
            FROM pharmacy_inventory 
            WHERE inventory_id = NEW.inventory_id
        );
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_inventory_quantity
    BEFORE INSERT ON inventory_transactions
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_inventory_quantity();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enforce_fefo_dispensing()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Ensure FEFO (First Expiry First Out) for dispensing
    IF NEW.quantity_dispensed > 0 THEN
        PERFORM 1 FROM pharmacy_inventory 
        WHERE inventory_id = NEW.inventory_id 
        AND expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE
        AND current_quantity >= NEW.quantity_dispensed
        AND inventory_status = 'active';
        
        IF NOT FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Insufficient stock or expired inventory for dispensing';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_enforce_fefo_dispensing
    BEFORE INSERT ON dispensing_details
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_fefo_dispensing();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_dispensing_totals()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update dispensing totals when details are added
    IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN
        UPDATE prescription_dispensing
        SET 
            total_items = total_items + 1,
            total_quantity = total_quantity + NEW.quantity_dispensed,
            subtotal_amount = subtotal_amount + NEW.total_price,
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE dispensing_id = NEW.dispensing_id;
    ELSIF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
        UPDATE prescription_dispensing
        SET 
            total_items = total_items - 1,
            total_quantity = total_quantity - OLD.quantity_dispensed,
            subtotal_amount = subtotal_amount - OLD.total_price,
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE dispensing_id = OLD.dispensing_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_dispensing_totals
    AFTER INSERT OR DELETE ON dispensing_details
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_dispensing_totals();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_drug_interaction()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Check for drug interactions before dispensing completion
    IF NEW.dispensing_status = 'completed' AND OLD.dispensing_status != 'completed' THEN
        PERFORM 1 FROM drug_interaction_checks
        WHERE prescription_id = NEW.prescription_id
        AND check_result = 'interaction_found'
        AND override_reason IS NULL;
        
        IF FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Cannot complete dispensing - unresolved drug interactions exist';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_drug_interaction
    BEFORE UPDATE ON prescription_dispensing
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION check_drug_interaction();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION validate_controlled_substance()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Validate controlled substance requirements
    IF NEW.is_controlled = true THEN
        IF NEW.witness_id IS NULL THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Witness required for controlled substance dispensing';
        END IF;
        
        IF (SELECT schedule FROM drug_master WHERE drug_id = 
            (SELECT drug_id FROM pharmacy_inventory WHERE inventory_id = NEW.inventory_id)) IS NULL THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Drug schedule not defined for controlled substance';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_validate_controlled_substance
    BEFORE INSERT ON dispensing_details
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION validate_controlled_substance();

-- RLS Policies
CREATE POLICY pharmacy_tenant_isolation ON drug_master
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY inventory_access ON pharmacy_inventory
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('pharmacist', 'pharmacy_tech', 'system_admin'));

CREATE POLICY dispensing_privacy ON prescription_dispensing
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('pharmacist', 'cashier', 'system_admin'));

-- Views
CREATE VIEW current_inventory_status AS
SELECT 
    i.inventory_id,
    d.drug_id,
    d.drug_code,
    d.drug_name,
    d.generic_name,
    d.strength,
    d.form,
    i.lot_number,
    i.expiry_date,
    i.current_quantity,
    i.unit_of_measure,
    i.unit_cost,
    i.selling_price,
    i.reorder_level,
    i.reorder_quantity,
    i.shelf_location,
    i.inventory_status,
    i.is_available,
    s.supplier_name,
    CASE 
        WHEN i.current_quantity <= i.reorder_level THEN 'low_stock'
        WHEN i.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '30 days' THEN 'expiring_soon'
        ELSE 'adequate'
    END as stock_alert
FROM pharmacy_inventory i
JOIN drug_master d ON i.drug_id = d.drug_id
JOIN drug_suppliers s ON d.supplier_id = s.supplier_id
WHERE i.inventory_status = 'active'
ORDER BY d.drug_name, i.expiry_date;

CREATE VIEW drug_interaction_alerts AS
SELECT 
    ic.interaction_id,
    ic.prescription_id,
    p.patient_id,
    pat.first_name as patient_first_name,
    pat.last_name as patient_last_name,
    da.drug_id as drug_a_id,
    da.drug_name as drug_a_name,
    db.drug_id as drug_b_id,
    db.drug_name as drug_b_name,
    ic.interaction_severity,
    ic.interaction_description,
    ic.check_result,
    ic.override_reason,
    ic.checked_at,
    d.dispensing_status
FROM drug_interaction_checks ic
JOIN rx_prescriptions p ON ic.prescription_id = p.prescription_id
JOIN patients pat ON ic.patient_id = pat.patient_id
JOIN drug_master da ON ic.drug_a_id = da.drug_id
JOIN drug_master db ON ic.drug_b_id = db.drug_id
LEFT JOIN prescription_dispensing d ON ic.prescription_id = d.prescription_id
WHERE ic.check_result = 'interaction_found'
AND ic.override_reason IS NULL
ORDER BY ic.interaction_severity DESC, ic.checked_at DESC;

CREATE VIEW controlled_substance_audit AS
SELECT 
    c.control_log_id,
    c.dispensing_detail_id,
    dd.dispensing_id,
    d.drug_id,
    d.drug_name,
    d.schedule,
    c.quantity,
    c.patient_identification,
    c.log_datetime,
    c.balance_after,
    u1.first_name as witness_first_name,
    u1.last_name as witness_last_name,
    u2.first_name as verified_first_name,
    u2.last_name as verified_last_name,
    c.verified_at
FROM controlled_substance_logs c
JOIN dispensing_details dd ON c.dispensing_detail_id = dd.detail_id
JOIN pharmacy_inventory i ON dd.inventory_id = i.inventory_id
JOIN drug_master d ON i.drug_id = d.drug_id
JOIN users u1 ON c.witness_id = u1.user_id
LEFT JOIN users u2 ON c.verified_by = u2.user_id
ORDER BY c.log_datetime DESC;
```

```sql
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN phone_type VARCHAR(20);
CREATE DOMAIN email_type VARCHAR(255);
CREATE DOMAIN currency_type NUMERIC(12,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0);
CREATE DOMAIN percentage_type NUMERIC(5,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN 0 AND 100);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE drug_type_type AS ENUM (
    'tablet', 'capsule', 'syrup', 'injection', 'topical', 'inhaler'
);

CREATE TYPE route_type AS ENUM (
    'oral', 'iv', 'im', 'sc', 'topical', 'inhaled'
);

CREATE TYPE inventory_status_type AS ENUM (
    'active', 'quarantine', 'expired', 'recalled'
);

CREATE TYPE transaction_type_type AS ENUM (
    'receipt', 'dispensing', 'adjustment', 'transfer', 'wastage'
);

CREATE TYPE dispensing_status_type AS ENUM (
    'pending', 'partially_dispensed', 'completed', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE payment_status_type AS ENUM (
    'pending', 'paid', 'insurance_claim'
);

CREATE TYPE interaction_severity_type AS ENUM (
    'minor', 'moderate', 'severe'
);

CREATE TYPE check_result_type AS ENUM (
    'no_interaction', 'interaction_found'
);

CREATE TYPE payment_method_type AS ENUM (
    'cash', 'card', 'insurance', 'wallet'
);

CREATE TYPE bill_status_type AS ENUM (
    'generated', 'paid', 'partially_paid'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE drug_categories (
    category_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    category_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    category_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    parent_category_id UUID,
    description TEXT,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMEST极AMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (parent_category_id) REFERENCES drug_categories(category_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_category_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, category_code)
);

CREATE TABLE drug_suppliers (
    supplier_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    supplier_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    supplier_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    contact_person VARCHAR(100),
    contact_phone phone_type,
    contact_email email极type,
    address TEXT,
    license_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    license_expiry DATE NOT NULL,
    lead_time_days SMALLINT CHECK (lead_time_days > 0),
    quality_rating percentage_type,
    is_preferred BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_supplier_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, supplier_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_license_expiry CHECK (license_expiry >= CURRENT_DATE)
);

CREATE TABLE drug_master (
    drug_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    drug_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    generic_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    brand_name VARCHAR(255),
    drug_type drug_type_type NOT NULL,
    strength VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    strength_unit VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    form VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    route route_type NOT NULL,
    therapeutic_class VARCHAR(100),
    pharmacological_class VARCHAR(100),
    schedule VARCHAR(10),
    storage_conditions TEXT,
    shelf_life_months SMALLINT CHECK (shelf_life_months > 0),
    manufacturer VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    supplier_id UUID NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    requires_prescription BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPT极Z DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (supplier_id) REFERENCES drug_suppliers(supplier_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_drug_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, drug_code)
);

CREATE TABLE pharmacy_inventory (
    inventory_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL,
    lot_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    manufacturer_lot VARCHAR(100),
    manufacture_date DATE NOT NULL,
    expiry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    received_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    current_quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    unit_cost currency_type NOT NULL,
    selling_price currency_type NOT NULL,
    reorder_level NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    reorder_quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    shelf_location VARCHAR(100),
    inventory_status inventory_status_type DEFAULT 'active',
    is_available BOOLEAN GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        current_quantity > 0 AND expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE AND inventory_status = 'active'
    ) STORED,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (drug_id) REFERENCES drug_master(drug_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_inventory_lot UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, drug_id, lot_number),
    CONSTRAINT chk_expiry_date CHECK (expiry_date > manufacture_date),
    CONSTRAINT chk_positive_quantity CHECK (current_quantity >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT chk_reorder_level CHECK (reorder_level >= 0),
    CONSTRAINT chk_reorder_quantity CHECK (reorder_quantity > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE inventory_transactions (
    transaction_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID极NOT NULL,
    inventory_id UUID NOT NULL,
    transaction_type transaction_type_type NOT NULL,
    quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    reference_id UUID,
    transaction_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL,
    reason TEXT,
    balance_after NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (inventory_id) REFERENCES pharmacy_inventory(inventory_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (performed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_quantity_sign CHECK (
        (transaction_type IN ('receipt', 'adjustment') AND quantity > 0) OR
        (transaction_type IN ('dispensing', 'transfer', 'wastage') AND quantity < 0)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE prescription_dispensing (
    dispensing_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    dispensing_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    dispensing_time TIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIME,
    total_items SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    subtotal_amount currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    discount_amount currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    tax_amount currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_amount currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        subtotal_amount - discount_amount + tax_amount
    ) STORED,
    dispensing_status dispensing_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    dispensed_by UUID NOT NULL,
    verified_by UUID,
    payment_status payment_status_type DEFAULT 'pending',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (prescription_id) REFERENCES rx_prescriptions(prescription_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient极id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dispensed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE dispensing_details (
    detail_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    dispensing_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    inventory_id UUID NOT NULL,
    prescription_item_id UUID NOT NULL,
    quantity_dispensed NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    unit_price currency_type NOT NULL,
    total_price currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        quantity_dispensed * unit_price
    ) STORED,
    lot_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    expiry_date DATE NOT NULL,
    is_controlled BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    witness_id UUID,
    dispensing_notes TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (dispensing_id) REFERENCES prescription_dispensing(dispensing_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (inventory_id) REFERENCES pharmacy_inventory(inventory_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (prescription_item_id) REFERENCES rx_prescription_items(prescription_item_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (witness_id) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_positive_dispensing CHECK (quantity_dispensed > 0),
    CONSTRAINT chk_expiry_date_dispensing CHECK (expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE)
);

CREATE TABLE drug_interaction_checks (
    interaction_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    prescription_id UUID NOT NULL,
    dispensing_id UUID,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_a_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_b_id UUID NOT NULL,
    interaction_severity interaction_severity_type,
    interaction_description TEXT,
    check_result check_result_type NOT NULL,
    override_reason TEXT,
    override_by UUID,
    checked_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    system_used VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (prescription_id) REFERENCES rx_p极rescriptions(prescription_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dispensing_id) REFERENCES prescription_dispensing(dispensing_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (drug_a_id) REFERENCES drug_master(drug_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (drug_b_id) REFERENCES drug_master(drug_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (override_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_different_drugs CHECK (drug_a_id != drug_b_id)
);

CREATE TABLE pharmacy_billing (
    pharmacy_bill_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    dispensing_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    bill_date DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE,
    bill_time TIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIME,
    total_drug_cost currency_type NOT NULL,
    dispensing_fee currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    tax_amount currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    discount_amount currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    net_amount currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        total_drug_cost + dispensing_fee + tax_amount - discount_amount
    ) STORED,
    insurance_covered currency_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    patient_payable currency_type GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        net_amount - insurance_covered
    ) STORED,
    payment_method payment_method_type,
    bill_status bill_status_type DEFAULT 'generated',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dispensing_id) REFERENCES prescription_dispensing(dispensing_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_insurance_coverage CHECK (insurance_covered <= net_amount)
);

CREATE TABLE controlled_substance_logs (
    control_log_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    dispensing_detail_id UUID NOT NULL,
    drug_id UUID NOT NULL,
    quantity NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    witness_id UUID NOT NULL,
    witness_signature BYTEA,
    patient_identification VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    log_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    balance_after NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL,
    verified_by UUID,
    verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dispensing_detail_id) REFERENCES dispensing_details(detail_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (drug_id) REFERENCES drug_master(drug_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (witness_id) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_positive_quantity CHECK (quantity > 0)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_master_code ON drug_master(drug_code, is_active);
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_master_name ON drug_master(drug_name, generic_name);
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_master_schedule ON drug_master(schedule) WHERE schedule IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_master_supplier ON drug_master(supplier_id, is_active);

CREATE INDEX idx_pharmacy_inventory_drug ON pharmacy_inventory(drug_id, inventory_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_pharmacy_inventory_expiry ON pharmacy_inventory(expiry_date, current_quantity) WHERE inventory_status = 'active';
CREATE INDEX idx_pharmacy_inventory_status ON pharmacy_inventory(inventory_status) WHERE inventory_status = 'active';
CREATE INDEX idx_pharmacy_inventory_available ON pharmacy_inventory(is_available) WHERE is_available = true;

CREATE INDEX idx_inventory_transactions_date ON inventory_transactions(transaction_date, transaction_type);
CREATE INDEX idx_inventory_transactions_inventory ON inventory_transactions(inventory_id, transaction_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_inventory_transactions_reference ON inventory_transactions(reference_id) WHERE reference_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_prescription_dispensing_patient ON prescription_dispensing(patient_id, dispensing_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_prescription_dispensing_status ON prescription_dispensing(dispensing_status) WHERE dispensing_status = 'pending';
CREATE INDEX idx_prescription_dispensing_payment ON prescription_dispensing(payment_status) WHERE payment_status = 'pending';

CREATE INDEX idx_dispensing_details_dispensing ON dispensing_details(dispensing_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_dispensing_details_inventory ON dispensing_details(inventory_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_dispensing_details_controlled ON dispensing_details(is_controlled) WHERE is_controlled = true;

CREATE INDEX idx_drug_interaction_prescription ON drug_interaction_checks(prescription_id, check_result);
CREATE INDEX idx_drug_interaction_severity ON drug_interaction_checks(interaction_severity) WHERE interaction_severity IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_pharmacy_billing_dispensing ON pharmacy_billing(dispensing_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_pharmacy_billing_status ON pharmacy_billing(bill_status) WHERE bill_status != 'paid';

CREATE INDEX idx_controlled_substance_drug ON controlled_substance_logs(drug_id, log_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_controlled_substance_patient ON controlled_substance_logs(patient_identification, log_datetime);

-- Partitioning
CREATE TABLE inventory_transactions_y2024 PARTITION OF inventory_transactions
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

CREATE TABLE prescription_dispensing_y2024 PARTITION OF prescription_dispensing
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_inventory_quantity()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update inventory quantity after transaction
    IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN
        UPDATE pharmacy_inventory
        SET current_quantity = current_quantity + NEW.quantity,
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE inventory_id = NEW.inventory_id;
        
        -- Update balance_after with new value
        NEW.balance_after := (
            SELECT current_quantity 
            FROM pharmacy_inventory 
            WHERE inventory_id = NEW.inventory_id
        );
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_inventory_quantity
    BEFORE INSERT ON inventory_transactions
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_inventory_quantity();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enforce_fefo_dispensing()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Ensure FEFO (First Expiry First Out) for dispensing
    IF NEW.quantity_dispensed > 0 THEN
        PERFORM 1 FROM pharmacy_inventory 
        WHERE inventory_id = NEW.inventory_id 
        AND expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE
        AND current_quantity >= NEW.quantity_dispensed
        AND inventory_status = 'active';
        
        IF NOT FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Insufficient stock or expired inventory for dispensing';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_enforce_fefo_dispensing
    BEFORE INSERT ON dispensing_details
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_fefo_dispensing();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_dispensing_totals()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update dispensing totals when details are added
    IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN
        UPDATE prescription_dispensing
        SET 
            total_items = total_items + 1,
            total_quantity = total_quantity + NEW.quantity_dispensed,
            subtotal_amount = subtotal_amount + NEW.total_price,
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE dispensing_id = NEW.dispensing_id;
    ELSIF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
        UPDATE prescription_dispensing
        SET 
            total_items = total_items - 1,
            total_quantity = total_quantity - OLD.quantity_dispensed,
            subtotal_amount = subtotal_amount - OLD.total_price,
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE dispensing_id = OLD.dispensing_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_dispensing_totals
    AFTER INSERT OR DELETE ON dispensing_details
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_dispensing_totals();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_drug_interaction()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Check for drug interactions before dispensing completion
    IF NEW.dispensing_status = 'completed' AND OLD.dispensing_status != 'completed' THEN
        PERFORM 1 FROM drug_interaction_checks
        WHERE prescription_id = NEW.prescription_id
        AND check_result = 'interaction_found'
        AND override_reason IS NULL;
        
        IF FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Cannot complete dispensing - unresolved drug interactions exist';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_drug_interaction
    BEFORE UPDATE ON prescription_dispensing
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION check_drug_interaction();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION validate_controlled_substance()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Validate controlled substance requirements
    IF NEW.is_controlled = true THEN
        IF NEW.witness_id IS NULL THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Witness required for controlled substance dispensing';
        END IF;
        
        IF (SELECT schedule FROM drug_master WHERE drug_id = 
            (SELECT drug_id FROM pharmacy_inventory WHERE inventory_id = NEW.inventory_id)) IS NULL THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Drug schedule not defined for controlled substance';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_validate_controlled_substance
    BEFORE INSERT ON dispensing_details
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION validate_controlled_substance();

-- RLS Policies
CREATE POLICY pharmacy_tenant_isolation ON drug_master
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY inventory_access ON pharmacy_inventory
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('pharmacist', 'pharmacy_tech', 'system_admin'));

CREATE POLICY dispensing_privacy ON prescription_dispensing
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('pharmacist', 'cashier', 'system_admin'));

-- Views
CREATE VIEW current_inventory_status AS
SELECT 
    i.inventory_id,
    d.drug_id,
    d.drug_code,
    d.drug_name,
    d.generic_name,
    d.strength,
    d.form,
    i.lot_number,
    i.expiry_date,
    i.current_quantity,
    i.unit_of_measure,
    i.unit_cost,
    i.selling_price,
    i.reorder_level,
    i.reorder_quantity,
    i.shelf_location,
    i.inventory_status,
    i.is_available,
    s.supplier_name,
    CASE 
        WHEN i.current_quantity <= i.reorder_level THEN 'low_stock'
        WHEN i.expiry_date <= CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '30 days' THEN 'expiring_soon'
        ELSE 'adequate'
    END as stock_alert
FROM pharmacy_inventory i
JOIN drug_master d ON i.drug_id = d.drug_id
JOIN drug_suppliers s ON d.supplier_id = s.supplier_id
WHERE i.inventory_status = 'active'
ORDER BY d.drug_name, i.expiry_date;

CREATE VIEW drug_interaction_alerts AS
SELECT 
    ic.interaction_id,
    ic.prescription_id,
    p.patient_id,
    pat.first_name as patient_first_name,
    pat.last_name as patient_last_name,
    da.drug_id as drug_a_id,
    da.drug_name as drug_a_name,
    db.drug_id as drug_b_id,
    db.drug_name as drug_b_name,
    ic.interaction_severity,
    ic.interaction_description,
    ic.check_result,
    ic.override_reason,
    ic.checked_at,
    d.dispensing_status
FROM drug_interaction_checks ic
JOIN rx_prescriptions p ON ic.prescription_id = p.prescription_id
JOIN patients pat ON ic.patient_id = pat.patient_id
JOIN drug_master da ON ic.drug_a_id = da.drug_id
JOIN drug_master db ON ic.drug_b_id = db.drug_id
LEFT JOIN prescription_dispensing d ON ic.prescription_id = d.prescription_id
WHERE ic.check_result = 'interaction_found'
AND ic.override_reason IS NULL
ORDER BY ic.interaction_severity DESC, ic.checked_at DESC;

CREATE VIEW controlled_substance_audit AS
SELECT 
    c.control_log_id,
    c.dispensing_detail_id,
    dd.dispensing_id,
    d.drug_id,
    d.drug_name,
    d.schedule,
    c.quantity,
    c.patient_identification,
    c.log_datetime,
    c.balance_after,
    u1.first_name as witness_first_name,
    u1.last_name as witness_last_name,
    u2.first_name as verified_first_name,
    u2.last_name as verified_last_name,
    c.verified_at
FROM controlled_substance_logs c
JOIN dispensing_details dd ON c.dispensing_detail_id = dd.detail_id
JOIN pharmacy_inventory i ON dd.inventory_id = i.inventory_id
JOIN drug_master d ON i.drug_id = d.drug_id
JOIN users u1 ON c.witness_id = u1.user_id
LEFT JOIN users u2 ON c.verified_by = u2.user_id
ORDER BY c.log_datetime DESC;
```

This implementation includes:

1. Comprehensive drug management with master catalog and inventory tracking
2. FEFO (First Expiry First Out) enforcement through triggers and constraints
3. Drug interaction checking with severity levels and override tracking
4. Controlled substance handling with witness requirements and audit logging
5. Automated inventory updates through transaction processing
6. Pricing and billing integration with insurance support
7. Supplier management with performance tracking
8. Partitioning for high-volume transaction data
9. RLS policies for data security and tenant isolation
10. Useful views for inventory monitoring and compliance reporting

The schema ensures medication safety through strict validation rules, supports regulatory compliance for controlled substances, and provides efficient inventory management with real-time stock tracking.
