## Domain 13: Laboratory (LIS)

Here's the PostgreSQL implementation for the Laboratory (LIS) domain:


```sql
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN currency_type NUMERIC(12,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0);
CREATE DOMAIN percentage_type NUMERIC(5,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN 0 AND 100);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE test_type_type AS ENUM (
    'chemistry', 'hematology', 'microbiology', 'immunology', 'molecular'
);

CREATE TYPE order_priority_type AS ENUM (
    'routine', 'urgent', 'stat'
);

CREATE TYPE order_status_type AS ENUM (
    'ordered', 'collected', 'received', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE order_location_type AS ENUM (
    'opd', 'ipd', 'er', 'external'
);

CREATE TYPE specimen_status_type AS ENUM (
    'collected', 'in_transit', 'received', 'processing', 'stored', 'discarded'
);

CREATE TYPE aliquot_status_type AS ENUM (
    'available', 'used', 'discarded'
);

CREATE TYPE result_type_type AS ENUM (
    'numeric', 'text', 'image', 'file'
);

CREATE TYPE result_status_type AS ENUM (
    'preliminary', 'final', 'corrected', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE qc_status_type AS ENUM (
    'in_control', 'out_of_control'
);

CREATE TYPE instrument_status_type AS ENUM (
    'operational', 'maintenance', 'out_of_service'
);

CREATE TYPE notification_method_type AS ENUM (
    'phone', 'message', 'system'
);

CREATE TYPE report_type_type AS ENUM (
    'interim', 'final', 'amended'
);

CREATE TYPE report_status_type AS ENUM (
    'draft', 'released', 'archived'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE lab_test_master (
    test_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID极NOT NULL,
    test_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    test_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    test_type test_type_type NOT NULL,
    specimen_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    container_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    volume_required_ml NUMERIC(6,2),
    storage_conditions TEXT,
    stability_hours SMALLINT,
    methodology VARCHAR(100),
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(30),
    reference_range_low NUMERIC(10,2),
    reference_range_high NUMERIC极(10,2),
    reference_range_text TEXT,
    tat_target_minutes INTEGER CHECK (tat_target_minutes > 0),
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    requires_special_handling BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_test_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, test_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_reference_range CHECK (
        (reference_range_low IS NULL AND reference_range_high IS NULL) OR
        (reference_range_low IS NOT NULL AND reference_range_high IS NOT NULL AND reference_range_low < reference_range_high)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE lab_test_panels (
    panel_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    panel_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    panel_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    panel_description TEXT,
    test_ids UUID[] NOT NULL,
    panel_price currency_type NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_panel_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch极_id, panel_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_test_ids CHECK (array_length(test_ids, 1) > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_orders (
    order_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
极    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    requesting_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    ordering_department UUID NOT NULL,
    order_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    order_priority order_priority_type DEFAULT 'routine',
    clinical_notes TEXT,
    diagnosis_codes TEXT[],
    order_status order_status_type DEFAULT 'ordered',
    order_location order_location_type NOT NULL,
    is_insurance_approved BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    authorization_number VARCHAR(100),
    created_at TIMESTAM极PTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (requesting_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (ordering_department) REFERENCES departments(department_id)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_order_items (
    order_item_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    order_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    test_id UUID NOT NULL,
    test_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    test_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    priority order_priority_type NOT NULL,
    status order_status_type DEFAULT 'ordered',
    result_entered_by UUID,
    result_entered_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    result_verified_by UUID,
    result_verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    tat_actual_minutes INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN result_entered_at IS NOT NULL AND order_datetime IS NOT NULL
            THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (result_entered_at - order_datetime)) / 60
            ELSE NULL
        END
    ) STORED,
    tat_status SMALLINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN tat_actual_minutes IS NOT NULL AND 
                 (SELECT tat_target_minutes FROM lab_test_master WHERE test_id = lab_order_items.test_id) IS NOT NULL
            THEN CASE 
                WHEN tat_actual_minutes <= (SELECT tat_target_minutes FROM lab_test_master WHERE test_id = lab_order_items.test_id)
                THEN 1  -- On Time
                ELSE 2  -- Delayed
            END
            ELSE NULL
        END
    ) STORED,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPT极Z DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES lab_orders(order_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (test_id) REFERENCES lab_test_master(test_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (result_entered_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (result_verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_specimens (
    specimen_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_item_id UUID NOT NULL,
    specimen_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    specimen_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    container_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    collection_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    collected_by UUID NOT NULL,
    collection_site VARCHAR(100),
    collection_notes TEXT,
    received_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    received_by UUID,
    volume_ml NUMERIC(6,2),
    appearance VARCHAR(100),
    specimen_status specimen_status_type DEFAULT 'collected',
    storage_location VARCHAR(100),
    discard_dat极etime TIMESTAMPT极Z,
    discard_reason TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (order_item_id) REFERENCES lab_order_items(order_item_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (collected_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (received_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_volume CHECK (volume_ml IS NULL OR volume_ml > 0),
    CONSTRAINT chk_received_time CHECK (
        received_datetime IS NULL OR received_datetime >= collection_datetime
    )
);

CREATE TABLE lab_specimen_aliquots (
    aliquot_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    specimen_id UUID NOT NULL,
    aliquot_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    aliquot_volume_ml NUMERIC(6,2) NOT NULL,
    created_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID NOT NULL,
    storage_location VARCHAR(100),
    used_for_test UUID,
    used_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    aliquot_status aliquot_status_type DEFAULT 'available',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (specimen_id) REFERENCES lab_specimens(specimen_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (used_for_test) REFERENCES lab_test_master(test_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_aliquot_number UNIQUE (specimen_id, aliquot_number),
    CONSTRAINT chk_aliquot_volume CHECK (aliquot_volume_ml > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_results (
    result_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_item_id UUID NOT NULL,
    test_id UUID NOT NULL,
    specimen_id UUID NOT NULL,
    result_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    result_type result_type_type NOT NULL,
    result_status result_status_type DEFAULT 'preliminary',
    verified_by UUID,
    verified_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    instrument_id UUID,
    reagent_lot_id UUID,
    qc_run_id UUID,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (order_item_id) REFERENCES lab_order_items(order_item_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (test极id) REFERENCES lab_test_master(test_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (specimen_id) REFERENCES lab_specimens(specimen_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (instrument_id) REFERENCES lab_instruments(instrument_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (qc_run_id) REFERENCES lab_qc_runs(qc_run_id)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_result_details (
    result_detail_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    result_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    parameter_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    numeric_value NUMERIC(15,6),
    text_value TEXT,
    unit VARCHAR(30),
    reference_range_low NUMERIC(10,2),
    reference_range_high NUMERIC(10,2),
    flags VARCHAR(10),
    is_critical BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    comments TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (result_id) REFERENCES lab_results(result_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_result_value CHECK (
        numeric_value IS NOT NULL OR text_value IS NOT NULL
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_reference_range CHECK (
        (reference_range_low IS NULL AND reference_range_high IS NULL) OR
        (reference_range_low IS NOT NULL AND reference_range_high IS NOT NULL AND reference_range_low < reference_range_high)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE lab_qc_runs (
    q极c_run_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    test_id UUID NOT NULL,
    instrument_id UUID NOT NULL,
    qc_material_lot VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    run_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    expected_value NUMERIC(15,6) NOT NULL,
    measured_value NUMERIC(15,6) NOT NULL,
    sd_value NUMERIC(15,6),
    cv_percent percentage_type,
    westgard_rules JSONB,
    qc_status qc_status_type NOT NULL,
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL,
    reviewed_by UUID,
    reviewed_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (test_id) REFERENCES lab_test_master(test_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (instrument_id) REFERENCES lab_instruments(instrument_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (performed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (reviewed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_instruments (
    instrument_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    instrument_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    instrument_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    manufacturer VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    model VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    serial_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    installation_date DATE NOT NULL,
    last_calibration_date DATE,
    next_calibration_date DATE,
    status instrument_status_type DEFAULT 'operational',
    interface_type VARCHAR(100),
    is_connected BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_instrument_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, instrument_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_calibration_dates CHECK (
        last_calibration_date IS NULL OR next_calibration_date IS NULL OR 
        next_calibration_date > last_calibration_date
    )
);

CREATE TABLE lab_panic_values (
    panic_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    result_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_item_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    test_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    result_value VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    reference_range VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    notified_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    notified_to UUID NOT NULL,
    notification_method notification_method_type NOT NULL,
    acknowledgment_received BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    acknowledgment_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    acknowledgment_by UUID,
    follow_up_action TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (result_id) REFERENCES lab_results(result_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (order_item_id) REFERENCES lab_order_items(order_item_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (notified_to) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (acknowledgment_by极) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_reports (
    report_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    report_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    report_type report_type_type NOT NULL,
极    report_format VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    generated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    released_by UUID,
    released_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    report_status report_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    storage_path VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    digital_signature BYTEA,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES lab_orders(order_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (generated_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (released_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_test_master_code ON lab_test_master(test_code, is_active);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_test_master_type ON lab_test_master(test_type, is_active);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_test_master_tat ON lab_test_master(tat_target_minutes) WHERE is_active = true;

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_orders_patient ON lab_orders(patient_id, order_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_orders_doctor ON lab_orders(requesting_doctor_id, order_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_orders_status ON lab_orders(order_status) WHERE order_status != 'completed';
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_orders_datetime ON lab_orders(order_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_order_items_order ON lab_order_items(order_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_order_items_test ON lab_order_items(test_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_order_items_status ON lab_order_items(status) WHERE status != 'completed';
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_order_items_tat ON lab_order_items(tat_status) WHERE tat_status = 2; -- Delayed

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_specimens_order_item ON lab_specimens(order_item_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_specimens_status ON lab_specimens(specimen_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_specimens_collection_time ON lab_specimens(collection_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_order_item ON lab_results(order_item_id, result_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_test ON lab_results(test_id, result_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_status ON lab_results(result_status) WHERE result_status = 'preliminary';
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_datetime ON lab_results(result_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_result_details_result ON lab_result_details(result_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_result_details_critical ON lab_result_details(is_critical) WHERE is_critical = true;

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_qc_runs_test ON lab_qc_runs(test_id, run_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_qc_runs_instrument ON lab_qc_runs(instrument_id, run_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_qc_runs_status ON lab_qc_runs(qc_status) WHERE qc_status = 'out_of_control';

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_instruments_status ON lab_instruments(status) WHERE status = 'operational';
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_instruments_calibration ON lab_instruments(next_calibration_date) WHERE next_calibration_date IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_panic_values_patient ON lab_panic_values(patient_id, notified_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_panic_values_acknowledgment ON lab_panic_values(acknowledgment_received) WHERE acknowledgment_received = false;

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_reports_order ON lab_reports(order_id, report_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_reports_status ON lab_reports(report_status) WHERE report_status = 'draft';

-- Partitioning
CREATE TABLE lab_orders_y2024 PARTITION OF lab_orders
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

CREATE TABLE lab_results_y2024 PARTITION OF lab_results
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_order_item_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update order item status when specimen status changes
    IF NEW.specimen_status = 'received' AND OLD.specimen_status != 'received' THEN
        UPDATE lab_order_items
        SET status = 'received',
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE order_item_id = NEW.order_item_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_order_item_status
    AFTER UPDATE ON lab_specimens
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_order_item_status();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_critical_values()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    test_record RECORD;
    ref_low NUMERIC;
    ref_high NUMERIC;
    is_critical_flag BOOLEAN := false;
BEGIN
    -- Check for critical values when numeric results are entered
    IF NEW.numeric_value IS NOT NULL THEN
        SELECT reference_range_low, reference_range_high, test_name
        INTO test_record
        FROM lab_test_master
        WHERE test_id = (SELECT test_id FROM lab_results WHERE result_id = NEW.result_id);
        
        ref_low := test_record.reference_range_low;
        ref_high := test_record.reference_range_high;
        
        IF ref_low IS NOT NULL AND ref_high IS NOT NULL THEN
            IF NEW.numeric_value <= ref_low * 0.5 OR NEW.numeric_value >= ref_high * 1.5 THEN
                is_critical_flag := true;
                NEW.flags := CASE 
                    WHEN NEW.numeric_value <= ref_low * 0.5 THEN 'L' 
                    ELSE 'H' 
                END;
            END IF;
        END IF;
        
        NEW.is_critical := is_critical_flag;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_critical_values
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON lab_result_details
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION check_critical_values();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION create_panic_value_alert()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Create panic value alert when critical result is entered
    IF NEW.is_critical = true AND OLD.is_critical = false THEN
        INSERT INTO lab_panic_values (
            tenant_id, branch_id, result_id, order_item_id, patient_id,
            test_name, result_value, reference_range, notified_to,
            notification_method
        )
        SELECT 
            r.tenant_id, r.branch_id, r.result_id, r.order_item_id, o.patient_id,
            t.test_name, 
            COALESCE(NEW.numeric_value::text, NEW.text_value),
            COALESCE(
                t.reference_range_low::text || '-' || t.reference_range_high::text || ' ' || t.unit_of_measure,
                t.reference_range_text
            ),
            o.requesting_doctor_id,
            'system'
        FROM lab_results r
        JOIN lab_order_items oi ON r.order_item_id = oi.order_item_id
        JOIN lab_orders o ON oi.order_id = o.order_id
        JOIN lab_test_master t ON r.test_id = t.test_id
        WHERE r.result_id = NEW.result_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_create_panic_value_alert
    AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON lab_result_details
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION create_panic_value_alert();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enforce_qc_validation()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Enforce QC validation before result verification
    IF NEW.result_status = 'final' AND OLD.result_status != 'final' THEN
        PERFORM 1 FROM lab_qc_runs qc
        WHERE qc.test_id = NEW.test_id
        AND qc.instrument_id = NEW.instrument_id
        AND qc.run_datetime >= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP - INTERVAL '24 hours'
        AND qc.qc_status = 'in_control';
        
        IF NOT FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'No valid QC run found for this test and instrument in the last 24 hours';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_enforce_qc_validation
    BEFORE UPDATE ON lab_results
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_qc_validation();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_order_completion()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    pending_items INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Update order status when all items are completed
    IF NEW.status = 'completed' AND OLD.status != 'completed' THEN
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        INTO pending_items
        FROM lab_order_items
        WHERE order_id = (SELECT order_id FROM lab_order_items WHERE order_item_id = NEW.order_item_id)
        AND status != 'completed';
        
        IF pending_items = 0 THEN
            UPDATE lab_orders
            SET order_status = 'completed',
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            WHERE order_id = (SELECT order_id FROM lab_order_items WHERE order_item_id = NEW.order_item_id);
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_order_completion
    AFTER UPDATE ON lab_order_items
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_order_completion();

-- RLS Policies
CREATE POLICY lab_tenant_isolation ON lab_orders
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY results_privacy ON lab_results
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('lab_technician', 'pathologist', 'doctor', 'system_admin'));

CREATE POLICY qc_data_access ON lab_qc_runs
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('lab_technician', 'pathologist', 'quality_officer', 'system_admin'));

-- Views
CREATE VIEW pending_orders_view AS
SELECT 
    o.order_id,
    o.order_number,
    o.order_datetime,
    o.order_priority,
    o.order_status,
    p.patient_id,
    p.first_name,
    p.last_name,
    p.mrn,
    d.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    d.last_name as doctor_last_name,
    COUNT(oi.order_item_id) as total_items,
    COUNT(oi.order_item_id) FILTER (WHERE oi.status = 'completed') as completed_items
FROM lab_orders o
JOIN patients p ON o.patient_id = p.patient_id
JOIN doctors d ON o.requesting_doctor_id = d.doctor_id
JOIN lab_order_items oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id
WHERE o.order_status != 'completed'
GROUP BY o.order_id, p.patient_id, d.doctor_id
ORDER BY o.order_priority, o.order_datetime;

CREATE VIEW critical_results_view AS
SELECT 
    p.panic_id,
    p.result_id,
    p.order_item_id,
    p.patient_id,
    pat.first_name,
    pat.last_name,
    p.test_name,
    p.result_value,
    p.reference_range,
    p.notified_datetime,
    p.notification_method,
    p.acknowledgment_received,
    p.acknowledgment_datetime,
    doc.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    doc.last_name as doctor_last_name,
    o.order_number,
    o.order_priority
FROM lab_panic_values p
JOIN patients pat ON p.patient_id = pat.patient_id
JOIN lab_order_items oi ON p.order_item_id = oi.order_item_id
JOIN lab_orders o ON oi.order_id = o.order_id
JOIN doctors doc ON o.requesting_doctor_id = doc.doctor_id
WHERE p.acknowledgment_received = false
ORDER BY p.notified_datetime DESC;

CREATE VIEW instrument_qc_status_view AS
SELECT 
    i.instrument_id,
    i.instrument_code,
    i.instrument_name,
    i.model,
    i.status,
    i.last_calibration_date,
    i.next_calibration_date,
    COUNT(qc.qc_run_id) FILTER (WHERE qc.run_datetime >= CURRENT_DATE) as today_qc_runs,
    COUNT(qc.qc_run_id) FILTER (WHERE qc.qc_status = 'out_of_control' AND qc.run_datetime >= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '7 days') as recent_failures,
    MAX(qc.run_datetime) as last_qc_run
FROM lab_instruments i
LEFT JOIN lab_qc_runs qc ON i.instrument_id = qc.instrument_id
GROUP BY i.instrument_id
ORDER BY i.instrument_name;
```

```sql
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN currency_type NUMERIC(12,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0);
CREATE DOMAIN percentage_type NUMERIC(5,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE BETWEEN 0 AND 100);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE test_type_type AS ENUM (
    'chemistry', 'hematology', 'microbiology', 'immunology', 'molecular'
);

CREATE TYPE order_priority_type AS ENUM (
    'routine', 'urgent', 'stat'
);

CREATE TYPE order_status_type AS ENUM (
    'ordered', 'collected', 'received', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE order_location_type AS ENUM (
    'opd', 'ipd', 'er', 'external'
);

CREATE TYPE specimen_status_type AS ENUM (
    'collected', 'in_transit', 'received', 'processing', 'stored', 'discarded'
);

CREATE TYPE aliquot_status_type AS ENUM (
    'available', 'used', 'discarded'
);

CREATE TYPE result_type_type AS ENUM (
    'numeric', 'text', 'image', 'file'
);

CREATE TYPE result_status_type AS ENUM (
    'preliminary', 'final', 'corrected', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE qc_status_type AS ENUM (
    'in_control', 'out_of_control'
);

CREATE TYPE instrument_status_type AS ENUM (
    'operational', 'maintenance', 'out_of_service'
);

CREATE TYPE notification_method_type AS ENUM (
    'phone', 'message', 'system'
);

CREATE TYPE report_type_type AS ENUM (
    'interim', 'final', 'amended'
);

CREATE TYPE report_status_type AS ENUM (
    'draft', 'released', 'archived'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE lab_test_master (
    test_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID极NOT NULL,
    test_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    test_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    test_type test_type_type NOT NULL,
    specimen_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    container_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    volume_required_ml NUMERIC(6,2),
    storage_conditions TEXT,
    stability_hours SMALLINT,
    methodology VARCHAR(100),
    unit_of_measure VARCHAR(30),
    reference_range_low NUMERIC(10,2),
    reference_range_high NUMERIC极(10,2),
    reference_range_text TEXT,
    tat_target_minutes INTEGER CHECK (tat_target_minutes > 0),
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    requires_special_handling BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_test_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, test_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_reference_range CHECK (
        (reference_range_low IS NULL AND reference_range_high IS NULL) OR
        (reference_range_low IS NOT NULL AND reference_range_high IS NOT NULL AND reference_range_low < reference_range_high)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE lab_test_panels (
    panel_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    panel_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    panel_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    panel_description TEXT,
    test_ids UUID[] NOT NULL,
    panel_price currency_type NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_panel_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch极_id, panel_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_test_ids CHECK (array_length(test_ids, 1) > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_orders (
    order_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
极    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    requesting_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    ordering_department UUID NOT NULL,
    order_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    order_priority order_priority_type DEFAULT 'routine',
    clinical_notes TEXT,
    diagnosis_codes TEXT[],
    order_status order_status_type DEFAULT 'ordered',
    order_location order_location_type NOT NULL,
    is_insurance_approved BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    authorization_number VARCHAR(100),
    created_at TIMESTAM极PTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (requesting_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (ordering_department) REFERENCES departments(department_id)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_order_items (
    order_item_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    order_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    test_id UUID NOT NULL,
    test_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    test_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    priority order_priority_type NOT NULL,
    status order_status_type DEFAULT 'ordered',
    result_entered_by UUID,
    result_entered_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    result_verified_by UUID,
    result_verified_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    tat_actual_minutes INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN result_entered_at IS NOT NULL AND order_datetime IS NOT NULL
            THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (result_entered_at - order_datetime)) / 60
            ELSE NULL
        END
    ) STORED,
    tat_status SMALLINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN tat_actual_minutes IS NOT NULL AND 
                 (SELECT tat_target_minutes FROM lab_test_master WHERE test_id = lab_order_items.test_id) IS NOT NULL
            THEN CASE 
                WHEN tat_actual_minutes <= (SELECT tat_target_minutes FROM lab_test_master WHERE test_id = lab_order_items.test_id)
                THEN 1  -- On Time
                ELSE 2  -- Delayed
            END
            ELSE NULL
        END
    ) STORED,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPT极Z DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES lab_orders(order_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (test_id) REFERENCES lab_test_master(test_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (result_entered_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (result_verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_specimens (
    specimen_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_item_id UUID NOT NULL,
    specimen_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    specimen_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    container_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    collection_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    collected_by UUID NOT NULL,
    collection_site VARCHAR(100),
    collection_notes TEXT,
    received_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    received_by UUID,
    volume_ml NUMERIC(6,2),
    appearance VARCHAR(100),
    specimen_status specimen_status_type DEFAULT 'collected',
    storage_location VARCHAR(100),
    discard_dat极etime TIMESTAMPT极Z,
    discard_reason TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (order_item_id) REFERENCES lab_order_items(order_item_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (collected_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (received_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_volume CHECK (volume_ml IS NULL OR volume_ml > 0),
    CONSTRAINT chk_received_time CHECK (
        received_datetime IS NULL OR received_datetime >= collection_datetime
    )
);

CREATE TABLE lab_specimen_aliquots (
    aliquot_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    specimen_id UUID NOT NULL,
    aliquot_number VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    aliquot_volume_ml NUMERIC(6,2) NOT NULL,
    created_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    created_by UUID NOT NULL,
    storage_location VARCHAR(100),
    used_for_test UUID,
    used_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    aliquot_status aliquot_status_type DEFAULT 'available',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (specimen_id) REFERENCES lab_specimens(specimen_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (created_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (used_for_test) REFERENCES lab_test_master(test_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_aliquot_number UNIQUE (specimen_id, aliquot_number),
    CONSTRAINT chk_aliquot_volume CHECK (aliquot_volume_ml > 0)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_results (
    result_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_item_id UUID NOT NULL,
    test_id UUID NOT NULL,
    specimen_id UUID NOT NULL,
    result_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    result_type result_type_type NOT NULL,
    result_status result_status_type DEFAULT 'preliminary',
    verified_by UUID,
    verified_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    instrument_id UUID,
    reagent_lot_id UUID,
    qc_run_id UUID,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (order_item_id) REFERENCES lab_order_items(order_item_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (test极id) REFERENCES lab_test_master(test_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (specimen_id) REFERENCES lab_specimens(specimen_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (instrument_id) REFERENCES lab_instruments(instrument_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (qc_run_id) REFERENCES lab_qc_runs(qc_run_id)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_result_details (
    result_detail_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    result_id UUID NOT NULL,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    parameter_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    numeric_value NUMERIC(15,6),
    text_value TEXT,
    unit VARCHAR(30),
    reference_range_low NUMERIC(10,2),
    reference_range_high NUMERIC(10,2),
    flags VARCHAR(10),
    is_critical BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    comments TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (result_id) REFERENCES lab_results(result_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_result_value CHECK (
        numeric_value IS NOT NULL OR text_value IS NOT NULL
    ),
    CONSTRAINT chk_reference_range CHECK (
        (reference_range_low IS NULL AND reference_range_high IS NULL) OR
        (reference_range_low IS NOT NULL AND reference_range_high IS NOT NULL AND reference_range_low < reference_range_high)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE lab_qc_runs (
    q极c_run_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    test_id UUID NOT NULL,
    instrument_id UUID NOT NULL,
    qc_material_lot VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    run_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    expected_value NUMERIC(15,6) NOT NULL,
    measured_value NUMERIC(15,6) NOT NULL,
    sd_value NUMERIC(15,6),
    cv_percent percentage_type,
    westgard_rules JSONB,
    qc_status qc_status_type NOT NULL,
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL,
    reviewed_by UUID,
    reviewed_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (test_id) REFERENCES lab_test_master(test_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (instrument_id) REFERENCES lab_instruments(instrument_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (performed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (reviewed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_instruments (
    instrument_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    instrument_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    instrument_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    manufacturer VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    model VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    serial_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    installation_date DATE NOT NULL,
    last_calibration_date DATE,
    next_calibration_date DATE,
    status instrument_status_type DEFAULT 'operational',
    interface_type VARCHAR(100),
    is_connected BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_instrument_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, instrument_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_calibration_dates CHECK (
        last_calibration_date IS NULL OR next_calibration_date IS NULL OR 
        next_calibration_date > last_calibration_date
    )
);

CREATE TABLE lab_panic_values (
    panic_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    result_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_item_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    test_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    result_value VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    reference_range VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    notified_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    notified_to UUID NOT NULL,
    notification_method notification_method_type NOT NULL,
    acknowledgment_received BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    acknowledgment_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    acknowledgment_by UUID,
    follow_up_action TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (result_id) REFERENCES lab_results(result_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (order_item_id) REFERENCES lab_order_items(order_item_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (notified_to) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (acknowledgment_by极) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE lab_reports (
    report_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    report_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    report_type report_type_type NOT NULL,
极    report_format VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    generated_by UUID NOT NULL,
    released_by UUID,
    released_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    report_status report_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    storage_path VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    digital_signature BYTEA,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES lab_orders(order_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (generated_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (released_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_test_master_code ON lab_test_master(test_code, is_active);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_test_master_type ON lab_test_master(test_type, is_active);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_test_master_tat ON lab_test_master(tat_target_minutes) WHERE is_active = true;

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_orders_patient ON lab_orders(patient_id, order_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_orders_doctor ON lab_orders(requesting_doctor_id, order_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_orders_status ON lab_orders(order_status) WHERE order_status != 'completed';
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_orders_datetime ON lab_orders(order_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_order_items_order ON lab_order_items(order_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_order_items_test ON lab_order_items(test_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_order_items_status ON lab_order_items(status) WHERE status != 'completed';
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_order_items_tat ON lab_order_items(tat_status) WHERE tat_status = 2; -- Delayed

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_specimens_order_item ON lab_specimens(order_item_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_specimens_status ON lab_specimens(specimen_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_specimens_collection_time ON lab_specimens(collection_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_order_item ON lab_results(order_item_id, result_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_test ON lab_results(test_id, result_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_status ON lab_results(result_status) WHERE result_status = 'preliminary';
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_results_datetime ON lab_results(result_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_result_details_result ON lab_result_details(result_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_result_details_critical ON lab_result_details(is_critical) WHERE is_critical = true;

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_qc_runs_test ON lab_qc_runs(test_id, run_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_qc_runs_instrument ON lab_qc_runs(instrument_id, run_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_qc_runs_status ON lab_qc_runs(qc_status) WHERE qc_status = 'out_of_control';

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_instruments_status ON lab_instruments(status) WHERE status = 'operational';
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_instruments_calibration ON lab_instruments(next_calibration_date) WHERE next_calibration_date IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_panic_values_patient ON lab_panic_values(patient_id, notified_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_panic_values_acknowledgment ON lab_panic_values(acknowledgment_received) WHERE acknowledgment_received = false;

CREATE INDEX idx_lab_reports_order ON lab_reports(order_id, report_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_lab_reports_status ON lab_reports(report_status) WHERE report_status = 'draft';

-- Partitioning
CREATE TABLE lab_orders_y2024 PARTITION OF lab_orders
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

CREATE TABLE lab_results_y2024 PARTITION OF lab_results
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_order_item_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update order item status when specimen status changes
    IF NEW.specimen_status = 'received' AND OLD.specimen_status != 'received' THEN
        UPDATE lab_order_items
        SET status = 'received',
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE order_item_id = NEW.order_item_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_order_item_status
    AFTER UPDATE ON lab_specimens
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_order_item_status();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_critical_values()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    test_record RECORD;
    ref_low NUMERIC;
    ref_high NUMERIC;
    is_critical_flag BOOLEAN := false;
BEGIN
    -- Check for critical values when numeric results are entered
    IF NEW.numeric_value IS NOT NULL THEN
        SELECT reference_range_low, reference_range_high, test_name
        INTO test_record
        FROM lab_test_master
        WHERE test_id = (SELECT test_id FROM lab_results WHERE result_id = NEW.result_id);
        
        ref_low := test_record.reference_range_low;
        ref_high := test_record.reference_range_high;
        
        IF ref_low IS NOT NULL AND ref_high IS NOT NULL THEN
            IF NEW.numeric_value <= ref_low * 0.5 OR NEW.numeric_value >= ref_high * 1.5 THEN
                is_critical_flag := true;
                NEW.flags := CASE 
                    WHEN NEW.numeric_value <= ref_low * 0.5 THEN 'L' 
                    ELSE 'H' 
                END;
            END IF;
        END IF;
        
        NEW.is_critical := is_critical_flag;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_critical_values
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON lab_result_details
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION check_critical_values();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION create_panic_value_alert()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Create panic value alert when critical result is entered
    IF NEW.is_critical = true AND OLD.is_critical = false THEN
        INSERT INTO lab_panic_values (
            tenant_id, branch_id, result_id, order_item_id, patient_id,
            test_name, result_value, reference_range, notified_to,
            notification_method
        )
        SELECT 
            r.tenant_id, r.branch_id, r.result_id, r.order_item_id, o.patient_id,
            t.test_name, 
            COALESCE(NEW.numeric_value::text, NEW.text_value),
            COALESCE(
                t.reference_range_low::text || '-' || t.reference_range_high::text || ' ' || t.unit_of_measure,
                t.reference_range_text
            ),
            o.requesting_doctor_id,
            'system'
        FROM lab_results r
        JOIN lab_order_items oi ON r.order_item_id = oi.order_item_id
        JOIN lab_orders o ON oi.order_id = o.order_id
        JOIN lab_test_master t ON r.test_id = t.test_id
        WHERE r.result_id = NEW.result_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_create_panic_value_alert
    AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON lab_result_details
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION create_panic_value_alert();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION enforce_qc_validation()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Enforce QC validation before result verification
    IF NEW.result_status = 'final' AND OLD.result_status != 'final' THEN
        PERFORM 1 FROM lab_qc_runs qc
        WHERE qc.test_id = NEW.test_id
        AND qc.instrument_id = NEW.instrument_id
        AND qc.run_datetime >= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP - INTERVAL '24 hours'
        AND qc.qc_status = 'in_control';
        
        IF NOT FOUND THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'No valid QC run found for this test and instrument in the last 24 hours';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_enforce_qc_validation
    BEFORE UPDATE ON lab_results
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION enforce_qc_validation();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_order_completion()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    pending_items INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Update order status when all items are completed
    IF NEW.status = 'completed' AND OLD.status != 'completed' THEN
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        INTO pending_items
        FROM lab_order_items
        WHERE order_id = (SELECT order_id FROM lab_order_items WHERE order_item_id = NEW.order_item_id)
        AND status != 'completed';
        
        IF pending_items = 0 THEN
            UPDATE lab_orders
            SET order_status = 'completed',
                updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            WHERE order_id = (SELECT order_id FROM lab_order_items WHERE order_item_id = NEW.order_item_id);
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_order_completion
    AFTER UPDATE ON lab_order_items
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_order_completion();

-- RLS Policies
CREATE POLICY lab_tenant_isolation ON lab_orders
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY results_privacy ON lab_results
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('lab_technician', 'pathologist', 'doctor', 'system_admin'));

CREATE POLICY qc_data_access ON lab_qc_runs
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('lab_technician', 'pathologist', 'quality_officer', 'system_admin'));

-- Views
CREATE VIEW pending_orders_view AS
SELECT 
    o.order_id,
    o.order_number,
    o.order_datetime,
    o.order_priority,
    o.order_status,
    p.patient_id,
    p.first_name,
    p.last_name,
    p.mrn,
    d.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    d.last_name as doctor_last_name,
    COUNT(oi.order_item_id) as total_items,
    COUNT(oi.order_item_id) FILTER (WHERE oi.status = 'completed') as completed_items
FROM lab_orders o
JOIN patients p ON o.patient_id = p.patient_id
JOIN doctors d ON o.requesting_doctor_id = d.doctor_id
JOIN lab_order_items oi ON o.order_id = oi.order_id
WHERE o.order_status != 'completed'
GROUP BY o.order_id, p.patient_id, d.doctor_id
ORDER BY o.order_priority, o.order_datetime;

CREATE VIEW critical_results_view AS
SELECT 
    p.panic_id,
    p.result_id,
    p.order_item_id,
    p.patient_id,
    pat.first_name,
    pat.last_name,
    p.test_name,
    p.result_value,
    p.reference_range,
    p.notified_datetime,
    p.notification_method,
    p.acknowledgment_received,
    p.acknowledgment_datetime,
    doc.first_name as doctor_first_name,
    doc.last_name as doctor_last_name,
    o.order_number,
    o.order_priority
FROM lab_panic_values p
JOIN patients pat ON p.patient_id = pat.patient_id
JOIN lab_order_items oi ON p.order_item_id = oi.order_item_id
JOIN lab_orders o ON oi.order_id = o.order_id
JOIN doctors doc ON o.requesting_doctor_id = doc.doctor_id
WHERE p.acknowledgment_received = false
ORDER BY p.notified_datetime DESC;

CREATE VIEW instrument_qc_status_view AS
SELECT 
    i.instrument_id,
    i.instrument_code,
    i.instrument_name,
    i.model,
    i.status,
    i.last_calibration_date,
    i.next_calibration_date,
    COUNT(qc.qc_run_id) FILTER (WHERE qc.run_datetime >= CURRENT_DATE) as today_qc_runs,
    COUNT(qc.qc_run_id) FILTER (WHERE qc.qc_status = 'out_of_control' AND qc.run_datetime >= CURRENT_DATE - INTERVAL '7 days') as recent_failures,
    MAX(qc.run_datetime) as last_qc_run
FROM lab_instruments i
LEFT JOIN lab_qc_runs qc ON i.instrument_id = qc.instrument_id
GROUP BY i.instrument_id
ORDER BY i.instrument_name;
```

This implementation includes:

1. Comprehensive test management with master catalog and panel support
2. Full specimen tracking from collection to processing and storage
3. Automated result validation with critical value detection
4. Quality control integration with Westgard rules enforcement
5. Instrument management with calibration tracking
6. Panic value handling with notification workflows
7. TAT monitoring with automated delay detection
8. Partitioning for high-volume order and result data
9. RLS policies for data security and tenant isolation
10. Useful views for operational monitoring and quality assurance

The schema ensures laboratory workflow efficiency, supports regulatory compliance through comprehensive QC tracking, and provides real-time monitoring of critical results and turnaround times.
