## Domain 14: Radiology (RIS)

Here's the PostgreSQL implementation for the Radiology (RIS) domain:



```sql
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN currency_type NUMERIC(12,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE modality_status_type AS ENUM (
    'operational', 'maintenance', 'out_of_service'
);

CREATE TYPE modality_type_type AS ENUM (
    'ct', 'mri', 'xray', 'usg', 'mammo', 'fluoro'
);

CREATE TYPE priority_type AS ENUM (
    'routine', 'urgent', 'stat'
);

CREATE TYPE order_status_type AS ENUM (
    'ordered', 'scheduled', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE appointment_status_type AS ENUM (
    'scheduled', 'checked_in', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'no_show'
);

CREATE TYPE preparation_status_type AS ENUM (
    'not_started', 'in_progress', 'completed'
);

CREATE TYPE step_type_type AS ENUM (
    'preparation', 'positioning', 'imaging', 'contrast', 'post_processing'
);

CREATE TYPE step_status_type AS ENUM (
    'planned', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'skipped'
);

CREATE TYPE contrast_route_type AS ENUM (
    'iv', 'oral', 'rectal'
);

CREATE TYPE report_type_type AS ENUM (
    'preliminary', 'final', 'addendum', 'correction'
);

CREATE TYPE report_status_type AS ENUM (
    'draft', 'completed', 'verified', 'released'
);

CREATE TYPE dose_type_type AS ENUM (
    'ctdi', 'dlp', 'effective_dose'
);

CREATE TYPE note_type_type AS ENUM (
    'technical', 'patient', 'equipment', 'quality'
);

CREATE TYPE patient_cooperation_type AS ENUM (
    'excellent', 'good', 'fair', 'poor'
);

CREATE TYPE note_status_type AS ENUM (
    'draft', 'submitted'
);

CREATE TYPE pacs_status_type AS ENUM (
    'sent', 'received', 'archived', 'retrieved'
);

CREATE TYPE pregnancy_status_type AS ENUM (
    'not_pregnant', 'pregnant', 'unknown'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE radiology_modalities (
    modality_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    modality_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    modality_name VARCHAR(100)极NOT NULL,
    manufacturer VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    model VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    serial_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    installation_date DATE NOT NULL,
    room_number VARCHAR(20),
    aetitle VARCHAR(50),
    ip_address VARCHAR(45),
    port INTEGER,
    last_calibration_date DATE,
    next_calibration_date DATE,
    status modality_status_type DEFAULT 'operational',
    max_p极atients_per_day SMALLINT CHECK (max_patients_per_day > 0),
    slot_duration_minutes SMALLINT CHECK (slot_duration_minutes > 0),
    is_dicom_compliant BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_modality_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, modality_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_calibration_dates CHECK (
        last_calibration_date IS NULL OR next_calibration_date IS NULL OR 
        next_calibration_date > last_calibration_date
    )
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_procedures (
    procedure_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    procedure_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    procedure_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    modality_type modality_type_type NOT NULL,
    body_part VARCHAR(100),
    contrast_required BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    contrast_type VARCHAR(100),
    preparation_instructions TEXT,
    duration_minutes SMALLINT CHECK (duration_minutes > 0),
    radiation_dose_typical NUMERIC(8,2) CHECK (radiation_dose_typical >= 0),
    professional_fee currency_type NOT NULL,
    technical_fee currency_type NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY极 (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_procedure_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, procedure_code)
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_orders (
    order_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    requesting_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    procedure_id UUID NOT NULL,
    clinical_indication TEXT,
    priority priority_type DEFAULT 'routine',
    order_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    order_status order_status_type DEFAULT极 'ordered',
    pre_procedure_instructions TEXT,
    contraindications TEXT[],
    pregnancy_status pregnancy_status_type DEFAULT 'unknown',
    creatinine_level NUMERIC(5,2) CHECK (creatinine_level IS NULL OR creatinine_level > 0),
    gfr_level NUMERIC(5,2) CHECK (gfr_level IS NULL OR gfr_level > 0),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (requesting_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (procedure_id) REFERENCES radiology_procedures(procedure_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_contrast_safety CHECK (
        (SELECT contrast_required FROM radiology_procedures WHERE procedure_id = radiology_orders.procedure_id) = false OR
        (creatinine_level IS NOT NULL AND gfr_level IS NOT NULL)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_appointments (
    appointment_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_id UUID NOT NULL,
    modality_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    scheduled_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    duration_minutes SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (duration_minutes > 0),
    technologist_id UUID,
    radiologist_id UUID,
    appointment_status appointment_status_type DEFAULT 'scheduled',
    checkin_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    start_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    end_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    room_id UUID,
    preparation_status preparation_status_type DEFAULT 'not_started',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES radiology_orders(order_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (modality_id) REFERENCES radiology_modalities(modality_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (technologist_id) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (radiologist_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (room_id) REFERENCES rooms(room_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_appointment_times CHECK (
        scheduled_datetime <= COALESCE(checkin_datetime, scheduled_datetime) AND
        COALESCE(checkin_datetime, scheduled_datetime) <= COALESCE(start_datetime, scheduled_datetime) AND
        COALESCE(start_datetime, scheduled_datetime) <= COALESCE(end_datetime, scheduled_datetime)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_procedure_steps (
    step_id UUID PRIMARY极 KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID NOT NULL,
    step_type step_type_type NOT NULL,
    step_start_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    step_end_dat极etime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    step_duration_minutes SMALLINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN step_start_datetime IS NOT NULL AND step_end_datetime IS NOT NULL 
            THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (step_end_datetime - step_start_datetime)) / 60
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL,
    step_notes TEXT,
    step_status step_status_type DEFAULT 'planned',
    images_taken SMALLINT CHECK (images_taken IS NULL OR images_taken >= 0),
    sequences_performed TEXT[],
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES radiology_appointments(appointment_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (performed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_step_times CHECK (
        step_start_datetime IS NULL OR step_end_datetime IS NULL OR 
        step_start_datetime <= step_end_datetime
    )
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_contrast_usage (
    contrast_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID NOT NULL,
    contrast_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    contrast_lot_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    volume_ml NUMERIC(6,2) NOT NULL CHECK (volume_ml > 0),
    concentration VARCHAR(50),
    route contrast_route_type NOT NULL,
    injection_rate_ml_sec NUMERIC(4,2) CHECK (injection_rate_ml_sec IS NULL OR injection_rate_ml_sec > 0),
    administered_by UUID NOT NULL,
    administration_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    patient_reaction BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    reaction_details TEXT,
    premedication_given BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    premedication_details TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES radiology_appointments(appointment_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (administered_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_reports (
极    report_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    radiologist_id UUID NOT NULL,
    report_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT极TIMESTAMP,
    report_type report_type_type DEFAULT 'final',
    clinical_history TEXT,
    comparison TEXT,
    technique TEXT,
    findings TEXT,
    impression TEXT,
    recommendations TEXT,
极    report_status report_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    dictated_by UUID,
    transcribed_by UUID,
    verified_by UUID,
    verified_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    dicom_study_uid VARCHAR(100),
    pacs_accession_number VARCHAR(100),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES radiology_appointments(appointment_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES radiology_orders(order_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (radiologist_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dictated_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (transcribed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_dose_monitoring (
    dose_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    procedure_id UUID NOT NULL,
    modality_id UUID NOT NULL,
    dose_type dose_type_type NOT NULL,
    dose_value NUMERIC(10,4) NOT NULL CHECK (dose_value >= 0),
    dose_unit VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    exposure_parameters JSONB,
    body_region VARCHAR(100),
    patient_size VARCHAR(50),
    dose_reference_level NUMERIC(10,4) CHECK (dose_reference_level IS NULL OR dose_reference_level >= 0),
    is_within_limits BOOLEAN GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        dose_reference_level IS NULL OR dose_value <= dose_reference_level
    ) STOR极ED,
    recorded_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    recorded_by UUID NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES radiology_appointments(appointment_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (procedure_id) REFERENCES radiology_procedures(procedure_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (modality_id) REFERENCES radiology_modalities(modality_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (recorded_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_technician_notes (
    note_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID NOT NULL,
    technologist_id UUID NOT NULL,
    note_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    note_type note_type_type NOT NULL,
    note_text TEXT NOT NULL,
    images_reference TEXT[],
    quality_issues TEXT[],
    patient_cooperation patient_cooperation_type,
    note_status note_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES radiology_appointments(appointment_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (technologist_id) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_pacs_integration (
    pacs_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID NOT NULL,
    study_uid VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    accession_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    series_count SMALLINT CHECK (series_count >= 0),
    image_count INTEGER CHECK (image_count >= 0),
    storage_size_mb NUMERIC(10,2) CHECK (storage_size_mb >= 0),
    pacs_status pacs_status_type DEFAULT 'sent',
    sent_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    received_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    archived_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    retrieval_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    pacs_server VARCHAR(100),
    integration_notes TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES radiology_appointments(appointment_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_pacs_times CHECK (
        sent_datetime <= COALESCE(received_datetime, sent_datetime) AND
        COALESCE(received_datetime, sent_datetime) <= COALESCE(archived_datetime, sent_datetime) AND
        COALESCE(archived_datetime, sent_datetime) <= COALESCE(retrieval_datetime, sent_datetime)
    )
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_modalities_status ON radiology_modalities(status) WHERE status = 'operational';
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_modalities_type ON radiology_modalities(modality_code, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_modalities_calibration ON radiology_modalities(next_calibration_date) WHERE next_calibration_date IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_procedures_active ON radiology_procedures(is_active) WHERE is_active = true;
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_procedures_modality ON radiology_procedures(modality_type, body_part);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_procedures_contrast ON radiology_procedures(contrast_required) WHERE contrast_required = true;

CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_orders_patient ON radiology_orders(patient_id, order_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_orders_status ON radiology_orders(order_status) WHERE order_status != 'completed';
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_orders_priority ON radiology_orders(priority, order_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_orders_procedure ON radiology_orders(procedure_id, order_status);

CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_appointments_datetime ON radiology_appointments(scheduled_datetime, modality_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_appointments_status ON radiology_appointments(appointment_status) WHERE appointment_status != 'completed';
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_appointments_patient ON radiology_appointments(patient_id, scheduled_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_appointments_modality ON radiology_appointments(modality_id, scheduled_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_reports_appointment ON radiology_reports(appointment_id, report_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_reports_radiologist ON radiology_reports(radiologist_id, report_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_reports_status ON radiology_reports(report_status) WHERE report_status = 'draft';

CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_dose_appointment ON radiology_dose_monitoring(appointment_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_dose_procedure ON radiology_dose_monitoring(procedure_id, dose_type);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_dose_limits ON radiology_dose_monitoring(is_within_limits) WHERE is_within_limits = false;

CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_pacs_status ON radiology_pacs_integration(pacs_status) WHERE pacs_status != 'archived';
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_pacs_study ON radiology_pacs_integration(study_uid, accession_number);

-- Partitioning
CREATE TABLE radiology_orders_y2024 PARTITION OF radiology_orders
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

CREATE TABLE radiology_appointments_y2024 PARTITION OF radiology_appointments
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_order_status_on_appointment()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update order status when appointment status changes
    IF NEW.appointment_status = 'scheduled' AND OLD.appointment_status != 'scheduled' THEN
        UPDATE radiology_orders
        SET order_status = 'scheduled',
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE order_id = NEW.order_id;
    ELSIF NEW.appointment_status = 'in_progress' AND OLD.appointment_status != 'in_progress' THEN
        UPDATE radiology_orders
        SET order_status = 'in_progress',
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE order_id = NEW.order_id;
    ELSIF NEW.appointment_status = 'completed' AND OLD.appointment_status != 'completed' THEN
        UPDATE radiology_orders
        SET order_status = 'completed',
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE order_id = NEW.order_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_order_status_on_appointment
    AFTER UPDATE ON radiology_appointments
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update极_order_status_on_appointment();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION validate_modality_availability()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    modality_record RECORD;
    overlapping_appointments INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Validate modality availability for new appointments
    IF NEW.appointment_status = 'scheduled' THEN
        SELECT status, max_patients_per_day, slot_duration_minutes
        INTO modality_record
        FROM radiology_modalities
        WHERE modality_id = NEW.modality_id;
        
        IF modality_record.status != 'operational' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Modality is not operational';
        END IF;
        
        -- Check for overlapping appointments
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        INTO overlapping_appointments
        FROM radiology_appointments
        WHERE modality_id = NEW.modality_id
        AND appointment_id != NEW.appointment_id
        AND appointment_status IN ('scheduled', 'checked_in', 'in_progress')
        AND scheduled_datetime < NEW.scheduled_datetime + INTERVAL '1 minute' * NEW.duration_minutes
        AND scheduled_datetime + INTERVAL '1 minute' * duration_minutes > NEW.scheduled_datetime;
        
        IF overlapping_appointments > 0 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Modality has overlapping appointments';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_validate_modality_availability
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON radiology_appointments
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION validate_modality_availability();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_contrast_safety()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    procedure_record RECORD;
BEGIN
    -- Check contrast safety requirements
    IF NEW.contrast_required = true THEN
        SELECT contrast_required, contrast_type
        INTO procedure_record
        FROM radiology_procedures
        WHERE procedure_id = NEW.procedure_id;
        
        IF procedure_record.contrast_required = true THEN
            IF NEW.creatinine_level IS NULL OR NEW.gfr_level IS NULL THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Creatinine and GFR levels required for contrast procedures';
            END IF;
            
            IF NEW.creatinine_level > 1.5 OR NEW.gfr_level < 30 THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Contrast contraindicated due to renal function';
            END IF;
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_contrast_safety
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON radiology_orders
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION check_contrast_safety();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION track_dose_compliance()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    typical_dose NUMERIC;
BEGIN
    -- Track radiation dose compliance
    IF NEW.dose_value IS NOT NULL THEN
        SELECT radiation_dose_typical
        INTO typical_dose
        FROM radiology_procedures
        WHERE procedure_id = NEW.procedure_id;
        
        IF typical_dose IS NOT NULL THEN
            NEW.dose_reference_level := typical_dose * 1.2; -- 20% above typical
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_track_dose_compliance
    BEFORE INSERT ON radiology_dose_monitoring
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION track_dose_compliance();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_pacs_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update PACS status based on DICOM events
    IF NEW.study_uid IS NOT NULL AND OLD.study_uid IS NULL THEN
        NEW.pacs_status := 'sent';
        NEW.sent_datetime := CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;
    ELSIF NEW.image_count > 0 AND OLD.image_count = 0 THEN
        NEW.pacs_status := 'received';
        NEW.received_datetime := CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TR极IGGER trigger_update_pacs_status
    BEFORE UPDATE ON radiology_pacs_integration
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_pacs_status();

-- RLS Policies
CREATE POLICY radiology_tenant_isolation ON radiology_orders
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY reports_privacy ON radiology_reports
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('radiologist', 'technologist', 'doctor', 'system_admin'));

CREATE POLICY dose_data_access ON radiology_dose_monitoring
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('radiologist', 'physicist', 'technologist', 'system_admin'));

-- Views
CREATE VIEW modality_schedule_view AS
SELECT 
    m.modality_id,
    m.modality_code,
    m.modality_name,
    m.room_number,
    a.scheduled_datetime::date as schedule_date,
    COUNT(a.appointment_id) as total_appointments,
    COUNT(a.appointment_id) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_status = 'completed') as completed_appointments,
    COUNT(a.appointment_id) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_status = 'scheduled') as pending_appointments,
    MIN(a.scheduled_datetime::time) as first_appointment,
    MAX(a.scheduled_datetime::time) as last_appointment
FROM radiology_modalities m
LEFT JOIN radiology_appointments a ON m.modality_id = a.modality_id
WHERE m.status = 'operational'
AND a.scheduled_datetime >= CURRENT_DATE
AND a.scheduled_datetime < CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '1 day'
GROUP BY m.modality_id, a.scheduled_datetime::date
ORDER BY m.modality_code, schedule_date;

CREATE VIEW pending_reports_view AS
SELECT 
    r.report_id,
    r.appointment_id,
    a.order_id,
    p.patient_id,
    p.first_name,
    p.last_name,
    p.mrn,
    proc.procedure_name,
    a.scheduled_datetime,
    r.report_datetime,
    doc.first_name as radiologist_first_name,
    doc.last_name as radiologist_last_name,
    r.report_status,
    r.report_type
FROM radiology_reports r
JOIN radiology_appointments a ON r.appointment_id = a.appointment_id
JOIN radiology_orders o ON a.order_id = o.order_id
JOIN patients p ON o.patient_id = p.patient_id
JOIN radiology_procedures proc ON o.procedure_id = proc.procedure_id
JOIN doctors doc ON r.radiologist_id = doc.doctor_id
WHERE r.report_status IN ('draft', 'completed')
ORDER BY a.scheduled_datetime DESC;

CREATE VIEW radiation_dose_summary AS
SELECT 
    p.procedure_id,
    p.procedure_name,
    p.modality_type,
    p.radiation_dose_typical as typical_dose,
    COUNT(d.dose_id) as total_procedures,
    AVG(d.dose_value) as average_dose,
    MAX(d.dose_value) as maximum_dose,
    MIN(d.dose_value) as minimum_dose,
    COUNT(d.dose_id) FILTER (WHERE d.is_within_limits = false) as exceeded_limits,
    PERCENTILE_CONT(0.95) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY d.dose_value) as percentile_95
FROM radiology_procedures p
JOIN radiology_dose_monitoring d ON p.procedure_id = d.procedure_id
WHERE d.dose_type = 'effective_dose'
GROUP BY p.procedure_id, p.procedure_name, p.modality_type, p.radiation_dose_typical
ORDER BY p.modality_type, p.procedure_name;
```

```sql
-- Custom Domains
CREATE DOMAIN currency_type NUMERIC(12,2) 
    CHECK (VALUE >= 0);

-- Enum Types
CREATE TYPE modality_status_type AS ENUM (
    'operational', 'maintenance', 'out_of_service'
);

CREATE TYPE modality_type_type AS ENUM (
    'ct', 'mri', 'xray', 'usg', 'mammo', 'fluoro'
);

CREATE TYPE priority_type AS ENUM (
    'routine', 'urgent', 'stat'
);

CREATE TYPE order_status_type AS ENUM (
    'ordered', 'scheduled', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'cancelled'
);

CREATE TYPE appointment_status_type AS ENUM (
    'scheduled', 'checked_in', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'no_show'
);

CREATE TYPE preparation_status_type AS ENUM (
    'not_started', 'in_progress', 'completed'
);

CREATE TYPE step_type_type AS ENUM (
    'preparation', 'positioning', 'imaging', 'contrast', 'post_processing'
);

CREATE TYPE step_status_type AS ENUM (
    'planned', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'skipped'
);

CREATE TYPE contrast_route_type AS ENUM (
    'iv', 'oral', 'rectal'
);

CREATE TYPE report_type_type AS ENUM (
    'preliminary', 'final', 'addendum', 'correction'
);

CREATE TYPE report_status_type AS ENUM (
    'draft', 'completed', 'verified', 'released'
);

CREATE TYPE dose_type_type AS ENUM (
    'ctdi', 'dlp', 'effective_dose'
);

CREATE TYPE note_type_type AS ENUM (
    'technical', 'patient', 'equipment', 'quality'
);

CREATE TYPE patient_cooperation_type AS ENUM (
    'excellent', 'good', 'fair', 'poor'
);

CREATE TYPE note_status_type AS ENUM (
    'draft', 'submitted'
);

CREATE TYPE pacs_status_type AS ENUM (
    'sent', 'received', 'archived', 'retrieved'
);

CREATE TYPE pregnancy_status_type AS ENUM (
    'not_pregnant', 'pregnant', 'unknown'
);

-- Main Tables
CREATE TABLE radiology_modalities (
    modality_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    modality_code VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    modality_name VARCHAR(100)极NOT NULL,
    manufacturer VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    model VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    serial_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    installation_date DATE NOT NULL,
    room_number VARCHAR(20),
    aetitle VARCHAR(50),
    ip_address VARCHAR(45),
    port INTEGER,
    last_calibration_date DATE,
    next_calibration_date DATE,
    status modality_status_type DEFAULT 'operational',
    max_p极atients_per_day SMALLINT CHECK (max_patients_per_day > 0),
    slot_duration_minutes SMALLINT CHECK (slot_duration_minutes > 0),
    is_dicom_compliant BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_modality_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, modality_code),
    CONSTRAINT chk_calibration_dates CHECK (
        last_calibration_date IS NULL OR next_calibration_date IS NULL OR 
        next_calibration_date > last_calibration_date
    )
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_procedures (
    procedure_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    procedure_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    procedure_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    modality_type modality_type_type NOT NULL,
    body_part VARCHAR(100),
    contrast_required BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    contrast_type VARCHAR(100),
    preparation_instructions TEXT,
    duration_minutes SMALLINT CHECK (duration_minutes > 0),
    radiation_dose_typical NUMERIC(8,2) CHECK (radiation_dose_typical >= 0),
    professional_fee currency_type NOT NULL,
    technical_fee currency_type NOT NULL,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY极 (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT uk_procedure_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, branch_id, procedure_code)
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_orders (
    order_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_number VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    requesting_doctor_id UUID NOT NULL,
    procedure_id UUID NOT NULL,
    clinical_indication TEXT,
    priority priority_type DEFAULT 'routine',
    order_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    order_status order_status_type DEFAULT极 'ordered',
    pre_procedure_instructions TEXT,
    contraindications TEXT[],
    pregnancy_status pregnancy_status_type DEFAULT 'unknown',
    creatinine_level NUMERIC(5,2) CHECK (creatinine_level IS NULL OR creatinine_level > 0),
    gfr_level NUMERIC(5,2) CHECK (gfr_level IS NULL OR gfr_level > 0),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (requesting_doctor_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (procedure_id) REFERENCES radiology_procedures(procedure_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_contrast_safety CHECK (
        (SELECT contrast_required FROM radiology_procedures WHERE procedure_id = radiology_orders.procedure_id) = false OR
        (creatinine_level IS NOT NULL AND gfr_level IS NOT NULL)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_appointments (
    appointment_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_id UUID NOT NULL,
    modality_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    scheduled_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    duration_minutes SMALLINT NOT NULL CHECK (duration_minutes > 0),
    technologist_id UUID,
    radiologist_id UUID,
    appointment_status appointment_status_type DEFAULT 'scheduled',
    checkin_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    start_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    end_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    room_id UUID,
    preparation_status preparation_status_type DEFAULT 'not_started',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES radiology_orders(order_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (modality_id) REFERENCES radiology_modalities(modality_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (technologist_id) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (radiologist_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (room_id) REFERENCES rooms(room_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_appointment_times CHECK (
        scheduled_datetime <= COALESCE(checkin_datetime, scheduled_datetime) AND
        COALESCE(checkin_datetime, scheduled_datetime) <= COALESCE(start_datetime, scheduled_datetime) AND
        COALESCE(start_datetime, scheduled_datetime) <= COALESCE(end_datetime, scheduled_datetime)
    )
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_procedure_steps (
    step_id UUID PRIMARY极 KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID NOT NULL,
    step_type step_type_type NOT NULL,
    step_start_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    step_end_dat极etime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    step_duration_minutes SMALLINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        CASE 
            WHEN step_start_datetime IS NOT NULL AND step_end_datetime IS NOT NULL 
            THEN EXTRACT(EPOCH FROM (step_end_datetime - step_start_datetime)) / 60
            ELSE NULL 
        END
    ) STORED,
    performed_by UUID NOT NULL,
    step_notes TEXT,
    step_status step_status_type DEFAULT 'planned',
    images_taken SMALLINT CHECK (images_taken IS NULL OR images_taken >= 0),
    sequences_performed TEXT[],
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES radiology_appointments(appointment_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (performed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_step_times CHECK (
        step_start_datetime IS NULL OR step_end_datetime IS NULL OR 
        step_start_datetime <= step_end_datetime
    )
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_contrast_usage (
    contrast_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID NOT NULL,
    contrast_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    contrast_lot_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    volume_ml NUMERIC(6,2) NOT NULL CHECK (volume_ml > 0),
    concentration VARCHAR(50),
    route contrast_route_type NOT NULL,
    injection_rate_ml_sec NUMERIC(4,2) CHECK (injection_rate_ml_sec IS NULL OR injection_rate_ml_sec > 0),
    administered_by UUID NOT NULL,
    administration_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    patient_reaction BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    reaction_details TEXT,
    premedication_given BOOLEAN DEFAULT false,
    premedication_details TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES radiology_appointments(appointment_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (administered_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_reports (
极    report_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID NOT NULL,
    order_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    radiologist_id UUID NOT NULL,
    report_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT极TIMESTAMP,
    report_type report_type_type DEFAULT 'final',
    clinical_history TEXT,
    comparison TEXT,
    technique TEXT,
    findings TEXT,
    impression TEXT,
    recommendations TEXT,
极    report_status report_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    dictated_by UUID,
    transcribed_by UUID,
    verified_by UUID,
    verified_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    dicom_study_uid VARCHAR(100),
    pacs_accession_number VARCHAR(100),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES radiology_appointments(appointment_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES radiology_orders(order_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (radiologist_id) REFERENCES doctors(doctor_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (dictated_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (transcribed_by) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (verified_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_dose_monitoring (
    dose_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID NOT NULL,
    patient_id UUID NOT NULL,
    procedure_id UUID NOT NULL,
    modality_id UUID NOT NULL,
    dose_type dose_type_type NOT NULL,
    dose_value NUMERIC(10,4) NOT NULL CHECK (dose_value >= 0),
    dose_unit VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    exposure_parameters JSONB,
    body_region VARCHAR(100),
    patient_size VARCHAR(50),
    dose_reference_level NUMERIC(10,4) CHECK (dose_reference_level IS NULL OR dose_reference_level >= 0),
    is_within_limits BOOLEAN GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
        dose_reference_level IS NULL OR dose_value <= dose_reference_level
    ) STOR极ED,
    recorded_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    recorded_by UUID NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES radiology_appointments(appointment_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (patient_id) REFERENCES patients(patient_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (procedure_id) REFERENCES radiology_procedures(procedure_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (modality_id) REFERENCES radiology_modalities(modality_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (recorded_by) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_technician_notes (
    note_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID NOT NULL,
    technologist_id UUID NOT NULL,
    note_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    note_type note_type_type NOT NULL,
    note_text TEXT NOT NULL,
    images_reference TEXT[],
    quality_issues TEXT[],
    patient_cooperation patient_cooperation_type,
    note_status note_status_type DEFAULT 'draft',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES radiology_appointments(appointment_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (technologist_id) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE radiology_pacs_integration (
    pacs_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    tenant_id UUID NOT NULL,
    branch_id UUID NOT NULL,
    appointment_id UUID NOT NULL,
    study_uid VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    accession_number VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    series_count SMALLINT CHECK (series_count >= 0),
    image_count INTEGER CHECK (image_count >= 0),
    storage_size_mb NUMERIC(10,2) CHECK (storage_size_mb >= 0),
    pacs_status pacs_status_type DEFAULT 'sent',
    sent_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    received_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    archived_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    retrieval_datetime TIMESTAMPTZ,
    pacs_server VARCHAR(100),
    integration_notes TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    
    FOREIGN KEY (tenant_id) REFERENCES tenants(tenant_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES branches(branch_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (appointment_id) REFERENCES radiology_appointments(appointment_id),
    
    CONSTRAINT chk_pacs_times CHECK (
        sent_datetime <= COALESCE(received_datetime, sent_datetime) AND
        COALESCE(received_datetime, sent_datetime) <= COALESCE(archived_datetime, sent_datetime) AND
        COALESCE(archived_datetime, sent_datetime) <= COALESCE(retrieval_datetime, sent_datetime)
    )
);

-- Indexes
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_modalities_status ON radiology_modalities(status) WHERE status = 'operational';
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_modalities_type ON radiology_modalities(modality_code, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_modalities_calibration ON radiology_modalities(next_calibration_date) WHERE next_calibration_date IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_procedures_active ON radiology_procedures(is_active) WHERE is_active = true;
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_procedures_modality ON radiology_procedures(modality_type, body_part);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_procedures_contrast ON radiology_procedures(contrast_required) WHERE contrast_required = true;

CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_orders_patient ON radiology_orders(patient_id, order_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_orders_status ON radiology_orders(order_status) WHERE order_status != 'completed';
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_orders_priority ON radiology_orders(priority, order_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_orders_procedure ON radiology_orders(procedure_id, order_status);

CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_appointments_datetime ON radiology_appointments(scheduled_datetime, modality_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_appointments_status ON radiology_appointments(appointment_status) WHERE appointment_status != 'completed';
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_appointments_patient ON radiology_appointments(patient_id, scheduled_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_appointments_modality ON radiology_appointments(modality_id, scheduled_datetime);

CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_reports_appointment ON radiology_reports(appointment_id, report_status);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_reports_radiologist ON radiology_reports(radiologist_id, report_datetime);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_reports_status ON radiology_reports(report_status) WHERE report_status = 'draft';

CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_dose_appointment ON radiology_dose_monitoring(appointment_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_dose_procedure ON radiology_dose_monitoring(procedure_id, dose_type);
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_dose_limits ON radiology_dose_monitoring(is_within_limits) WHERE is_within_limits = false;

CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_pacs_status ON radiology_pacs_integration(pacs_status) WHERE pacs_status != 'archived';
CREATE INDEX idx_radiology_pacs_study ON radiology_pacs_integration(study_uid, accession_number);

-- Partitioning
CREATE TABLE radiology_orders_y2024 PARTITION OF radiology_orders
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

CREATE TABLE radiology_appointments_y2024 PARTITION OF radiology_appointments
    FOR VALUES FROM ('2024-01-01') TO ('2025-01-01');

-- Functions and Triggers
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_order_status_on_appointment()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update order status when appointment status changes
    IF NEW.appointment_status = 'scheduled' AND OLD.appointment_status != 'scheduled' THEN
        UPDATE radiology_orders
        SET order_status = 'scheduled',
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE order_id = NEW.order_id;
    ELSIF NEW.appointment_status = 'in_progress' AND OLD.appointment_status != 'in_progress' THEN
        UPDATE radiology_orders
        SET order_status = 'in_progress',
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE order_id = NEW.order_id;
    ELSIF NEW.appointment_status = 'completed' AND OLD.appointment_status != 'completed' THEN
        UPDATE radiology_orders
        SET order_status = 'completed',
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        WHERE order_id = NEW.order_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_update_order_status_on_appointment
    AFTER UPDATE ON radiology_appointments
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update极_order_status_on_appointment();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION validate_modality_availability()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    modality_record RECORD;
    overlapping_appointments INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Validate modality availability for new appointments
    IF NEW.appointment_status = 'scheduled' THEN
        SELECT status, max_patients_per_day, slot_duration_minutes
        INTO modality_record
        FROM radiology_modalities
        WHERE modality_id = NEW.modality_id;
        
        IF modality_record.status != 'operational' THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Modality is not operational';
        END IF;
        
        -- Check for overlapping appointments
        SELECT COUNT(*)
        INTO overlapping_appointments
        FROM radiology_appointments
        WHERE modality_id = NEW.modality_id
        AND appointment_id != NEW.appointment_id
        AND appointment_status IN ('scheduled', 'checked_in', 'in_progress')
        AND scheduled_datetime < NEW.scheduled_datetime + INTERVAL '1 minute' * NEW.duration_minutes
        AND scheduled_datetime + INTERVAL '1 minute' * duration_minutes > NEW.scheduled_datetime;
        
        IF overlapping_appointments > 0 THEN
            RAISE EXCEPTION 'Modality has overlapping appointments';
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_validate_modality_availability
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON radiology_appointments
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION validate_modality_availability();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_contrast_safety()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    procedure_record RECORD;
BEGIN
    -- Check contrast safety requirements
    IF NEW.contrast_required = true THEN
        SELECT contrast_required, contrast_type
        INTO procedure_record
        FROM radiology_procedures
        WHERE procedure_id = NEW.procedure_id;
        
        IF procedure_record.contrast_required = true THEN
            IF NEW.creatinine_level IS NULL OR NEW.gfr_level IS NULL THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Creatinine and GFR levels required for contrast procedures';
            END IF;
            
            IF NEW.creatinine_level > 1.5 OR NEW.gfr_level < 30 THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION 'Contrast contraindicated due to renal function';
            END IF;
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_contrast_safety
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON radiology_orders
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION check_contrast_safety();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION track_dose_compliance()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
DECLARE
    typical_dose NUMERIC;
BEGIN
    -- Track radiation dose compliance
    IF NEW.dose_value IS NOT NULL THEN
        SELECT radiation_dose_typical
        INTO typical_dose
        FROM radiology_procedures
        WHERE procedure_id = NEW.procedure_id;
        
        IF typical_dose IS NOT NULL THEN
            NEW.dose_reference_level := typical_dose * 1.2; -- 20% above typical
        END IF;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_track_dose_compliance
    BEFORE INSERT ON radiology_dose_monitoring
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION track_dose_compliance();

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_pacs_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    -- Update PACS status based on DICOM events
    IF NEW.study_uid IS NOT NULL AND OLD.study_uid IS NULL THEN
        NEW.pacs_status := 'sent';
        NEW.sent_datetime := CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;
    ELSIF NEW.image_count > 0 AND OLD.image_count = 0 THEN
        NEW.pacs_status := 'received';
        NEW.received_datetime := CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TR极IGGER trigger_update_pacs_status
    BEFORE UPDATE ON radiology_pacs_integration
    FOR EACH ROW
    EXECUTE FUNCTION update_pacs_status();

-- RLS Policies
CREATE POLICY radiology_tenant_isolation ON radiology_orders
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID);

CREATE POLICY reports_privacy ON radiology_reports
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('radiologist', 'technologist', 'doctor', 'system_admin'));

CREATE POLICY dose_data_access ON radiology_dose_monitoring
    USING (tenant_id = current_setting('app.current_tenant')::UUID)
    WITH CHECK (current_user IN ('radiologist', 'physicist', 'technologist', 'system_admin'));

-- Views
CREATE VIEW modality_schedule_view AS
SELECT 
    m.modality_id,
    m.modality_code,
    m.modality_name,
    m.room_number,
    a.scheduled_datetime::date as schedule_date,
    COUNT(a.appointment_id) as total_appointments,
    COUNT(a.appointment_id) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_status = 'completed') as completed_appointments,
    COUNT(a.appointment_id) FILTER (WHERE a.appointment_status = 'scheduled') as pending_appointments,
    MIN(a.scheduled_datetime::time) as first_appointment,
    MAX(a.scheduled_datetime::time) as last_appointment
FROM radiology_modalities m
LEFT JOIN radiology_appointments a ON m.modality_id = a.modality_id
WHERE m.status = 'operational'
AND a.scheduled_datetime >= CURRENT_DATE
AND a.scheduled_datetime < CURRENT_DATE + INTERVAL '1 day'
GROUP BY m.modality_id, a.scheduled_datetime::date
ORDER BY m.modality_code, schedule_date;

CREATE VIEW pending_reports_view AS
SELECT 
    r.report_id,
    r.appointment_id,
    a.order_id,
    p.patient_id,
    p.first_name,
    p.last_name,
    p.mrn,
    proc.procedure_name,
    a.scheduled_datetime,
    r.report_datetime,
    doc.first_name as radiologist_first_name,
    doc.last_name as radiologist_last_name,
    r.report_status,
    r.report_type
FROM radiology_reports r
JOIN radiology_appointments a ON r.appointment_id = a.appointment_id
JOIN radiology_orders o ON a.order_id = o.order_id
JOIN patients p ON o.patient_id = p.patient_id
JOIN radiology_procedures proc ON o.procedure_id = proc.procedure_id
JOIN doctors doc ON r.radiologist_id = doc.doctor_id
WHERE r.report_status IN ('draft', 'completed')
ORDER BY a.scheduled_datetime DESC;

CREATE VIEW radiation_dose_summary AS
SELECT 
    p.procedure_id,
    p.procedure_name,
    p.modality_type,
    p.radiation_dose_typical as typical_dose,
    COUNT(d.dose_id) as total_procedures,
    AVG(d.dose_value) as average_dose,
    MAX(d.dose_value) as maximum_dose,
    MIN(d.dose_value) as minimum_dose,
    COUNT(d.dose_id) FILTER (WHERE d.is_within_limits = false) as exceeded_limits,
    PERCENTILE_CONT(0.95) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY d.dose_value) as percentile_95
FROM radiology_procedures p
JOIN radiology_dose_monitoring d ON p.procedure_id = d.procedure_id
WHERE d.dose_type = 'effective_dose'
GROUP BY p.procedure_id, p.procedure_name, p.modality_type, p.radiation_dose_typical
ORDER BY p.modality_type, p.procedure_name;
```

This implementation includes:

1. Comprehensive modality management with DICOM compliance tracking
2. Procedure catalog with radiation dose and contrast requirements
3. Order management with clinical indication and safety checks
4. Appointment scheduling with resource allocation and conflict prevention
5. Procedure workflow tracking with detailed step monitoring
6. Contrast administration with safety monitoring and reaction tracking
7. Structured reporting with verification workflows
8. Radiation dose monitoring with compliance tracking
9. PACS integration with DICOM study management
10. Technician notes for quality assurance

The schema ensures radiology workflow efficiency, supports radiation safety compliance, and provides comprehensive integration with DICOM and PACS systems for seamless imaging management.
